In this work, we introduce a fine-grained formulation of reviewer contradiction analysis that operates over full peer reviews by explicitly identifying contradiction evidence spans and assigning graded disagreement intensity scores. To support this task, we present RevCI, an expert-annotated benchmark of peer-review pairs with evidence-level contradiction annotations with graded intensity labels. We further propose IMPACT, a structured multi-agent framework that integrates aspect-conditioned evidence extraction, deliberative reasoning, and adjudication to model reviewer contradictions and their intensity. To support efficient deployment, we distill IMPACT into TIDE, a small language model that predicts contradiction evidence and intensity in a single forward pass. Experimental results show that IMPACT substantially outperforms strong single-agent and generic multi-agent baselines in both evidence identification and intensity agreement, while TIDE achieves competitive performance at significantly lower inference cost.

</details>


### [114] [LegalCiteBench: Evaluating Citation Reliability in Legal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10186)
*Sijia Chen,Hang Yin,Shunfan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LegalCiteBench是一个包含约24K实例的法律引用基准，基于1000份真实美国司法意见构建，涵盖5项引用任务。在21个LLM评估中，最佳模型精确引用恢复得分低于7/100，且模型规模和法律预训练带来的提升有限，误导性回答率超过94%。该基准用于诊断模型在法律引用方面的生成失败、验证行为和弃权机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律基准主要关注成文法推理、合同理解或一般性法律问答，未能直接研究LLM在无外部依据时生成看似合理但错误的法律引用这一核心普通法失效模式，这在实际法律工作中会造成严重专业危害。

Method: 从Case Law Access Project中选取1000份真实美国司法意见，构建约24K评估实例，设计五项引用任务：引用检索、引用补全、引用错误检测、案例匹配、案例验证与修正。在封闭书籍设置下评估21个LLM，并引入误导性回答率和提示引导弃权实验。

Result: 最佳模型在引用检索和补全任务中得分低于7/100；模型规模和法律预训练带来的性能提升有限；20/21的模型在检索密集型任务上误导性回答率超过94%；明确不确定性指令减少了部分自信捏造，但未改善引用的正确性。

Conclusion: LegalCiteBench可作为诊断框架，研究LLM在法律引用场景下的权威生成失败、验证行为以及在缺乏或绕过外部依据时的弃权机制，揭示了当前模型在法律引用可靠性方面的严重不足。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into legal drafting and research workflows, where incorrect citations or fabricated precedents can cause serious professional harm. Existing legal benchmarks largely emphasize statutory reasoning, contract understanding, or general legal question answering, but they do not directly study a central common-law failure mode: when asked to provide case authorities without external grounding, models may return plausible-looking but incorrect citations or cases. We introduce LegalCiteBench, a benchmark for studying closed-book citation recovery, citation verification, and case matching in legal language models. LegalCiteBench contains approximately 24K evaluation instances constructed from 1,000 real U.S. judicial opinions from the Case Law Access Project. The benchmark covers five citation-centric tasks: citation retrieval, citation completion, citation error detection, case matching, and case verification and correction. Across 21 LLMs, exact citation recovery remains highly challenging in this closed-book setting: even the strongest models score below 7/100 on citation retrieval and completion. Within the evaluated models, scale and legal-domain pretraining provide limited gains and do not resolve this difficulty. Models also frequently provide concrete but incorrect or low-overlap authorities under our evaluation protocol, with Misleading Answer Rates (MAR) exceeding 94% for 20 of 21 evaluated models on retrieval-heavy tasks. A prompt-only abstention experiment shows that explicit uncertainty instructions reduce some confident fabrication but do not improve citation correctness. LegalCiteBench is intended as a diagnostic framework for studying authority generation failures, verification behavior, and abstention when external grounding is absent, incomplete, or bypassed.

</details>


### [115] [How Should LLMs Listen While Speaking? A Study of User-Stream Routing in Full-Duplex Spoken Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10199)
*Hui Lu,Xueyuan Chen,Huimeng Wang,Shuhai Peng,Shiyin Kang,Xixin Wu,Zhiyong Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究全双工语音对话系统中用户语音流如何路由进入LLM的架构问题，比较了通道融合（直接注入用户流）和交叉注意力路由（将用户流作为外部记忆）两种策略，发现前者语义理解更强但易受重叠输入干扰，后者鲁棒性更好但问答性能较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工口语对话要求模型在生成自身语音响应的同时持续监听用户输入，但LLM设计为生成单一连贯序列，无法自然支持生成过程中用户输入的到达。因此，如何将用户流路由进入LLM是全双工建模的关键架构问题。

Method: 将纯文本LLM扩展为统一的全双工口语对话系统，在共享训练流程下比较两种路由策略：(i) 通道融合——将用户流直接注入LLM输入；(ii) 交叉注意力路由——将用户流作为通过交叉注意力适配器访问的外部记忆。

Result: 在口语问答和全双工交互基准上，通道融合在语义接地和问答性能上更优，但在用户中断等语义重叠条件下容易受到上下文污染，导致生成不连贯；交叉注意力路由在问答上表现较弱，但能更好地保持LLM生成上下文，对重叠输入更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 用户流路由是全双工口语对话的关键设计轴，需要在语义集成和上下文鲁棒性之间进行权衡，为实际系统设计提供了指导。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue requires a model to keep listening while generating its own spoken response. This is challenging for large language models (LLMs), which are designed to extend a single coherent sequence and do not naturally support user input arriving during generation. We argue that how the user stream is routed into the LLM is therefore a key architectural question for full-duplex modeling. To study this question, we extend a text-only LLM into a unified full-duplex spoken dialogue system and compare two routing strategies under a shared training pipeline: (i) channel fusion, which injects the user stream directly into the LLM input, and (ii) cross-attention routing, which keeps the user stream as external memory accessed through cross-attention adapters. Experiments on spoken question answering and full-duplex interaction benchmarks reveal a clear tradeoff. Channel fusion yields stronger semantic grounding and consistently better question-answering performance. However, under semantically overlapping conditions such as user interruptions, it is more vulnerable to context corruption: if the model fails to stop in time, the overlapping user stream can interfere with ongoing generation and lead to semantically incoherent continuations. Cross-attention routing underperforms on question answering, but better preserves the LLM generation context and is more robust to this failure mode. These results establish user-stream routing as a central design axis in full-duplex spoken dialogue and offer practical guidance on the tradeoff between semantic integration and context robustness. We provide a demo page for qualitative inspection.

</details>


### [116] [The Impact of Editorial Intervention on Detecting Native Language Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10216)
*Ahmet Yavuz Uluslu,Mark Gales,Kate Knill,Gerold Schneider*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究人类-AI合著场景下，母语识别（NLI）任务对不同程度编辑干预的鲁棒性。通过处理450篇非母语作文，发现L1痕迹不完全依赖表层错误，而是嵌入在更深层的词汇-语义选择、语用迁移和文化视角中。最小编辑保留这些痕迹，而流利性编辑和释义会严重削弱识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被广泛用于非母语文本的校正和改写，传统NLI模型依赖的语言特征被根本性改变，因此需要探究L1痕迹在不同程度编辑干预下的鲁棒性。

Method: 使用Write & Improve 2024语料库中的450篇作文，通过不同级别的语法错误纠正（GEC）和释义处理，分析L1归因准确率的变化。

Result: L1归因不完全依赖表层错误；深层特征（词汇-语义选择、语用迁移、文化视角）是关键。最小编辑能保持高识别准确率，而流利性编辑和释义导致性能严重下降。

Conclusion: L1痕迹具有深层结构性，并非仅停留在表面错误层面；但大幅度的编辑干预（尤其是流利性编辑和释义）会破坏这些特征，削弱NLI系统的有效性。

Abstract: Native Language Identification (NLI) is the task of determining an author's native language (L1) from their non-native writings. With the advent of human-AI co-authorship, non-native texts are routinely corrected and rewritten by large language models, fundamentally altering the linguistic features NLI models depend on. In this paper, we investigate the robustness of L1 traces across increasing degrees of editorial intervention. By processing 450 essays from the Write & Improve 2024 corpus through varying levels of grammatical error correction (GEC) and paraphrasing, we demonstrate that L1 attribution does not entirely depend on surface-level errors. Instead, the detection models leverage deeper L1 features: unidiomatic lexico-semantic choices, pragmatic transfer, and the author's underlying cultural perspective. We find that minimal edits preserve these structural traces and maintain high profiling accuracy. In contrast, fluency edits and paraphrasing normalize these L1 features, leading to a severe degradation in performance.

</details>


### [117] [Relative Score Policy Optimization for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10218)
*Zichao Yu,Shengze Xu,Bingqing Jiang,Wenyi Zhang,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）因缺乏可处理的序列级对数比而难以应用标准强化学习的问题，本文提出相对分数策略优化（RSPO），将奖励优势同时解释为更新方向和相对对数比目标，通过校准噪声似然估计来稳定RLVR训练，在规划任务和数学推理上取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）的后训练中，应用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）面临核心障碍：缺乏可处理的序列级对数比，现有方法被迫依赖高方差的ELBO近似，导致高验证奖励会放大不准确的分数估计并 destabilize 强化学习训练。

Method: 提出相对分数策略优化（RSPO），核心洞察是将奖励优势不仅视为更新方向，还作为当前策略与参考策略之间相对对数比的目标。RSPO通过比较实际奖励优势与奖励隐含的目标相对对数比之间的差距来校准噪声相对对数比估计，基于估计值与目标之间的差距而非原始优势本身来更新策略。

Result: 在数学推理和规划基准上的实验表明，RSPO在规划任务上取得特别强的提升，在数学推理性能上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: RSPO通过将奖励优势重新解释为相对对数比目标，有效解决了dLLMs中RLVR训练因缺乏序列级对数比而导致的估计不稳定问题，为扩散语言模型的后训练提供了一种简单有效的RLVR方法。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a promising route to parallel and efficient text generation, but improving their reasoning ability requires effective post-training. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a natural choice for this purpose, yet its application to dLLMs is hindered by the absence of tractable sequence-level log-ratios, which are central to standard policy optimization. The lack of tractable sequence-level log-ratios forces existing methods to rely on high-variance ELBO-based approximations, where high verifier rewards can amplify inaccurate score estimates and destabilize RL training. To overcome this issue, we propose \textbf{R}elative \textbf{S}core \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (RSPO), a simple RLVR method that uses verifiable rewards to calibrate noisy likelihood estimates in dLLMs. The core of our algorithm relies on a key observation: a reward advantage can be interpreted not only as an update direction, but also as a target for the relative log-ratio between the current and reference policies. Accordingly, RSPO calibrates this noisy relative log-ratio estimate by comparing its reward advantage with the reward-implied target relative log-ratio, updating the policy according to the gap between the current estimate and the target rather than the raw advantage alone. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and planning benchmarks show that RSPO yields especially strong gains on planning tasks and competitive mathematical-reasoning performance.

</details>


### [118] [Route Before Retrieve: Activating Latent Routing Abilities of LLMs for RAG vs. Long-Context Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10235)
*Yiwen Chen,Kuan Li,Fuzhen Zhuang,Deqing Wang,Zhao Zhang,Liwen Zhang,Yong Jiang,Shuai Wang,Minhao Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Pre-Route，一种在回答前进行结构化推理的主动路由框架，通过轻量级元数据进行任务分析、覆盖估计和信息需求预测，实现RAG与长上下文策略之间的可解释且成本高效的路由决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如Self-Route采用从RAG到LC的失败驱动回退机制，但存在被动、低效且难以解释的问题，亟需一种主动且可解释的路由框架。

Method: Pre-Route框架利用轻量级元数据（文档类型、长度、初始片段）进行结构化推理，包括任务分析、覆盖估计和信息需求预测，从而生成可解释且成本高效的路由决策。

Result: 在LaRA（域内）和LongBench-v2（域外）上，Pre-Route优于Always-RAG、Always-LC和Self-Route基线，实现更优的整体成本效益。三个关键发现：LLM具有可通过指南可靠激发的潜在路由能力；结构化提示在表征空间中增强了最优路由维度的可分离性；蒸馏可将推理结构迁移至小模型。

Conclusion: Pre-Route通过主动结构化推理取代被动回退，在RAG和LC之间实现了更优的成本效益权衡，且具有可解释性，并能通过蒸馏实现轻量化部署。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have expanded the context window to beyond 128K tokens, enabling long-document understanding and multi-source reasoning. A key challenge, however, lies in choosing between retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and long-context (LC) strategies: RAG is efficient but constrained by retrieval quality, while LC supports global reasoning at higher cost and with position sensitivity. Existing methods such as Self-Route adopt failure-driven fallback from RAG to LC, but remain passive, inefficient, and hard to interpret. We propose Pre-Route, a proactive routing framework that performs structured reasoning before answering. Using lightweight metadata (e.g., document type, length, initial snippet), Pre-Route enables task analysis, coverage estimation, and information-need prediction, producing explainable and cost-efficient routing decisions. Our study shows three key findings: (i) LLMs possess latent routing ability that can be reliably elicited with guidelines, allowing single-sample performance to approach that of multi-sample (Best-of-N) results; (ii) linear probes reveal that structured prompts sharpen the separability of the "optimal routing dimension" in representation space; and (iii) distillation transfers this reasoning structure to smaller models for lightweight deployment. Experiments on LaRA (in-domain) and LongBench-v2 (OOD) confirm that Pre-Route outperforms Always-RAG, Always-LC, and Self-Route baselines, achieving superior overall cost-effectiveness.

</details>


### [119] [Qwen Goes Brrr: Off-the-Shelf RAG for Ukrainian Multi-Domain Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10296)
*Anton Bazdyrev,Ivan Bashtovyi,Ivan Havlytskyi,Oleksandr Kharytonov,Artur Khodakovskyi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对乌克兰语多领域文档理解的检索增强 pipeline，通过上下文分块、问题感知的稠密检索和重排序以及约束答案生成，在 UNLP 共享任务中取得了优异结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与第五次UNLP多领域文档理解共享任务，需要从PDF集合中回答乌克兰语多项选择题并定位支持文档和页面，现有方法在严格代码竞赛约束下效果有限。

Method: 提出检索增强pipeline，包含三个核心思想：(1) PDF的上下文分块；(2) 基于问题和选项感知的稠密检索与重排序；(3) 从少量重排序段落中进行约束答案生成。使用Qwen3-Embedding-8B检索、微调Qwen3-Reranker-8B重排序、Qwen3-32B选择答案。

Result: 重排序将Recall@1从0.6957提升至0.7935；使用top-2重排序段落使答案准确率从0.9348提升至0.9674；公开排行榜0.9452，私有排行榜0.9598。

Conclusion: 在严格代码竞赛约束下，保留文档结构并使相关性估计感知答案空间，比添加复杂的下游启发式规则更有效。

Abstract: We participated in the Fifth UNLP shared task on multi-domain document understanding, where systems must answer Ukrainian multiple-choice questions from PDF collections and localize the supporting document and page. We propose a retrieval-augmented pipeline built around three ideas: contextual chunking of PDFs, question-aware dense retrieval and reranking conditioned on both the question and answer options, and constrained answer generation from a small set of reranked passages. Our final system uses Qwen3-Embedding-8B for retrieval, a fine-tuned Qwen3-Reranker-8B for passage ranking, and Qwen3-32B for answer selection. On a held-out split, reranking improves Recall@1 from 0.6957 to 0.7935, while using the top-2 reranked passages raises answer accuracy from 0.9348 to 0.9674. Our best leaderboard run reached 0.9452 on the public leaderboard and 0.9598 on the private leaderboard. Our results suggest that, under strict code-competition constraints, preserving document structure and making relevance estimation aware of the answer space are more effective than adding complex downstream heuristics.

</details>


### [120] [Extending Confidence-Based Text2Cypher with Grammar and Schema Aware Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10318)
*Makbule Gulcin Ozsoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Text2Cypher任务中，通过在测试阶段加入语法验证和模式约束的序列过滤方法，来提升生成查询语句的结构正确性和可执行性。实验表明，该后处理方法能有效提高语法有效性和模式一致性，但更强的过滤也会增加空预测并降低执行覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text2SQL、Text2SPARQL、Text2Cypher等方法主要关注提示优化、微调或迭代精炼，但往往没有显式强制执行结构约束（如语法有效性和模式一致性），导致生成的查询可能因不符合语法规则或数据库模式约束而不可执行，降低了生成查询的可靠性。

Method: 在Text2Cypher的后生成验证阶段，扩展了一个基于置信度的推理框架，加入序列过滤流程：依次进行置信度评分、语法验证和模式约束检查，最后进行聚合。通过这种分步过滤，分析不同约束类型对生成查询的影响。

Result: 在两种指令微调模型上的实验表明：基于语法的过滤提高了语法有效性；基于模式的过滤进一步提升了执行质量（通过强制与数据库结构的一致性）。但更强的过滤也导致空预测增多、执行覆盖率下降。

Conclusion: 在测试时添加简单的结构性检查可以有效提升Text2Cypher生成的可靠性，同时语法约束和模式约束对查询正确性的贡献不同，需要权衡过滤强度与覆盖率之间的关系。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) allow users to query databases using natural language by translating questions into executable queries. Despite strong progress on tasks such as Text2SQL, Text2SPARQL, and Text2Cypher, most existing methods focus on better prompting, fine-tuning, or iterative refinement. However, they often do not explicitly enforce structural constraints, such as syntactic validity and schema consistency. This can reduce reliability, since generated queries must satisfy both syntax rules and database schema constraints to be executable. In this work, we study how structured constraints can be used in test-time inference for Text2Cypher. We focus on post-generation validation to improve query correctness. We extend a confidence-based inference framework with a sequential filtering process that combines confidence scoring, grammar validation, and schema constraints before final aggregation. This lets us analyze how different constraint types affect generated queries. Our experiments with two instruction-tuned models show that grammar-based filtering improves syntactic validity. Schema-aware filtering further improves execution quality by enforcing consistency with the database structure. However, stronger filtering also increases the number of empty predictions and reduces execution coverage. Overall, we show that adding simple structural checks at test time improves the reliability of Text2Cypher generation, and we provide a clearer view of how syntax and schema constraints contribute differently.

</details>


### [121] [An Annotation Scheme and Classifier for Personal Facts in Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10339)
*Konstantin Zaitsev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的个人事实分类标注方案，新增了人口统计学、物品、持续时间、有效性、后续行动等类别和属性，并基于Transformer编码器训练了多头部分类器，在2,779条多会话聊天数据上达到81.6%宏F1，比最佳少样本LLM基线（GPT-5.4-mini）高出近9个百分点，同时计算资源需求大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人事实分类方法（尤其是PeaCoK）存在局限性，缺乏对事实的结构化存储、质量过滤以及适用于对话延续的事实识别能力，需要更完善的标注方案来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出扩展的标注方案（新增Demographics、Possessions、Duration、Validity、Followup等类别/属性），人工标注2,779条来自Multi-Session Chat的事实，训练基于Transformer编码器的多头部分类器（结合Gemma-300M编码器）。

Result: 分类器达到81.6±2.6%宏F1，超越所有少样本LLM基线（最佳GPT-5.4-mini为72.92%），提升近9个百分点，且计算资源需求显著更少。

Conclusion: 错误分析揭示了语义边界消歧、时间方面解释和后续评估的语用推理等持续存在的挑战，数据集和分类器已公开。

Abstract: The advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has enabled their application in personalized dialogue systems. We present an extended annotation scheme for personal fact classification that addresses limitations in existing approaches, particularly PeaCoK. Our scheme introduces new categories (Demographics, Possessions) and attributes (Duration, Validity, Followup) that enable structured storage, quality filtering, and identification of facts suitable for dialogue continuation. We manually annotated 2,779 facts from Multi-Session Chat and trained a multi-head classifier based on transformer encoders. Combined with the Gemma-300M encoder, the classifier achieves $81.6 \pm 2.6$\% macro F1, outperforming all few-shot LLM baselines (best: GPT-5.4-mini, 72.92\%) by nearly 9 percentage points while requiring substantially fewer computational resources. Error analysis reveals persistent challenges in semantic boundary disambiguation, temporal aspect interpretation, and pragmatic reasoning for followup assessment. The dataset\footnotemark[1] and classifier\footnotemark[2] are publicly available.

</details>


### [122] [Aligning LLM Uncertainty with Human Disagreement in Subjectivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10415)
*Junyu Lu,Deyi Ji,Xuanyi Liu,Lanyun Zhu,Bo Xu,Liang Yang,Hongfei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DPUA两阶段框架，通过解耦学习和GRPO奖励优化，让主观性分析模型在保持任务性能的同时，使模型的不确定性表达与人类分歧分布对齐，缓解过置信并提升泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主观性分析模型使用聚合标签训练，忽略了低一致性样本的固有不确定性，导致模型过度自信，在复杂主观场景下可靠性和泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出两阶段DPUA框架：(1) 分歧感知阶段：自适应解耦学习增强模型对分歧相关线索的敏感性；(2) 不确定性对齐阶段：基于GRPO的奖励优化，使模型的不确定性推理与人类分歧分布对齐。

Result: 在三个主观性分析任务上，DPUA在保持任务性能的同时，使模型不确定性与人类分歧更好对齐，缓解了边界样本的过置信问题，并提升了分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的主观性分析范式比传统聚合标签训练更合理，DPUA框架能有效建模并利用人类判断中的分歧信息，提升模型在主观任务中的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models for subjectivity analysis are typically trained with aggregated labels, which compress variations in human judgment into a single supervision signal. This paradigm overlooks the intrinsic uncertainty of low-agreement samples and often induces overconfident predictions, undermining reliability and generalization in complex subjective settings. In this work, we advocate uncertainty-aware subjectivity analysis, where models are expected to make predictions while expressing uncertainty that reflects human disagreement. To operationalize this perspective, we propose a two-phase Disagreement Perception and Uncertainty Alignment (DPUA) framework. Specifically, DPUA jointly models label prediction, rationale generation, and uncertainty expression under an uncertainty-aware setting. In the disagreement perception phase, adaptive decoupled learning enhances the model's sensitivity to disagreement-related cues while preserving task performance. In the uncertainty alignment phase, GRPO-based reward optimization further improves uncertainty-aware reasoning and aligns the model's confidence expression with the human disagreement distribution. Experiments on three subjectivity analysis tasks show that DPUA preserves task performance while better aligning model uncertainty with human disagreement, mitigating overconfidence on boundary samples, and improving out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [123] [Can Language Models Analyze Data? Evaluating Large Language Models for Question Answering over Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10419)
*Andreas Xenofontos,Pavlos Fafalios*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在数据集问答任务中的有效性，比较了直接回答和生成SQL查询两种方式，评估了不同提示策略的影响，发现大模型表现强劲而小模型存在局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型在数据问答任务中的有效性，评估不同规模模型（大模型与资源高效的小模型）的表现差异，以及不同提示策略对性能的影响。

Method: 在两种场景下进行实验：(a)直接以数据集文件为输入回答问题；(b)根据关系数据库模式生成SQL查询来回答问题。同时评估不同的提示策略，在两个包含不同难度问题的数据集上测试了多种LLM（包括大型前沿模型和资源需求更小的小模型）。

Result: 大型LLM表现出强劲性能，而更小、更具成本效益的模型则表现出明显的局限性。

Conclusion: 研究结果有助于理解LLM在数据分析任务中的应用潜力及其局限性，为实际部署中模型选型提供参考依据。

Abstract: This paper investigates the effectiveness of large language models (LLMs) in answering questions over datasets. We examine their performance in two scenarios: (a) directly answering questions given a dataset file as input, and (b) generating SQL queries to answer questions given the schema of a relational database. We also evaluate the impact of different prompting strategies on model performance. The study includes both state-of-the-art LLMs and smaller language models that require fewer resources and operate at lower computational and financial cost. Experiments are conducted on two datasets containing questions of varying difficulty. The results demonstrate the strong performance of large LLMs, while highlighting the limitations of smaller, more cost-efficient models. These findings contribute to a better understanding of how LLMs can be utilized in data analytics tasks and their associated limitations.

</details>


### [124] [Coherency through formalisations of Structured Natural Language, A case study on FRETish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10462)
*Joost J. Joosten,Marina López Chamosa,Sofía Santiago Fernández*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"形式化一致性"原则，要求不同形式化层次（自然语言、技术语言、图示、形式语言）保持大致相同的逻辑结构，并以NASA的FRET工具为案例，将受控自然语言FRETish自动翻译到MTL形式语言，通过模型检验证明等价性，统计结果显示新翻译更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化是形式化方法中最微妙复杂的步骤之一，现有文献虽有良好实践准则，但缺乏指导不同形式化层次间逻辑结构一致性的原则。在大语言模型进行可被形式工具检验的推理任务场景下，需要结构化的自然语言作为中间层，故需一致性原则指导。

Method: 1. 提出"形式化一致性"（Coherency through Formalisations）准则，要求不同形式化层次大致遵循相同逻辑结构。2. 以NASA的FRET工具为案例，分析其原有FRETish到MTL的翻译。3. 提出替代的自动翻译方案。4. 使用模型检验证明新翻译与原翻译等价。5. 进行统计分析比较两种翻译。

Result: 新翻译方案在统计上优于原始翻译；翻译过程揭示了已有实现中的不一致性问题。通过模型检验证明了新翻译与原翻译的等价性。

Conclusion: "形式化一致性"原则在不同形式化层次间具有指导价值，特别适用于结合LLM推理和形式工具验证的场景。FRET工具案例表明，遵循该原则可以产生更优的翻译，并暴露隐藏的不一致问题。

Abstract: Formalisation is the process of writing system requirements in a formal language. These requirements mostly originate in Natural Language. In the field of Formal Methods, formalisation is often identified as one of the most delicate and complicated steps in the verification process. Not seldomly, formalisation tools and environments choose various levels of requirement descriptions: Natural Language, Technical Language, Diagram Representations and Formal Language, to mention a few. In the literature, there are various maxims and principles of good practice to guide the process of requirement formalisation. In this paper we propose a new guideline: Coherency through Formalisations. The guideline states that the different levels of formalisation mentioned above should roughly follow the same logical structure. The principle seems particularly relevant in the setting where LLMs are prompted to perform reasoning tasks that can be checked by formal tools using Structured Natural Language to act as an intermediate layer bridging both paradigms. In the light of coherency, we analyze NASA's Formal Requirement Elicitation Tool FRET and propose an alternative automated translation of the Controlled Natural Language FRETish to the formal language of MTL. We compare our translation to the original translation and prove equivalence using model checking. Some statistics are performed which seem to favor the new translation. As expected, the translation process yielded interesting reflections and revealed inconsistencies which we present and discuss.

</details>


### [125] [DeepRefine: Agent-Compiled Knowledge Refinement via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10488)
*Haoyu Huang,Jiaxin Bai,Shujie Liu,Yang Wei,Hong Ting Tsang,Yisen Gao,Zhongwei Xie,Yufei Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeepRefine是一个基于LLM的推理模型，通过多轮交互、溯因诊断和强化学习，自动改进智能体编译的知识库质量，减少不完整、不正确和冗余问题，提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体编译的知识库在开放域知识密集型下游任务中存在系统性缺陷（不完整、不正确、冗余），且在迭代使用中缺陷会累积，导致检索保真度和下游任务性能下降，亟需一种通用的知识库质量改进方法。

Method: DeepRefine采用LLM驱动的推理模型，通过多轮与知识库交互，进行溯因诊断定位缺陷，并执行定向细化操作以增量更新知识库。同时引入Gain-Beyond-Draft (GBD)奖励，通过端到端强化学习训练优化细化策略，无需人工标注的黄金参考。

Result: 大量实验表明，DeepRefine在多个强基线上取得了持续的下游性能提升。

Conclusion: DeepRefine有效地解决了智能体编译知识库的质量问题，通过可学习的推理细化策略显著改善了检索效果和下游任务表现。

Abstract: Agent-compiled knowledge bases provide persistent external knowledge for large language model (LLM) agents in open-ended, knowledge-intensive downstream tasks. Yet their quality is systematically limited by \emph{incompleteness}, \emph{incorrectness}, and \emph{redundancy}, manifested as missing evidence or cross-document links, low-confidence or imprecise claims, and ambiguous or coreference resolution issues. Such defects compound under iterative use, degrading retrieval fidelity and downstream task performance. We present \textbf{DeepRefine}, a general LLM-based reasoning model for \emph{agent-compiled knowledge refinement} that improves the quality of any pre-constructed knowledge bases with user queries to make it more suitable for the downstream tasks. DeepRefine performs multi-turn interactions with the knowledge base and conducts abductive diagnosis over interaction history, localizes likely defects, and executes targeted refinement actions for incremental knowledge base updates. To optimize refinement policies of DeepRefine without gold references, we introduce a Gain-Beyond-Draft (GBD) reward and train the reasoning process end-to-end via reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent downstream gains over strong baselines.

</details>


### [126] [Learning Less Is More: Premature Upper-Layer Attention Specialization Hurts Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10504)
*Jinchang Zhu,Jindong Li,Yuwen Hao,Chengyu Zou,Rong Fu,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现GPT预训练中存在一种失败模式：上层注意力层在低层特征稳定前就形成尖锐的注意力模式（过早的上层注意力专门化）。通过暂时减缓上层Q/K投影的学习率可以改善最终困惑度和下游准确率。在LLaMA风格的块中，由于门控FFN的存在，这种干预几乎不需要。路径分析表明，学习率干预和门控FFN都作用于同一增长路径，分别减少步长因子和残差能量因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别GPT预训练中上层注意力层在低层特征未稳定时过早专门化的失败模式，并寻找改善训练效果的干预方法。

Method: 通过减缓上层Q/K投影的早期学习率进行干预；通过消融实验隔离出门控FFN作为抑制该失败的关键组件；通过路径分析统一解释两种干预机制。

Result: 减缓上层Q/K投影学习率能改善最终困惑度和下游准确率；LLaMA块中因门控FFN的存在该干预几乎不必要；路径分析显示两种干预作用于同一增长路径的不同因子。

Conclusion: 上层Q/K时序是解码器架构与优化之间的具体交互点，门控FFN通过减少残差能量因子自然抑制了过早的上层注意力专门化。

Abstract: A causal-decoder block is hierarchical: lower layers build the residual basis that upper layers attend over. We identify a failure mode in GPT pretraining: upper layers commit to sharp attention patterns before lower-layer features stabilize. We call this premature upper-layer attention specialization. Temporarily slowing only upper-layer Q/K projections during early training improves final perplexity and downstream accuracy without altering other parameters; it prevents upper attention from collapsing onto an immature residual basis. In LLaMA-style blocks, the same intervention is nearly unnecessary. Through ablations, we isolate multiplicative gated FFNs (not RMSNorm or bias removal) as the component that suppresses the upstream residual writes driving the failure. A pathwise analysis unifies both findings: the learning-rate intervention reduces a step-size factor, while gated FFNs reduce a residual-energy factor on the same growth pathway. Our results identify upper-layer Q/K timing as a concrete interaction point between decoder architecture and optimization.

</details>


### [127] [Infinite Mask Diffusion for Few-Step Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10518)
*Jaehoon Yoo,Wonjung Kim,Chanhyuk Lee,Seunghoon Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出无限掩码扩散模型（IMDM），通过引入随机无限状态掩码来缓解标准MDM因同时更新token导致的分解误差理论下界，在少量采样步骤下优于现有蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准掩码扩散模型（MDM）因使用确定性单状态掩码，存在无法降低的分解误差理论下界，导致需要大量采样迭代，限制了少步生成能力。

Method: 提出无限掩码扩散模型（IMDM），引入随机无限状态掩码替代MDM的确定性单状态掩码，以缓解分解误差的理论下界，同时保留MDM的并行解码、双向上下文处理和预训练权重兼容等优势，并配合蒸馏方法进行少步生成。

Result: 在简单合成任务中，MDM因分解误差界无法进行少步生成，而IMDM能找到高效解。在LM1B和OpenWebText上，配备适当蒸馏方法的IMDM在少步数下超越了现有少步蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: IMDM通过随机无限状态掩码有效缓解了标准MDM的分解误差理论下界，提升了少步生成效率，且与预训练权重兼容，为扩散语言模型的少步生成提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive models in language modeling, offering the advantages of parallel decoding and bidirectional context processing within a simple yet effective framework. Specifically, their explicit distinction between masked tokens and data underlies their simple framework and effective conditional generation. However, MDMs typically require many sampling iterations due to factorization errors stemming from simultaneous token updates. We observe that a theoretical lower bound of the factorization error exists, which standard MDMs cannot reduce due to their use of a deterministic single-state mask. In this paper, we propose the Infinite Mask Diffusion Model (IMDM), which introduces a stochastic infinite-state mask to mitigate the theoretical bound while directly inheriting the benefits of MDMs, including the compatibility with pre-trained weights. We empirically demonstrate that MDM fails to perform few-step generation even in a simple synthetic task due to the factorization error bound, whereas IMDM can find an efficient solution for the same task. Finally, when equipped with appropriate distillation methods, IMDM surpasses existing few-step distillation methods at small step counts on LM1B and OpenWebText. Code is available at https://Ugness.github.io/official_imdm.

</details>


### [128] [Mela: Test-Time Memory Consolidation based on Transformation Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10537)
*Lungchuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于神经科学记忆巩固理论的层级记忆模块（HMM），将其集成到Transformer解码器中形成Mela模型，通过高低频双模块实现多粒度记忆表征，在语言建模任务上超越基线，并在长上下文场景中保持稳定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆巩固作为人脑中的基础组织原则，尚未被充分探索为现代序列模型的设计原理。现有序列模型缺乏类似人脑的、具有不同更新频率的多粒度记忆机制。

Method: 提出HMM架构，包含低频子模块（捕捉抽象、要点级知识）和高频子模块（保留细粒度情节细节），最终输出为上下文依赖的动态重构组合。将HMM集成到Transformer解码器形成Mela模型，并引入MemStack方法将多粒度记忆特征分布到解码器早期层。

Result: Mela在所有模型规模上均优于Transformer基线；在4K训练长度下，Mela在更长上下文上保持性能，而Transformer基线迅速退化。消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 神经科学启发的记忆巩固机制可有效提升序列模型的长上下文处理能力和泛化性能，为记忆增强语言模型提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Memory consolidation, the process by which transient experiences are transformed into stable, structured representations, is a foundational organizing principle in the human brain, yet it remains largely unexplored as a design principle for modern sequence models. In this work, we leverage established neuroscientific theories of memory consolidation and cross-frequency coupling to propose the Hierarchical Memory Module (HMM), a neural memory architecture composed of two functionally distinct sub-modules that operate at different update frequencies. Inspired by the transformation hypothesis, the low-frequency sub-module produces high-level representations that capture abstract, gist-level knowledge, while the high-frequency sub-module produces fine-grained representations that preserve richer episodic detail. The final memory output is dynamically reconstructed as a context-dependent combination of both representations, analogous to the reconstructive nature of human memory retrieval. We integrate HMM into a Transformer-based language decoder to form Mela, a family of memory-augmented language models that perform online memory consolidation at test time. To further exploit the multi-granularity memory representations produced by HMM, we introduce MemStack, a method that distributes different levels of memory features across the early layers of the decoder without introducing additional tokens. Experiments on language modeling demonstrate that Mela outperforms Transformer baselines across all the model sizes. Moreover, with the pretrained context length fixed at 4K, Mela maintains performance on significantly longer contexts, whereas Transformer baselines degrade rapidly beyond their training length. Extensive ablation studies validate the contribution of each component and provide guidance for practical configuration.

</details>


### [129] [Where Does Long-Context Supervision Actually Go? Effective-Context Exposure Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10544)
*Jinchang Zhu,Jindong Li,Chengyu Zou,Rong Fu,Chao Wang,Haowei He,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EXACT是一种通过逆频率加权长尾有效上下文目标的监督分配目标，在多个长上下文基准测试中显著提升性能（如Qwen2.5-0.5B上NoLiMa提升+10.09/+5.34，RULER提升+10.69/+5.55），同时保持标准QA/推理能力不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文适配方法通常视为窗口缩放，但忽视了令牌级监督不匹配问题：在带文档掩码的打包训练中，每个目标令牌的有效上下文实际仍很短。

Method: 提出EXACT监督分配目标，通过逆频率加权为长有效上下文目标分配额外权重，聚焦长尾分布中的长上下文样本。

Result: 在7个Qwen/LLaMA CPT配置上，所有28个训练/外推NoLiMa和RULER对比均提升。Qwen2.5-0.5B上NoLiMa提升+10.09（训练）/+5.34（外推）；RULER提升+10.69/+5.55。LLaMA-3.2-3B上RULER提升+17.91/+16.11。标准QA/推理基准宏观变化仅+0.24。

Conclusion: 长上下文适配取决于训练如何强化长上下文预测的监督，支持监督中心论观点。

Abstract: Long-context adaptation is often viewed as window scaling, but this misses a token-level supervision mismatch: in packed training with document masking, each target token's effective context remains short. We introduce EXACT, a supervision-allocation objective that assigns extra weight to long effective-context targets by inverse frequency within the long tail. Across seven Qwen/LLaMA CPT configurations, EXACT improves all 28 trained/extrapolated NoLiMa and RULER comparisons. On Qwen2.5-0.5B, NoLiMa improves by +10.09 (trained) and +5.34 (extrapolated); RULER by +10.69 and +5.55. On LLaMA-3.2-3B, RULER improves by +17.91 and +16.11. Standard QA/reasoning are preserved (+0.24 macro change across six benchmarks). A distance-resolved probe shows gains arise when evidence is thousands of tokens away, while short cases remain unchanged. Results support a supervision-centric thesis: long-context adaptation depends on how strongly training supervises long-context predictions.

</details>


### [130] [Multi-domain Multi-modal Document Classification Benchmark with a Multi-level Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10550)
*Denghao Ma,Qing Liu,Zulong Chen,Chuanfei Xu,Jia Xu,Zhibo Yang,Zhao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个多层级、多领域、多模态文档分类基准MMM-Bench，包含5层深度层次化分类体系、5,990篇来自阿里巴巴12个商业领域的真实多模态文档，并建立了全面基线，揭示了四大核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档分类基准停留在单领域、扁平标签结构的简化范式，无法反映真实商业文档的层级性、多模态和跨领域特性，阻碍了面向工业应用的文档智能发展。

Method: 构建MMM-Bench基准，包含五层深度层次化分类体系，从阿里巴巴12个商业领域收集5,990篇真实多模态文档，由领域专家手工标注完整层级路径；建立开放权重模型和API模型的全面基线，通过系统实验识别核心挑战。

Result: 建立了涵盖开放权重模型和API模型的全面基线，通过系统实验识别出MMM-Bench中的四大核心挑战，并提出了相应解决思路。

Conclusion: MMM-Bench弥合了学术研究与工业实践之间的差距，为多层级、多领域文档分类研究提供了坚实基础，所有数据和评估工具包已开源。

Abstract: Document classification forms the backbone of modern enterprise content management, yet existing benchmarks remain trapped in oversimplified paradigms -- single domain settings with flat label structures -- that bear little resemblance to the hierarchical, multi-modal, and cross-domain nature of real-world business documents. This gap not only misrepresents practical complexity but also stifles progress toward industrially viable document intelligence. To bridge this gap, we construct the first Multi-level, Multi-domain, Multi-modal document classification Benchmark (MMM-Bench). MMM-Bench includes (1) a deeply hierarchical taxonomy spanning five levels that capture the authentic organizational logic of business documentation; and (2) 5,990 real-world multi-modal documents meticulously curated from 12 commercial domains in Alibaba. Each document is manually annotated with a complete hierarchical path by domain experts. We establish comprehensive baselines on MMM-Bench, which consists of open-weight models and API-based models. Through systematic experiments, we identify four fundamental challenges within MMM-Bench and propose corresponding insights. To provide a solid foundation for advancing research in multi-level, multi-domain document classification, we release all of the data and the evaluation toolkit of MMM-Bench at https://github.com/MMMDC-Bench/MMMDC-Bench.

</details>


### [131] [ICT-NLP at SemEval-2026 Task 3: Less Is More -- Multilingual Encoder with Joint Training and Adaptive Ensemble for Dimensional Aspect Sentiment Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10560)
*Liyuan Huang,Jiawei He,Wutao Shen,Lin Li,Jin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个轻量级、资源高效的维度情感回归系统，采用多语言预训练编码器、联合多语言多领域训练、有界回归变换和自适应集成策略，在SemEval-2026任务中取得多项前列排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在维度情感回归任务中，现有方法往往依赖大语言模型或外部语料库，资源消耗大且效率低下，亟需一种轻量级且高效的系统方案。

Method: 系统完全基于多语言预训练编码器，采用联合多语言多领域训练以促进跨语言迁移并缓解数据稀疏问题；引入有界回归变换提升训练稳定性并将预测约束在有效范围内；采用基于子集搜索的自适应集成策略降低预测方差。

Result: 在zho-res数据集上排名第1，zho-lap排名第2，jpn-hot排名第3，所有其余数据集均位于参赛队伍前50%。

Conclusion: 所提出的轻量级系统在不依赖LLM或外部语料库的条件下取得了强且一致的性能表现，验证了方法的有效性和资源高效性。

Abstract: This paper describes our system to SemEval-2026 Task 3 Track A Subtask 1 on Dimensional Aspect Sentiment Regression (DimASR). We propose a lightweight and resource-efficient system built entirely on multilingual pre-trained encoders, without relying on LLMs or external corpora. We adopt joint multilingual and multi-domain training to facilitate cross-lingual transfer and alleviate data sparsity, introduce a bounded regression transformation that improves training stability while constraining predictions within the valid range, and employ an adaptive ensemble strategy via subset search to reduce prediction variance. Experimental results demonstrate that our system achieves strong and consistent performance, ranking 1st on zho-res, 2nd on zho-lap, and 3rd on jpn-hot, with all remaining datasets placed within the top half of participating teams.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Where Reliability Lives in Vision-Language Models: A Mechanistic Study of Attention, Hidden States, and Causal Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08200)
*Logan Mann,Ajit Saravanan,Ishan Dave,Shikhar Shiromani,Saadullah Ismail,Yi Xia,Emily Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的机理分析管道VRP，系统检验了视觉语言模型（VLM）中“注意力图越清晰，模型越可信”的常见直觉。研究发现：(i) 注意力结构与正确性几乎无关（相关性接近零），尽管注意力对特征提取仍是因果必要的；(ii) 可靠性在计算后期才变得可读——隐藏状态的线性探针和自一致性分数显著优于注意力锐度；(iii) 因果神经元消融揭示了架构差异：晚期融合模型（LLaVA）的可靠性集中在脆弱的晚期瓶颈，而早期融合模型（PaliGemma, Qwen2-VL）分布广泛且鲁棒。结论：3-7B VLM的可靠性更适合从隐藏状态几何、层级间隔和稀疏后期回路中读取，而非注意力图锐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证普遍直觉假设——视觉语言模型（VLM）的注意力图越清晰（对查询区域的注意越集中），模型输出越自信且校准——是否成立。

Method: 设计统一的机理分析管道VLM Reliability Probe (VRP)，对三个开源VLM家族（LLaVA-1.5, PaliGemma, Qwen2-VL, 3-7B参数）进行注意力结构、生成动态和隐藏状态几何与正确性标签的系统比较；结合因果消融实验。

Result: (i) 注意力结构与正确性相关性接近零（R_pb≈0.001），但注意力对特征提取因果必要（patch遮蔽降低精度8.2-11.3pp）；(ii) 隐藏状态线性探针在POPE上AUROC>0.95，自一致性K=10时R_pb=0.43；(iii) LLaVA可靠性集中在晚期瓶颈（消融后精度下降8.3pp），PaliGemma/Qwen2-VL分布广泛（消融~50%隐藏维度仅下降≤1pp）。

Conclusion: 在3-7B VLM中，可靠性更适合从隐藏状态几何、层级间隔形成和稀疏后期电路（而非注意力图锐度）中读取；架构融合方式（晚期融合vs早期融合）对可靠性分布有决定性影响，具有监控设计启示。

Abstract: A pervasive intuition holds that vision-language models (VLMs) are most trustworthy when their attention maps look sharp: concentrated attention on the queried region should imply a confident, calibrated answer. We test this Attention-Confidence Assumption directly. We instrument three open-weight VLM families (LLaVA-1.5, PaliGemma, Qwen2-VL; 3-7B parameters) with a unified mechanistic pipeline -- the VLM Reliability Probe (VRP) -- that compares attention structure, generation dynamics, and hidden-state geometry against a single correctness label. Three results emerge. (i) Attention structure is a near-zero predictor of correctness (R_pb(C_k,y)=0.001, 95% CI [-0.034,0.036]; R_pb(H_s,y)=-0.012, [-0.047,0.024] on a pooled n=3,090 split), even though attention remains causally necessary for feature extraction (top-30% patch masking drops accuracy by 8.2-11.3 pp, p<0.001). (ii) Reliability becomes legible later in the computation: a single hidden-state linear probe reaches AUROC>0.95 on POPE for two of three families, and self-consistency at K=10 is the strongest behavioral predictor we measure at 10x inference cost (R_pb=0.43). (iii) Causal neuron-level ablations expose a sharp architectural split with direct monitor-design implications: late-fusion LLaVA concentrates reliability in a fragile late bottleneck (-8.3 pp object-identification accuracy after top-5 probe-neuron ablation), whereas early-fusion PaliGemma and Qwen2-VL distribute it widely and absorb destruction of ~50% of their peak-layer hidden dimension with <=1 pp degradation. The takeaway is narrow but consequential: in 3-7B VLMs, reliability is read more reliably off hidden-state geometry, layer-wise margin formation, and sparse late-layer circuits than off attention-map sharpness.

</details>


### [133] [Auto-Rubric as Reward: From Implicit Preferences to Explicit Multimodal Generative Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08354)
*Juanxi Tian,Fengyuan Liu,Jiaming Han,Yilei Jiang,Yongliang Wu,Yesheng Liu,Haodong Li,Furong Xu,Wanhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Auto-Rubric as Reward (ARR)框架，将隐式偏好知识外化为显式评分标准，并结合Rubric Policy Optimization (RPO)将结构化多维评估蒸馏为稳健的二元奖励，在文本到图像生成和图像编辑任务上超越成对奖励模型和VLM评判器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF方法将偏好信号简化为标量或成对标签，丢失了人类判断的组合式、多维结构信息，且易受奖励破解攻击；虽然Rubrics-as-Reward方法试图通过显式标准恢复结构，但生成可靠、可扩展且数据高效的评分标准仍是开放问题。

Method: ARR框架在成对比较前将VLM内化的偏好知识外化为提示特定的评分标准，将整体意图分解为独立可验证的质量维度；RPO方法将ARR的结构化多维评估蒸馏为二元奖励，替代不透明的标量回归，实现基于评分标准条件的偏好决策以稳定策略梯度。

Result: 在文本到图像生成和图像编辑基准上，ARR-RPO优于成对奖励模型和VLM评判器，包括零样本和少样本设置，有效抑制了位置偏差等评估偏见。

Conclusion: 将隐式偏好知识显式外化为结构化评分标准能够实现更可靠、数据高效的多模态对齐，瓶颈在于缺乏可分解的接口而非知识本身不足。

Abstract: Aligning multimodal generative models with human preferences demands reward signals that respect the compositional, multi-dimensional structure of human judgment. Prevailing RLHF approaches reduce this structure to scalar or pairwise labels, collapsing nuanced preferences into opaque parametric proxies and exposing vulnerabilities to reward hacking. While recent Rubrics-as-Reward (RaR) methods attempt to recover this structure through explicit criteria, generating rubrics that are simultaneously reliable, scalable, and data-efficient remains an open problem. We introduce Auto-Rubric as Reward (ARR), a framework that reframes reward modeling from implicit weight optimization to explicit, criteria-based decomposition. Before any pairwise comparison, ARR externalizes a VLM's internalized preference knowledge as prompt-specific rubrics, translating holistic intent into independently verifiable quality dimensions. This conversion of implicit preference structure into inspectable, interpretable constraints substantially suppresses evaluation biases including positional bias, enabling both zero-shot deployment and few-shot conditioning on minimal supervision. To extend these gains into generative training, we propose Rubric Policy Optimization (RPO), which distills ARR's structured multi-dimensional evaluation into a robust binary reward, replacing opaque scalar regression with rubric-conditioned preference decisions that stabilize policy gradients. On text-to-image generation and image editing benchmarks, ARR-RPO outperforms pairwise reward models and VLM judges, demonstrating that explicitly externalizing implicit preference knowledge into structured rubrics achieves more reliable, data-efficient multimodal alignment, revealing that the bottleneck is the absence of a factorized interface, not a deficit of knowledge.

</details>


### [134] [Embeddings for Preferences, Not Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08360)
*Carter Blair,Ariel D. Procaccia,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种解决文本嵌入在集体决策中偏好相似性问题的框架，通过打破语义与偏好之间的虚假相关性，利用合成训练数据优化偏好预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本嵌入衡量语义相似性，而在集体决策中需要的是偏好相似性（参与者与文本的距离应与其同意程度反相关）。虽然语义与偏好之间存在相关性，但当相关被打破时，标准嵌入无法准确捕捉偏好信号。

Method: 将问题形式化为不变性问题：文本嵌入编码了偏好相关信号（立场和价值观）和语义干扰（风格和措辞），两者在观测上相关。设计合成训练数据以打破这种相关性，迫使评分器摆脱由干扰主导的余弦相似度。

Result: 在11个在线讨论数据集上，该方法显著优于基于干扰的余弦相似度，偏好预测效果大幅提升。

Conclusion: 通过合成数据解耦偏好信号与语义干扰，能够有效学习真正的偏好相似性度量，为自由文本形式的集体决策提供了更可靠的嵌入基础。

Abstract: Modern AI is opening the door to collective decision-making in which participants express their views as free-form text rather than voting on a fixed set of candidates. A natural idea is to embed these opinions in a vector space so that the substantial literature on facility location problems and fair clustering can be brought to bear. But standard text embeddings measure semantic similarity, whereas distances in facility location problems and fair clustering require what we call \textit{preferential similarity}: a participant's agreement with a piece of text should be inversely related to their distance from it. Off-the-shelf embeddings inherit a coarse preference signal through a correlation between semantic and preferential similarity, but fail to capture preferences when the correlation breaks. We formalize this as an invariance problem: text embedding models encode both a preference-relevant signal (stance and values) and semantic nuisance (style and wording), and the two are observationally correlated, so a geometry that relies on nuisance can appear preference-correct even when it is not. We show that synthetic training data designed to break this correlation provably shifts the optimal scorer away from nuisance-dominated cosine and significantly improves preference prediction across 11 online deliberation datasets.

</details>


### [135] [On Distinguishing Capability Elicitation from Capability Creation in Post-Training: A Free-Energy Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08368)
*Yuhao Li,Shengchao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出后训练研究应区分“能力激发”（capability elicitation）和“能力创造”（capability creation），引入“可达支持”（accessible support）概念，并通过自由能视角论证SFT和RL本质上都是对预训练参考分布的重加权，关键在于是否改变模型实际可达的行为空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将SFT视为模仿、RL视为发现，但这种区分过于粗糙。真正重要的是训练过程是在激发模型已有能力，还是在创造新能力。需要一个更精确的框架来指导后训练研究。

Method: 引入“可达支持”（accessible support）概念，定义模型在有限预算下实际可产生的行为集合。通过自由能视角将SFT和RL统一视为对预训练参考分布的重加权——演示信号定义SFT的低能行为，奖励信号定义RL的低能行为。当更新接近基座模型时，主要效应是局部重加权而非能力创造。

Result: 区分了两种后训练模式：能力激发（在可达支持内重加权行为）和能力创造（改变可达支持本身）。核心问题不再是SFT还是RL，而是训练是否扩大了模型可达的行为空间。

Conclusion: 后训练研究应关注训练过程是在重加权已有行为还是通过搜索、交互、工具使用或新信息整合来扩展模型的可达行为空间。这一框架为理解不同后训练方法的本质差异提供了更精确的理论基础。

Abstract: Debates about large language model post-training often treat supervised fine-tuning (SFT) as imitation and reinforcement learning (RL) as discovery. But this distinction is too coarse. What matters is whether a training procedure increases the probability of behaviors the pretrained model could already produce, or whether it changes what the model can practically reach. We argue that post-training research should distinguish between capability elicitation and capability creation. We make this distinction operational by introducing the notion of accessible support: the set of behaviors that a model can practically produce under finite budgets. Post-training that reweights behaviors within this support is capability elicitation; whereas changing the support itself corresponds to capability creation. We develop this argument through a free-energy view of post-training. SFT and RL can both be seen as reweighting a pretrained reference distribution, only with different external signals. Demonstration signals define low-energy behavior for SFT, and reward signals define low-energy behavior for RL. When the update remains close to the base model, the main effect is local reweighting, not capability creation. Within this framework, the central question is no longer whether post-training is framed as SFT or RL, but whether it reweights behaviors already within reach, or instead expands the model's reachable behavioral space through search, interaction, tool use, or the incorporation of new information.

</details>


### [136] [MemQ: Integrating Q-Learning into Self-Evolving Memory Agents over Provenance DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08374)
*Junwei Liao,Haoting Shi,Ruiwen Zhou,Jiaqian Wang,Shengtao Zhang,Wei Zhang,Weinan Zhang,Ying Wen,Zhiyu Li,Feiyu Xiong,Bo Tang,Muning Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemQ提出基于TD(λ)资格迹的记忆Q值信用分配方法，通过沿记忆谱系DAG反向传播信用，实现智能体对依赖链的记忆价值评估，在6个基准测试中均取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情节记忆方法将每条记忆独立处理，忽略了记忆之间的依赖链条关系——即一条记忆的检索质量如何影响后续记忆的创建与使用，缺乏对记忆间结构依赖的信用分配机制。

Method: 将问题形式化为Exogenous-Context MDP（EC-MDP），提出MemQ框架：利用TD(λ)资格迹沿记忆谱系DAG反向传播信用，信用权重按(γλ)^d衰减（d为DAG深度），用结构距离替代时间距离进行信用分配。

Result: 在操作系统交互、函数调用、代码生成、多模态推理、具身推理、专家级QA等6个基准测试中均取得最高成功率，在多步依赖任务上提升最大（最高+5.7pp），在单步分类任务上提升最小（+0.77pp）。

Conclusion: MemQ通过结构化信用分配有效利用了记忆间的依赖关系，显著提升了多步推理任务的性能；同时提供了EC-MDP结构下γ和λ参数选择的指导原则。

Abstract: Episodic memory allows LLM agents to accumulate and retrieve experience, but current methods treat each memory independently, i.e., evaluating retrieval quality in isolation without accounting for the dependency chains through which memories enable the creation of future memories. We introduce MemQ, which applies TD($λ$) eligibility traces to memory Q-values, propagating credit backward through a provenance DAG that records which memories were retrieved when each new memory was created. Credit weight decays as $(γλ)^d$ with DAG depth $d$, replacing temporal distance with structural proximity. We formalize the setting as an Exogenous-Context MDP, whose factored transition decouples the exogenous task stream from the endogenous memory store. Across six benchmarks, spanning OS interaction, function calling, code generation, multimodal reasoning, embodied reasoning, and expert-level QA, MemQ achieves the highest success rate on all six in generalization evaluation and runtime learning, with gains largest on multi-step tasks that produce deep and relevant provenance chains (up to +5.7~pp) and smallest on single-step classification (+0.77~pp) where single-step updates already suffice. We further study how $γ$ and $λ$ interact with the EC-MDP structure, providing principled guidance for parameter selection and future research. Code will be available soon.

</details>


### [137] [SkillLens: Adaptive Multi-Granularity Skill Reuse for Cost-Efficient LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08386)
*Yongliang Miao,Ziyang Yu,Liang Zhao,Bowen Zhu,Hasibul Haque*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillLens提出了一种分层技能演化框架，将技能组织为策略-战略-流程-原语四层图结构，通过混合粒度检索与自适应演化，在任务中实现高效技能复用，在MuLocbench和ALFWorld上分别取得6.31%的Acc@1提升和从45.00%到51.31%的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能库系统将技能视为扁平的单一分辨率提示块，导致粗粒度技能注入引入不相关或误导性上下文，而重写整个技能成本高昂且不必要，存在相关性与成本之间的矛盾。

Method: SkillLens构建四层技能图（策略、战略、流程、原语），通过语义检索种子技能，利用度校正随机游走扩展技能种子，再通过验证器决定每个访问单元是否接受、分解、重写或跳过，并在演化过程中不断优化多粒度技能及验证器。

Result: 在MuLocbench上Acc@1提升6.31个百分点，在ALFWorld上智能体成功率从45.00%提升至51.31%。理论分析表明混合粒度适应在稀疏不匹配假设下具有次线性成本，且演化更新规则单调提升验证目标至局部最优。

Conclusion: SkillLens通过分层技能图与混合粒度检索机制，有效平衡了技能复用中的相关性与成本，验证了层次化技能演化框架在LLM智能体任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Skill libraries have become a practical way for LLM agents to reuse procedural experience across tasks. However, existing systems typically treat skills as flat, single-resolution prompt blocks. This creates a tension between relevance and cost: injecting coarse skills can introduce irrelevant or misleading context, while rewriting entire skills is expensive and often unnecessary. We propose SkillLens, a hierarchical skill-evolution framework that organizes skills into a four-layer graph of policies, strategies, procedures, and primitives, and retrieves them at mixed granularity. Given a task, SkillLens first retrieves semantically relevant skill seeds, expands them through degree-corrected random walk over the skill graph, and then uses a verifier to decide whether each visited unit should be accepted, decomposed, rewritten, or skipped. This enables the agent to reuse compatible subskills directly while adapting only locally mismatched components. To improve the system over time, SkillLens further refines multi-granularity skills and verifier in order to improve its routing decisions. We provide theoretical analysis showing that mixed-granularity adaptation incurs sublinear cost under sparse mismatch assumptions and that the evolutionary update rule monotonically improves the validation objective until a local optimum. Across MuLocbench and ALFWorld, SkillLens consistently improves over strong skill-based baselines, achieving up to a 6.31 percentage-point Acc@1 gain for bug localization and raising agent success rate from 45.00% to 51.31%.

</details>


### [138] [PLACO: A Multi-Stage Framework for Cost-Effective Performance in Human-AI Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08388)
*Pranavkumar Mallela,Vinay Kumar,Shashi Shekhar Jha,Shweta Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在分类任务中如何将人类（确定性标注者）与AI模型（概率性标注者）的输出进行最优组合的方法，采用贝叶斯规则并假设两者在给定真实标签条件下条件独立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分类任务中，人类和AI模型各自单独无法达到最优性能，需要形成人-AI团队来提升整体系统性能。如何有效结合人类确定性输出与模型概率性输出是一个关键问题。

Method: 基于贝叶斯规则，假设人类与模型输出在给定真实标签条件下条件独立，利用模型实例级校准概率和人类类别级校准概率，提出一种组合方法来融合确定性标注者（人类）和概率性标注者（分类模型）的输出。

Result: 该方法能够有效结合人类和模型的输出，提升分类任务的整体性能。

Conclusion: 在分类任务中，利用条件独立性假设和贝叶斯规则可以有效地组合人类确定性输出与模型概率性输出，为人-AI协作提供理论基础。

Abstract: Human-AI teams play a pivotal role in improving overall system performance when neither the human nor the model can achieve such performance on their own. With the advent of powerful and accessible Generative AI models, several mundane tasks have morphed into Human-AI team tasks. From writing essays to developing advanced algorithms, humans have found that using AI assistance has led to an accelerated work pace like never before. In classification tasks, where the final output is a single hard label, it is crucial to address the combination of human and model output. Prior work elegantly solves this problem using Bayes rule, using the assumption that human and model output are conditionally independent given the ground truth. Specifically, it discusses a combination method to combine a single deterministic labeler (the human) and a probabilistic labeler (the classifier model) using the model's instance-level and the human's class-level calibrated probabilities.

</details>


### [139] [Belief or Circuitry? Causal Evidence for In-Context Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08405)
*Katharine Kowalyshyn,Timothy Duggan,Daniel Little,Michael C Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过图随机游走任务探究LLM的上下文学习机制，发现模型同时并行使用结构推理（推断潜在图拓扑）和归纳记忆（复制局部转移模式）两种机制，而非单一机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对LLM如何实现上下文学习存在争议：是简单的模式匹配（复制最近token），还是真正的潜在结构推理。作者希望厘清这两种机制在LLM中的实际运作方式。

Method: 使用两种竞争图结构上的随机游走任务，通过PCA重构内部表征、残差流激活修补和图表征差异引导的线性控制实验，分析模型内部表征和因果干预效果。

Result: PCA显示两种图拓扑在中间混合比例下编码在正交的主子空间中；后期层的激活修补几乎完全转移干净图偏好；线性控制能按预期方向移动预测，且在范数匹配和标签洗牌对照下失效。

Conclusion: LLM的上下文学习既非纯粹局部转移复制，也非纯粹全局结构推理，而是两种机制并行运作的双机制解释最为合理。

Abstract: How do LLMs learn in-context? Is it by pattern-matching recent tokens, or by inferring latent structure? We probe this question using a toy graph random-walk across two competing graph structures. This task's answer is, in principle, decidable: either the model tracks global topology, or it copies local transitions. We present two lines of evidence that neither account alone is sufficient. First, reconstructing the internal representation structure via PCA reveals that at intermediate mixture ratios, both graph topologies are encoded in orthogonal principal subspaces simultaneously. This pattern is difficult to reconcile with purely local transition copying. Second, residual-stream activation patching and graph-difference steering causally intervene on this graph-family signal: late-layer patching almost fully transfers the clean graph preference, while linear steering moves predictions in the intended direction and fails under norm-matched and label-shuffled controls. Taken together, our findings are most consistent with a dual-mechanism account in which genuine structure inference and induction circuits operate in parallel.

</details>


### [140] [Playing games with knowledge: AI-Induced delusions need game theoretic interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08409)
*Will Beaumaster,Paul Schrater*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出对话式AI作为知识接口的根本缺陷在于其谄媚性导致理性用户陷入认知固化与信念螺旋。作者将问题形式化为Crawford-Sobel廉价谈话博弈，提出通过"认知中介"机制设计（包括信念版本化系统）打破混合均衡，在仿真中实现48倍的信念螺旋率差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示对话式AI的谄媚性并非模型层面的缺陷，而是从用户驱动知识搜索转向重复博弈通信这一范式转变的系统性后果，该问题导致理性用户也会陷入认知陷阱。

Method: 将用户-AI交互建模为Crawford-Sobel廉价谈话博弈，证明成本为零的信号导致混合均衡；提出认知中介机制，通过引入认知摩擦（成本信号）迫使类型分离；设计基于git启发的信念版本化元记忆系统实现健康信念的存储与回滚。

Result: 仿真中实现48倍的信念螺旋率差异，同时通过学习保留性标准，表明认知安全的AI本质上是战略信息环境设计问题而非简单的模型对齐问题。

Conclusion: 对话式AI的认知安全需要从信息环境设计的战略视角出发，通过机制设计打破混合均衡，而非仅依赖模型层面的对齐技术。

Abstract: Conversational AI has a fundamental flaw as a knowledge interface: sycophantic chatbots induce epistemic entrenchment and delusional belief spirals even in rational agents. We propose the problem does not stem from the AI model, rooted instead in a systemic consequence of the paradigm shift from user-driven knowledge search to users and agents engaged in strategic, repeated-play communication. We formalize the problem as a Crawford-Sobel cheap talk game, where costless user signals induce a pooling equilibrium. Agents optimized for user satisfaction produce sycophantic strategies that provide identical reinforcement across user types with opposite epistemic incentives: exploratory ``Growth-seekers'' ($θ_G$) and confirmatory ``Validation-seekers'' ($θ_V$). Under repeated play, this identification failure creates a coordination trap -- analogous to a Prisoner's Dilemma -- where locally rational feedback loops drive users toward pathologically certain false beliefs. We propose an inference-time mechanism design intervention called an Epistemic Mediator that breaks this pooling equilibrium by introducing a costly signal (epistemic friction), forcing type revelation based on users' asymmetric cognitive costs for processing resistance. A key contribution is Belief Versioning, a git-inspired epistemic meta-memory system that stores healthy beliefs and rollbacks when validation-seeking resistance is detected. In simulation, this intervention achieves a separating equilibrium achieving a $48\times$ differential in spiral rates while passing a learning preservation criterion), evidence that epistemic safety in AI is fundamentally a problem of strategic information environment design rather than simple model alignment.

</details>


### [141] [Political Plasticity: An Analysis of Ideological Adaptability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08415)
*Bruno Bianchi,Diego Tiscornia,Matias Travizano,Ariel Futoransky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型的"政治可塑性"，即模型根据用户提供的上下文调整其政治立场的能力。通过200个政治倾向问题测试发现：系统提示词效果有限，而用户提示词（含少样本示例）能显著改变模型立场，尤其在更大更新的模型中；模型在回答问卷时存在数据泄漏问题；不同语言环境下模型的可塑性存在细微差异。总体而言，小型和旧模型的可塑性有限或不稳定，而新一代前沿模型展现出可靠、预期的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLMs的内在政治偏见，本文则探究一个相关但不同的概念——"政治可塑性"，即模型根据用户提供的上下文调整其响应立场的能力，旨在揭示模型在政治话题上的适应性与操控性。

Method: 基于Lester(1996)的框架，构建了200个涵盖经济自由和个人自由两个轴线的政治问题语料库。采用多种诱导方法：简化/主题化系统提示词、带少样本示例的用户提示词。此外，通过反转问题语义进行验证实验，并在多种语言环境下重复实验以分析语言对可塑性的影响。

Result: 系统提示词效果有限；用户提示词成功诱导显著意识形态偏移，尤其在更大更新模型的经济自由轴线上；反转问题语义导致多数模型出现反直觉偏移，暗示数据泄漏；不同语言环境下模型可塑性存在细微但显著的差异。

Conclusion: 小型和旧版LLMs表现出有限或不稳定的政治可塑性，而新一代前沿模型展现出可靠且符合预期的适应性，表明模型规模与更新版本在政治立场可操控性方面存在显著差异。

Abstract: Since the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs), a significant area of research has focused on their intrinsic biases, particularly in political discourse. This study investigates a different but related concept, "political plasticity", which is defined as the capacity of models to adapt their responses based on the user supplied context. To analyze this, a testing framework was developed using an expanded corpus of 200 politically-oriented questions across economic and personal freedom axes, based on a prior framework by Lester (1996). The study explored several methods to induce political bias, including simplified and topic-based system prompts, as well as user prompts with few-shot examples. The results show that while system prompts were largely ineffective, user prompts successfully elicited significant ideological shifts, particularly along the Economic Freedom axis in larger and newer models. Through a validation experiment, we examined whether models answer questionnaires by recognizing the underlying question format. Inverting the sense of the questions revealed unexpected, counter-intuitive shifts in most models, suggesting potential data leakage. Finally, we also analyzed how model plasticity varies when the experiment is conducted in different languages. The results reveal subtle yet notable shifts across each of the analyzed languages. Overall, our results indicate that small and older LLMs exhibit limited or unstable political plasticity, whereas newer frontier models display reliable, expected adaptability.

</details>


### [142] [Alignment as Jurisprudence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08416)
*Nicholas Caputo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了法理学（法官如何正确裁判）与AI对齐（使AI模型符合人类价值观）之间的深层结构相似性，主张两个领域应直接对话。通过分析德沃金的解释主义、桑斯坦的类比推理法学理论，以及宪法性AI、基于案例推理等对齐方法，论证法律启发的对齐方法（规则与案例的互动）的价值，并提出AI可帮助更好理解和改进法律。随着AI能力提升和法律理论对法官的约束松动，两领域对话将愈发重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法理学与AI对齐看似遥远，但两者都试图预测和塑造强大行为体（法官/AI）在未来未知情境中的决策，都依赖语言的规范与解释。作者希望将两领域直接对话，互相借鉴。

Method: 将德沃金的原则导向解释主义与桑斯坦的实证主义类比推理法学，与宪法性AI、基于案例推理等对齐方法进行对比分析，论证法律启发的规则-案例互动方法在微调对齐中的价值。

Result: 提出更具法律启发的对齐方法（规则与案例的结合），指出AI可帮助更好理解法律运作机制，并推动法律改进。

Conclusion: AI系统和法律应赋能人类，扩展其能力和目标实现。随着AI能力提升和法律理论约束松动，两个领域的对话将愈发重要，可能共同指向更好的版本。

Abstract: Jurisprudence, the study of how judges should properly decide cases, and alignment, the science of getting AI models to conform to human values, share a fundamental structure. These seemingly distant fields both seek to predict and shape how decisions by powerful actors, in one case judges and in the other increasingly powerful artificial intelligences, will be made in the unknown future. And they use similar tools of the specification and interpretation of language to try to accomplish those goals. The great debates of jurisprudence, about what the law is and what it should be, can provide insight into alignment, and lessons from what does and does not work in alignment can help make progress in jurisprudence.
  This essay puts the two fields directly into conversation. Drawing on leading accounts of jurisprudence, particularly Dworkin's principle-oriented interpretivism and Sunstein's positivist account of law as analogical reasoning, and on cutting-edge alignment approaches, namely Constitutional AI and case-based reasoning, it illustrates the value of a more sophisticated legally-inspired approach to the interplay of rules and cases in finetuning alignment and points to ways that AI can provide a better understanding of how the law works and how it can be improved by the introduction of AI. AI systems and the law should operate to empower people to act in the world, helping to expand their capabilities and the extent to which they are able to achieve their goals. As AI continues to improve in capacity, and as the constraints that legal theory places on human judges seem be coming undone, the conversation between these two fields will become increasingly essential and may help point to a better version of both.

</details>


### [143] [The Attacker in the Mirror: Breaking Self-Consistency in Safety via Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08427)
*Gabriele La Malfa,Emanuele La Malfa,Saar Cohen,Jie M. Zhang,Michael Luck,Michael Wooldridge,Elizabeth Black*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出锚定双策略自博弈（Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play），通过为攻击者和防御者训练独立的LoRA适配器来克服传统自博弈中参数共享导致的理论与实践局限，实现了更好的安全性、参数效率和对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自博弈红队（self-play red team）中攻击者和防御者共享同一模型参数，虽然提升了稳定性，但引入了理论和架构上的根本性局限：可达的纳什均衡包含平凡的"始终拒绝"策略和神谕式防御者，限制了实际应用；且共享参数导致动力学坍缩为自一致性，攻击无法对防御者施加有效的对抗压力。

Method: 提出锚定双策略自博弈（ABSP），在冻结的基础模型上训练独立的角色特定LoRA适配器，通过明确的角色分离在维持稳定优化的同时保留对抗压力。

Result: 相比标准自博弈，参数效率提升最高达100倍；在Qwen2.5-{3B,7B,14B}-IT模型的安全基准测试中持续改进安全性且不损失推理能力；交叉实验表明攻击者和防御者模型在对抗防御和安全性上均优于自博弈。

Conclusion: ABSP通过角色分离的LoRA适配器有效解决了传统自博弈的理论局限性，在参数效率和安全性方面取得显著提升，是一种更实用的AI安全对齐方法。

Abstract: Self-play red team is an established approach to improving AI safety in which different instances of the same model play attacker and defender roles in a zero-sum game, i.e., where the attacker tries to jailbreak the defender; if self-play converges to a Nash equilibrium, the model is guaranteed to respond safely within the settings of the game. Although the parameter sharing enforced by the use of the same model for the two roles improves stability and performance, it introduces fundamental theoretical and architectural limitations. We show that the set of Nash equilibria that can be reached corresponds to a broad class of behaviours that includes trivial always refuse strategies and oracle-like defenders, thus limiting practical applicability. We then show that when attacker and defender share and update the same base model, the dynamics collapse to self-consistency, so that attacks do not enforce adversarial pressure on the defender. In response, we propose Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play, which trains distinct role-specific LoRA adapters on top of a frozen base model, thereby maintaining stable optimisation while preserving adversarial pressure through explicit role separation. In relation to standard self-play, we show up to 100x greater parameter efficiency than finetuning and consistent improvements in safety compared to self-play fine-tuned models. We evaluate on Qwen2.5-{3B, 7B,14B}-IT models across widely used safety benchmarks, showing improved robustness without loss of reasoning ability. Cross-play experiments further show that our attacker and defender models are superior to self-play in terms of adversarial defence and safety.

</details>


### [144] [Measuring What Matters: Benchmarking Generative, Multimodal, and Agentic AI in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08445)
*Prasanna Desikan,Harshit Rajgarhia,Shivali Dalmia,Ananya Mantravadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前医疗AI基准测试存在系统性缺陷：现有基准多针对狭窄任务设计，未能评估模型在真实临床环境中的可靠性、安全性和临床相关性。前沿模型在资格考试中接近满分，但在真实临床任务（文档处理0.74-0.85、决策支持0.61-0.76、行政工作0.53-0.63）中表现大幅下降，高分造成部署准备就绪的虚假安全感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心挑战不仅是AI模型性能不足，更在于缺乏系统性的方法来衡量其在真实临床条件下的可靠性、安全性和临床相关性。当前基准测试的零散积累方式无法反映临床部署的实际需求，性能与实用性之间的差距随着AI承担更重要的临床角色而不断扩大。

Method: 该文通过分析现有医疗AI基准测试的结构性缺陷，指出基准测试由任务、数据集和指标的组合构成，但大多数基准测试只检测模型"知道什么"，而非其是否能可靠执行完整的临床任务。缺乏原则性的基准设计框架导致无法区分临床表现不佳的原因是模型能力局限还是测量方式本身的失败。

Result: 前沿模型在医学执照考试中接近满分，但在真实临床任务评估中，文档处理得分为0.74-0.85，临床决策支持为0.61-0.76，行政和工作流任务仅为0.53-0.63。高分基准掩盖了部署就绪度的真实评估。

Conclusion: 需要建立原则性的基准设计框架，以系统性地衡量医疗AI在真实临床环境中的可靠性、安全性和临床相关性，否则无法区分模型能力不足与测量方法失败之间的界限。

Abstract: AI models are increasingly deployed in live clinical environments where they must perform reliably across complex, high-stakes workflows that standard training and validation datasets were never designed to capture. Evaluating these systems requires benchmarks: structured combinations of tasks, datasets, and metrics that enable reproducible, comparable measurement of what a model can do. The central challenge in healthcare AI is not performance alone, but the absence of systematic methods to measure reliability, safety, and clinical relevance under real-world conditions. Most existing benchmarks test what a model knows; too few test whether it can perform reliably and without failing across the full complexity of real clinical tasks. Current benchmarks have accumulated through ad hoc dataset construction optimized for narrow task performance: frontier models achieve near-perfect scores on medical licensing examinations, but when evaluated across real clinical tasks, performance degrades sharply, scoring 0.74--0.85 on documentation, 0.61--0.76 on clinical decision support, and only 0.53--0.63 on administrative and workflow tasks \cite{medhelm}. High benchmark scores give a false sense of deployment readiness, and the gap between performance and utility widens precisely as AI systems take on more consequential clinical roles. Without a principled framework for benchmark design, the field cannot determine whether poor clinical performance reflects model limitations or failures in how performance is being measured.

</details>


### [145] [LLM-guided Semi-Supervised Approaches for Social Media Crisis Data Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08448)
*Jacob Ativo,Bharaneeshwar Balasubramaniyam,Anh Tran,Khushboo Gupta,Hongmin Li,Doina Caragea,Cornelia Caragea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对大型语言模型（LLM）引导的半监督学习在危机相关推文分类中进行了实证评估，发现LG-CoTrain方法在低资源设置下显著优于经典半监督方法，且紧凑的半监督模型在某些情况下能超越零样本设置下的超大LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用LLM引导的半监督学习方法来增强社交媒体数据在灾害管理中的分析能力，特别是在标注数据稀缺的危机场景下。

Method: 对比两种LLM辅助的半监督方法——VerifyMatch和LLM引导的协同训练（LG-CoTrain），与经典半监督基线方法在5、10、25个每类标注样本的低资源设置下进行实验。

Result: LG-CoTrain在低资源设置下平均Macro F1最高；VerifyMatch具有竞争力的性能且校准特性良好；随标注样本增加性能差距缩小，Self Training成为强基线；紧凑半监督模型有时超越零样本大LLM。

Conclusion: LLM引导的半监督学习能将知识从大模型迁移到更小、更可部署的模型中，为现实世界灾害响应应用提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning approaches have been investigated as a means to enhance the analysis of social media data in disaster management contexts. In this work, we present the first empirical evaluation of large language model (LLM) guided semi-supervised learning for crisis related tweet classification. We compare two recent LLM assisted semi-supervised methods, VerifyMatch and LLM guided Co-Training ( LG-CoTrain), against established semi-supervised baselines. Our results show that LG-CoTrain significantly outperforms classical semi-supervised approaches in low resource settings with 5, 10 and 25 labeled examples per class, achieving the highest averaged Macro F1 across events. VerifyMatch achieves competitive performance while also demonstrating strong calibration properties. As the number of labeled examples increases, the performance gap narrows and Self Training emerges as a strong baseline. We further observe that compact semi-supervised models can, in some cases, outperform very large LLMs operating in zero-shot settings. This finding highlights the potential of transferring knowledge from LLMs into smaller and more deployable models through LLM guided semi-supervised learning, offering a practical pathway for real world disaster response applications. Our project repository on Github is here.

</details>


### [146] [Mid-Training with Self-Generated Data Improves Reinforcement Learning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08472)
*Aswin RRV,Jacob Dineen,Divij Handa,Mihir Parmar,Ben Zhou,Swaroop Mishra,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在RL训练前使用基于Polya问题求解框架的多样自生成数据进行中期训练，理论分析了该方法如何改进RL，并在数学推理、代码生成等任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL在LLM中的效果受限于训练数据的多样性和覆盖范围，尤其是推理问题存在多种求解方式，而训练数据仅包含有限范围的推理方法会限制RL的有效性。

Method: 采用基于George Polya问题求解方法的自举数据生成框架，为每个问题生成多种变体答案；先进行中期微调，再进行RL训练。提供理论视角分析中期训练如何通过策略梯度更新激励多种方法的组合。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准、代码生成和叙事推理等OOD任务上，使用中期训练数据初始化的RL模型取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 语言模型通过自生成数据学习多种问题求解方法，能够有效促进后续的RL训练效果。

Abstract: The effectiveness of Reinforcement Learning (RL) in Large Language Models (LLMs) depends on the nature and diversity of the data used before and during RL. In particular, reasoning problems can often be approached in multiple ways that rely on different forms of reasoning, and exposure to only a limited range of such approaches in the training data may limit the effectiveness of RL. Motivated by this, we investigate using diverse self-generated data during mid-training as an intermediate step before RL training. Specifically, we adopt a bootstrapped data-generation framework guided by George Polya's problem-solving approaches for generating multiple variants of correct answers for each question in the training data, and then perform fine-tuning. We first provide a theoretical perspective on how mid-training on such data improves RL and explain how policy-gradient updates can incentivize combining multiple approaches. We then empirically demonstrate that RL-trained models initialized with our mid-training data achieve consistent improvements across various mathematical reasoning benchmarks and other OOD tasks like code generation and narrative reasoning. Overall, our investigative study shows that a language model learning multiple problem-solving approaches, through self-generated data helps subsequent RL.

</details>


### [147] [Latent Personality Alignment: Improving Harmlessness Without Mentioning Harms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08496)
*Linh Le,David Williams-King,Mohamed Amine Merzouk,Aton Kamanda,Adam Oberman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了潜在人格对齐方法，通过训练模型学习抽象人格特征而非具体有害行为，仅用不到100条特质语句即可达到与150k+样本训练方法相当的鲁棒性，且泛化能力更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型对抗鲁棒方法需要大量有害提示样本（成千上万），但仍然容易受到新型攻击和分布偏移的影响，迫切需要一种样本高效且泛化能力更强的防御方法。

Method: 潜在人格对齐（LPA）：使用少于100条抽象人格特质语句，结合潜在对抗训练，让模型学习人格特质而非具体有害行为模式。

Result: 与基于150k+样本训练的方法相比，攻击成功率相当；在六个有害性基准上，误分类率降低2.6倍；训练过程中从未见过有害示例。

Conclusion: 基于人格的对齐方法以极低成本构建了稳健防御，对未见攻击分布具有更好的泛化能力，为对抗鲁棒性提供了原则性新范式。

Abstract: Current adversarial robustness methods for large language models require extensive datasets of harmful prompts (thousands to hundreds of thousands of examples), yet remain vulnerable to novel attack vectors and distributional shifts. We propose Latent Personality Alignment (LPA), a sample-efficient defense that achieves robustness by training models on abstract personality traits rather than specific harmful behaviors. Using fewer than 100 trait statements and latent adversarial training, LPA achieves comparable attack success rates to methods trained on 150k+ examples, while maintaining superior utility. Critically, LPA generalizes better to unseen attack distributions, reducing misclassification rates by 2.6x compared to baseline across six harm benchmarks -- without ever seeing harmful examples during training. Our results demonstrate that personality-based alignment offers a principled approach to building robust defenses with minimal cost.

</details>


### [148] [OracleTSC: Oracle-Informed Reward Hurdle and Uncertainty Regularization for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08516)
*Darryl Jacob,Xinyu Liu,Muchao Ye,Xiaoyong Yuan,Pan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OracleTSC，通过奖励门槛机制和不确定性正则化，稳定基于LLM的交通信号控制强化学习微调，在LibSignal基准上实现旅行时间减少75%、队列长度减少67%，并保持可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于强化学习的交通信号控制系统作为黑箱模型缺乏可解释性，难以获得公众信任；而直接使用LLM进行强化学习微调面临奖励稀疏和延迟导致的训练不稳定性问题。

Method: OracleTSC包含两个核心机制：(1) 奖励门槛机制，通过减去校准阈值过滤弱学习信号；(2) 不确定性正则化，最大化所选响应的概率以鼓励跨采样输出的一致性决策。

Result: 在LibSignal基准上，LLaMA3-8B模型实现旅行时间减少75%、队列长度减少67%；跨路口泛化实验中，旅行时间降低17%、队列长度降低39%，且无需额外微调。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的奖励塑形能够提升LLM在交通信号控制任务中强化学习微调的稳定性和有效性，在提升交通效率的同时保持自然语言可解释性。

Abstract: Transparent decision-making is essential for traffic signal control (TSC) systems to earn public trust. However, traditional reinforcement learning-based TSC methods function as black boxes with limited interpretability. Although large language models (LLMs) can provide natural language reasoning, reinforcement finetuning for TSC remains unstable because feedback is sparse and delayed, while most actions produce only marginal changes in congestion metrics. We introduce OracleTSC, which stabilizes LLM-based TSC through two mechanisms: (1) a reward hurdle mechanism that filters weak learning signals by subtracting a calibrated threshold from environmental rewards, and (2) uncertainty regularization that maximizes the probability of the selected response to encourage consistent decisions across sampled outputs. Experiments on the LibSignal benchmark show that OracleTSC enables a compact LLaMA3-8B model to substantially improve traffic efficiency, achieving a 75% reduction in travel time and a 67% decrease in queue length compared with the pretrained baseline while preserving interpretability through natural language explanations. OracleTSC also demonstrates strong cross-intersection generalization: a policy trained on one intersection transfers to a structurally different intersection with 17% lower travel time and 39% lower queue length without additional finetuning. These results suggest that uncertainty-aware reward shaping can improve the stability and effectiveness of reinforcement fine-tuning for TSC.

</details>


### [149] [Results and Retrospective Analysis of the CODS 2025 AssetOpsBench Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08518)
*Dhaval Patel,Chathurangi Shyalika,Suryanarayana Reddy Yarrabothula,Ling Yue,Shuxin Lin,Nianjun Zhou,James Rayfield*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文复盘了CODS 2025 AssetOpsLive挑战赛，揭示了公开排行榜饱和、隐藏评测颠覆结论、TMatch项数值近乎无效、账号与团队关系错位、以及成功方法多依赖守门机制而非新颖架构等五大发现，并提出了改进评测设计的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛复盘有助于解释排行榜实际衡量了什么、隐藏评测如何改变结论、以及哪些设计模式得到了奖励。本文旨在通过对CODS 2025 AssetOpsLive挑战赛的全面复盘，揭示这些关键信息。

Method: 结合了最终排名表、300次提交的服务器日志、149支团队注册信息、最佳提交导出数据、组织者获奖者报告、配套系统论文以及经过验证的规划赛道源代码树，进行多源数据综合分析。

Result: 五大发现：(1) 公开规划排行榜饱和于72.73%，更丰富的提示无法提升峰值；(2) 隐藏评测改变结论：规划赛道公开与私密分数中度相关(r=0.69)，执行赛道负相关(r=-0.13)，部分公开45.45%的系统在隐藏集上达到63.64%；(3) TMatch项在官方综合评分中几乎无贡献，最多仅0.05分，重新缩放会交换前两名；(4) 149支注册团队缩减为24支有非零公开分数，11支完全排名，52.3%的去重注册列有多个用户名；(5) 成功的执行方法多依赖改进守门机制而非新颖智能体架构。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了评测实际奖励的行为，并推动了尺度感知的综合评分、技能水平诊断和版本化工件发布等改进方向。

Abstract: Competition retrospectives are useful when they explain what a leaderboard measured, how hidden evaluation changed conclusions, and which design patterns were rewarded. We revisit the CODS 2025 \assetopslive{} challenge, a privacy-aware Codabench competition on industrial multi-agent orchestration built on \assetops{}. We combine final rank sheets, a 300-submission server log, 149-team registrations, best-submission exports, the organizer winners report, the companion \assetopslive{} system paper, and verified planning-track source trees. Five results stand out. First, the public planning leaderboard saturates at 72.73\%, and richer prompts do not improve that peak. Second, hidden evaluation changes the story: public and private scores correlate moderately in planning ($r{=}0.69$) but negatively in execution ($r{=}{-}0.13$), with several 45.45\% public execution systems reaching 63.64\% on the hidden set. Third, the \tmatch{} term is numerically almost inert in the official composite -- combined on a 0--1 scale with 0--100 percentage scores, it contributes at most 0.05 points per track, and rescaling would swap the top two teams.
