Abstract: While experience replay is essential for data efficiency in reinforcement learning (RL), standard methods treat the replay buffer as a passive memory system, prioritizing samples based on numerical prediction errors rather than their semantic significance. This approach stands in contrast to human learning, which accelerates mastery by actively abstracting fragmented experiences into behavioral rules. To bridge this gap, we propose Neuro-Symbolic Experience Replay (NSER), a framework that transforms experience replay from a passive sample reuse mechanism into an active engine for knowledge construction. Specifically, NSER addresses the incompatibility between linguistic reasoning and numerical optimization through a novel neuro-symbolic grounding pipeline. It leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) in a zero-shot manner to induce candidate behavioral rules from accumulated trajectories, grounds these insights into differentiable first-order logic representations, and utilizes the resulting symbolic structures to dynamically reweight the replay distribution. By allowing abstract knowledge to directly shape policy optimization, NSER achieves consistent superior sample efficiency and convergence speed across reactive, rule-based, and procedural benchmarks.

</details>


### [225] [SimWorld Studio: Automatic Environment Generation with Evolving Coding Agent for Embodied Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09423)
*Haoqiang Kang,Xiaokang Ye,Yuhan Liu,Siddhant Hitesh Mantri,Lingjun Mao,James Fleming,Drishti Regmi,Lianhui Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SimWorld Studio是基于Unreal Engine 5的开源平台，通过SimCoder编码智能体从语言/图像指令自动生成可部署的3D物理世界，并支持环境与具身智能体共进化，在导航任务中显著提升智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体缺乏丰富、多样且可自动生成的3D环境用于交互式学习；已有的仿真器依赖人工场景或程序化模板，而基于LLM的3D生成系统主要产生静态场景，无法提供可验证任务和标准学习接口。

Method: 构建SimWorld Studio平台，核心是SimCoder——一个工具/技能增强的编码智能体，能编写和执行引擎级代码从语言/图像指令构建物理3D世界；SimCoder通过验证反馈（编译错误、物理检查、VLM批评）自我进化，自主添加可复用工具和技能；生成的世界导出为Gym风格环境；进一步支持环境生成与具身学习的共进化，利用智能体性能反馈指导SimCoder生成自适应课程。

Result: 三个具身导航案例研究表明：自我进化提升了生成可靠性；生成的环境显著提升了具身智能体性能，且泛化到未见过的基准测试；共进化相比固定环境学习获得18个百分点的成功率提升，相比未训练智能体提升40个百分点。

Conclusion: SimWorld Studio通过自动化生成可演化的3D学习环境，有效解决了具身智能体训练环境稀缺的问题，且环境-智能体共进化机制能持续推动学习能力提升。

Abstract: LLM/VLM-based digital agents have advanced rapidly thanks to scalable sandboxes for coding, web navigation, and computer use, which provide rich interactive training grounds. In contrast, embodied agents still lack abundant, diverse, and automatically generated 3D environments for interactive learning. Existing embodied simulators rely on manually crafted scenes or procedural templates, while recent LLM-based 3D generation systems mainly produce static scenes rather than deployable environments with verifiable tasks and standard learning interfaces. We introduce SimWorld Studio, an open-source platform built on Unreal Engine 5 for generating evolving embodied learning environments. At its core is SimCoder, a tool/skill-augmented coding agent that writes and executes engine-level code to construct physically grounded 3D worlds from language/image instructions. SimCoder self-evolves by using verifier feedback (e.g., compilation errors, physics checks, VLM critiques) to revise environments and autonomously add reusable tools and skills to its library. Generated worlds are exported as Gym-style environments for embodied agent learning. SimWorld Studio further enables co-evolution between environment generation and embodied learning: agent performance feedback guides SimCoder to generate adaptive curricula near the learner's capability frontier, so that environments become increasingly challenging as the embodied agent improves. Three case studies on embodied navigation show that self-evolution improves generation reliability, generated environments substantially improve embodied agent performance that generalizes to unseen benchmarks, and co-evolution yields an 18-point success-rate gain over fixed-environment learning and a 40-point gain over an untrained agent.

</details>


### [226] [VulTriage: Triple-Path Context Augmentation for LLM-Based Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09461)
*Wenxin Tang,Xiang Zhang,Junliang Liu,Jingyu Xiao,Xi Xiao,Jinlong Yang,Yuehe Ma,Zhenyu Liu,Zhengheng Li,Zicheng Wang,Wang Luo,Qing Li,Lei Wang,Peng Xiangli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VulTriage 提出了一种三路径上下文增强框架，通过控制路径（AST/CFG/DFG）、知识路径（CWE检索）和语义路径（功能摘要）来增强LLM的漏洞检测能力，在PrimeVul对集上达到SOTA，并展现了跨语言泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的方法难以捕捉结构依赖、领域特定的漏洞知识和复杂程序语义；直接使用LLM对原始源码进行漏洞检测容易遗漏漏洞或产生误报，尤其在脆弱函数与良性函数仅存在细微语义差异时。

Method: VulTriage 采用三路径上下文增强框架：（1）控制路径：提取并言语化AST、CFG、DFG信息，暴露控制流和数据流依赖；（2）知识路径：通过混合稠密-稀疏检索获取相关的CWE漏洞模式和示例；（3）语义路径：在最终判断前对代码的功能行为进行摘要。三者整合为统一指令，引导LLM进行更可靠的漏洞推理。

Result: 在PrimeVul对测试集上达到SOTA，优于现有深度学习和LLM基线方法；消融实验验证了每条路径的有效性；在Kotlin数据集上的实验展示了低资源和类别不平衡场景下的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 三路径上下文增强能有效提升LLM在漏洞检测任务中的表现，且具有跨语言迁移的良好泛化性。

Abstract: Automated vulnerability detection is a fundamental task in software security, yet existing learning-based methods still struggle to capture the structural dependencies, domain-specific vulnerability knowledge, and complex program semantics required for accurate detection. Recent Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong code understanding ability, but directly prompting them with raw source code often leads to missed vulnerabilities or false alarms, especially when vulnerable and benign functions differ only in subtle semantic details. To address this, we propose VulTriage, a triple-path context augmentation framework for LLM-based vulnerability detection. VulTriage enhances the LLM input through three complementary paths: a Control Path that extracts and verbalizes AST, CFG, and DFG information to expose control and data dependencies; a Knowledge Path that retrieves relevant CWE-derived vulnerability patterns and examples through hybrid dense--sparse retrieval; and a Semantic Path that summarizes the functional behavior of the code before the final judgment. These contexts are integrated into a unified instruction to guide the LLM toward more reliable vulnerability reasoning. Experiments on the PrimeVul pair test set show that VulTriage achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing deep learning and LLM-based baselines on key pair-wise and classification metrics. Further ablation studies verify the effectiveness of each path, and additional experiments on the Kotlin dataset demonstrate the generalization ability of VulTriage under low-resource and class-imbalanced settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/vinsontang1/VulTriage

</details>


### [227] [EpiGraph: A Knowledge Graph and Benchmark for Evidence-Intensive Reasoning in Epilepsy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09505)
*Yuyang Dai,Zheng Chen,Jathurshan Pradeepkumar,Yasuko Matsubara,Jimeng Sun,Yasushi Sakurai,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了癫痫领域大规模知识图谱EpiGraph（包含24,324个实体、32,009个三元组）及配套基准EpiBench（5项临床任务），验证了知识图谱增强的大语言模型在癫痫临床推理中的有效性，其中药物基因组推理提升达30-41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫诊断与治疗需要跨异构临床知识（生物信号、遗传机制、药物基因组学、治疗策略及患者预后）的密集型证据推理，现有模型缺乏结构化知识支撑。

Method: 从48,166篇同行评审论文和7个临床资源中构建异构图（5个临床层级），定义5项临床任务（临床决策、脑电图报告生成、药物基因组精准医学、治疗推荐、深度研究规划），在标准设置和Graph-RAG设置下评估6种LLM。

Result: 知识图谱增强持续提升所有任务性能，药物基因组推理提升最显著（30-41%）。

Conclusion: 结构化癫痫知识能显著增强基于证据的临床推理能力，为现实神经病学场景中知识增强型LLM的评估提供了实用基准框架。

Abstract: Epilepsy diagnosis and treatment require evidence-intensive reasoning across heterogeneous clinical knowledge, including biosignal patterns, genetic mechanisms, pharmacogenomics, treatment strategies, and patient outcomes. In this work, we present \textsc{EpiGraph}, a large-scale epilepsy knowledge graph and benchmark for evaluating knowledge-augmented clinical reasoning. \textsc{EpiGraph} integrates 48,166 peer-reviewed papers and seven clinical resources into a heterogeneous graph containing 24,324 entities and 32,009 evidence-grounded triplets across five clinical layers. Built upon this graph, \textsc{EpiBench} defines five clinically motivated tasks spanning clinical decision-making, EEG report generation, pharmacogenomic precision medicine, treatment recommendation, and deep research planning. We evaluate six LLMs under both standard and Graph-RAG settings. Results show that integrating \textsc{EpiGraph} consistently improves performance across all tasks, with the largest gains observed in pharmacogenomic reasoning (+30--41\%). Our findings demonstrate that structured epilepsy knowledge substantially enhances evidence-grounded clinical reasoning and provides a practical benchmark framework for evaluating knowledge-augmented LLMs in real-world neurological settings. Our code is available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/EEG-KG.

</details>


### [228] [Weighted Rules under the Stable Model Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09519)
*Joohyung Lee,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在稳定模型语义下引入加权规则概念，借鉴马尔可夫逻辑的对数线性模型，为回答集程序提供概率化扩展，支持不一致消解、稳定模型排序与概率关联，并与ASP、马尔可夫逻辑、ProbLog、P-log等形式化方法进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定模型语义本质上是确定性的，缺乏处理不确定性、不一致性以及概率推理的能力。作者希望借鉴马尔可夫逻辑中成功的对数线性模型思想，将加权规则引入稳定模型语义，从而为回答集程序赋予概率化、灵活的表达能力。

Method: 借鉴马尔可夫逻辑的对数线性模型框架，在稳定模型语义中引入加权规则，每个规则关联一个权重，通过权重定义稳定模型的概率分布（如吉布斯分布形式），从而实现不确定性建模。

Result: 提出了加权稳定模型的形式化定义，展示了该方法在解决回答集程序不一致性、对稳定模型进行排序和概率赋值等方面的有效性，并与ASP、马尔可夫逻辑、ProbLog、P-log进行了形式化比较，明确了异同关系。

Conclusion: 加权规则为稳定模型语义提供了一种灵活的概率化扩展，能够有效处理不确定性和不一致性，具有良好的形式化基础，且与多种相关形式化方法存在联系与差异。

Abstract: We introduce the concept of weighted rules under the stable model semantics following the log-linear models of Markov Logic. This provides versatile methods to overcome the deterministic nature of the stable model semantics, such as resolving inconsistencies in answer set programs, ranking stable models, associating probability to stable models, and applying statistical inference to computing weighted stable models. We also present formal comparisons with related formalisms, such as answer set programs, Markov Logic, ProbLog, and P-log.

</details>


### [229] [Cplus2ASP: Computing Action Language C+ in Answer Set Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09528)
*Joseph Babb,Joohyung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Cplus2ASP Version 2系统，它实现了C+动作语言的确定子片段，通过现代回答集求解技术大幅提升性能，并支持增量执行模式、外部原子、用户交互模式以及多模态翻译扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升C+动作语言求解器的性能，将现代回答集求解技术（如增量推理、嵌套表达式模块定理）整合到系统中，同时保持对Causal Calculator Version 2的语言兼容性。

Method: 系统采用工具链架构（f2lp, clingo, iclingo, as2transition），组合多个理论成果实现翻译；增量模式下将C+描述翻译为iclingo输入语言，并利用增量接地机制；支持Lua外部原子调用和用户交互模式。

Result: 新系统在完全兼容Causal Calculator Version 2语言的基础上，实现了显著的性能提升，并扩展了外部原子、交互模式等实用功能，同时支持动作语言B和BC的多模态翻译。

Conclusion: Cplus2ASP Version 2凭借增量推理和模块定理等理论成果，在保持语言兼容性的同时大幅提升了求解效率，并提供了丰富的扩展功能，适用于多种动作语言的翻译与求解。

Abstract: We present Version 2 of system Cplus2ASP, which implements the definite fragment of action language C+. Its input language is fully compatible with the language of the Causal Calculator Version 2, but the new system is significantly faster thanks to modern answer set solving techniques. The translation implemented in the system is a composition of several recent theoretical results. The system orchestrates a tool chain, consisting of f2lp, clingo, iclingo, and as2transition. Under the incremental execution mode, the system translates a C+ description into the input language of iclingo, exploiting its incremental grounding mechanism. The correctness of this execution is justified by the module theorem extended to programs with nested expressions. In addition, the input language of the system has many useful features, such as external atoms by means of Lua calls and the user interactive mode. The system supports extensible multi-modal translations for other action languages, such as B and BC, as well.

</details>


### [230] [LLM-Guided Monte Carlo Tree Search over Knowledge Graphs: Composing Mechanistic Explanations for Drug-Disease Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09542)
*Rishabh Jakhar,Michel Dumontier,Remzi Celebi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TESSERA是一个神经符号框架，结合LLM的局部判别能力、知识图谱的结构约束和MCTS的长程搜索，用于从知识图谱中提取多步解释，在药物机制阐明任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从知识图谱中提取多步解释面临组合爆炸和信用分配两大挑战，而前沿LLM虽然具备丰富的知识/推理能力，但其知识无保证且组合性能随链长增加而下降。

Method: 提出TESSERA三部分神经符号框架：1) 知识图谱定义假设空间并施加硬结构约束；2) LLM在受限角色中执行局部判别判断（作为先验策略引导探索和比较状态评估器提供奖励信号）；3) MCTS通过反向传播进行长程搜索与原则性信用分配。

Result: 在两个互补知识图谱上的药物机制阐明任务中，TESSERA忠实于已审编的生物学知识，同时揭示了连贯的替代机制；消融实验证实了两个LLM组件的判别性贡献。

Conclusion: TESSERA提供了一种在结构化知识上进行组合推理的通用范式，超越当前应用场景。

Abstract: Extracting multi-step explanations from knowledge graphs poses a combinatorial challenge requiring both heuristic guidance (as candidates proliferate with depth) and credit assignment (as path quality emerges over extended sequences). Frontier LLMs, strong on knowledge/reasoning benchmarks, offer a compelling source of such heuristics, yet their knowledge comes sans guarantees and compositional performance degrades as chains lengthen. We thus present TESSERA, a 3-part neuro-symbolic framework that uses LLMs in a circumscribed role: for local discriminative judgement rather than autonomous multi-step generation; the knowledge graph then defines the hypothesis space enforcing hard structural constraints, and MCTS coordinates the long-horizon search with principled credit assignment via backpropagation. LLMs perform dual roles as a prior policy biasing exploration and a comparative state evaluator supplying reward signals. Evaluation on drug mechanism elucidation across two complementary knowledge graphs demonstrates fidelity to curated biology while surfacing coherent alternative mechanisms, with ablations confirming discriminative contribution from both LLM components. Beyond its current application, our framework offers a general paradigm for compositional reasoning over structured knowledge.

</details>


### [231] [TIDE-Bench: Task-Aware and Diagnostic Evaluation of Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09544)
*Yize Li,Junzhi Li,Jason Song,Chuxiong Sun,Rui Wang,Changwen Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TIDE-Bench是一个高质量、统一的工具集成推理（TIR）评估基准，涵盖多样化任务、全面评估协议和高判别性数据集，实验揭示了工具接地方面的持续瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TIR评估在数据集质量、任务多样性、诊断全面性和评估效率方面存在局限，缺乏高质量且统一的评估基准。

Method: 提出TIDE-Bench基准，包含：(1)多样化的任务设置，结合数学推理、知识密集型QA及两个新任务（工具接地实验设计任务、动态交互任务）；(2)综合且任务感知的评估协议，同时衡量答案质量、过程可靠性、工具使用效率和推理成本；(3)通过过滤低判别性实例构建高质量、高判别力的评估集以降低评估成本。

Result: 在多种基础模型和TIR方法上的广泛实验揭示了工具接地（tool grounding）方面的持续瓶颈。

Conclusion: TIDE-Bench为TIR研究提供了更全面的评估框架，并指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Tool-integrated reasoning has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing large language models with external computation, retrieval, and execution capabilities. However, the field still lacks a high-quality and unified evaluation benchmark, and existing TIR evaluations remain limited in dataset quality, task diversity, diagnostic comprehensiveness, and evaluation efficiency. In this work, we introduce TIDE-Bench, a holistic and efficient benchmark for evaluating TIR methods, featuring three key advantages. First, it provides diverse task settings, combining widely used mathematical reasoning and knowledge-intensive QA tasks with two newly designed tasks, namely the tool-grounded experimental design task and the dynamic interactive task, to probe models' abilities in complex tool invocation and multi-tool coordination. Second, TIDE-Bench adopts a comprehensive yet task-aware evaluation protocol, jointly measuring final answer quality, process reliability, tool-use efficiency, and inference cost across heterogeneous task settings. Third, TIDE-Bench constructs high-quality and discriminative evaluation sets by filtering low-discrimination instances from existing datasets, substantially reducing evaluation cost while focusing on more challenging samples. Extensive experiments on multiple foundation models and TIR methods reveal persistent bottlenecks in tool grounding, offering insights for future TIR research.

</details>


### [232] [PDEAgent-Bench: A Multi-Metric, Multi-Library Benchmark for PDE Solver Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09636)
*Zhen Hang,Yushan Yashengjiang,Junhui Li,Huanshuo Dong,Yang Wei,Zhezheng Hao,Jiangtao Ma,Songlin Bai,Haozhong Kai,Xihang Yue,Gangzong Si,Dongming Jiang,Chao Yao,Zhanhua Hu,Jiangqing Zhang,Pengwei Liu,Yaomin Shen,Xingyu Ren,Lei Liu,Zikang Xu,Han Li,Qingsong Yao,Hande Dong,Hong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PDEAgent-Bench，据我们所知是首个面向PDE到求解器代码生成的多指标、多库基准测试，包含645个实例、6个数学类别和11个PDE族，采用分阶段评估框架（可执行性→数值精度→计算效率）。实验表明当前LLM和代码智能体虽能生成可运行代码，但在精度和效率约束下通过率大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准主要评估语法正确性或预定义测试用例，缺乏专门针对PDE到求解器代码生成的公开基准，且通用代码基准无法捕捉数值PDE求解的独特挑战（如求解精度、效率、与专业FEM库的兼容性）。

Method: 构建PDEAgent-Bench基准，包含645个实例，覆盖6个数学类别和11个PDE族，支持DOLFINx、Firedrake、deal.II三种常见FEM库。每个实例包含面向智能体的问题规范、评估网格上的参考解、以及特定案例的精度和运行时目标。采用分阶段评估框架，依次检查可执行性、数值精度和计算效率。

Result: 代表性LLM和代码智能体实验表明：模型通常能生成可运行代码，但一旦施加精度和效率要求，通过率显著下降，说明当前智能体在生成数值可靠且高效的PDE求解器方面仍存在局限。

Conclusion: PDEAgent-Bench为PDE到求解器代码生成提供了基于数值PDE求解实际需求的可复现测试平台，揭示了现有LLM和智能体在生成数值可靠且高效的PDE求解器方面的不足。

Abstract: PDE-to-solver code generation aims to automatically synthesize executable numerical solvers from partial differential equation (PDE) specifications. This task requires not only understanding the mathematical structure of PDEs, but also selecting appropriate discretization schemes and solver configurations, and correctly implementing the resulting formulations in finite-element method (FEM) libraries. Existing code generation benchmarks mainly evaluate syntactic correctness, or success on predefined test cases. To our knowledge, there is currently no publicly available benchmark specifically for PDE-to-solver code generation, and general-purpose code benchmarks do not fully capture the unique challenges of numerical PDE solution, such as ensuring solver accuracy, efficiency, and compatibility with professional FEM libraries. We introduce PDEAgent-Bench, to the best of our knowledge, the first multi-metric, multi-library benchmark for PDE-to-solver code generation. PDEAgent-Bench contains 645 instances across 6 mathematical categories and 11 PDE families, with common FEM libraries for DOLFINx, Firedrake, and deal.II. Each instance provides an agent-facing problem specification, a reference solution on a prescribed evaluation grid, and case-specific accuracy and runtime targets. PDEAgent-Bench adopts a staged evaluation framework in which generated solvers must sequentially pass executability, numerical accuracy, and computational efficiency checks. Experiments with representative LLMs and code agents show that models can often produce runnable code, but their pass rate drops substantially once accuracy and efficiency requirements are enforced. These results indicate that current agents remain limited in producing numerically reliable and efficient PDE solvers, and that PDEAgent-Bench provides a reproducible testbed grounded in the practical requirements of numerical PDE solving.

</details>


### [233] [Workspace Optimization: How to Train Your Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09650)
*Elad Sarafian,Gal Kaplun,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"工作空间优化"(workspace optimization)概念，通过演化智能体的外部结构化工作空间(而非模型权重)来提升性能。作者在ARC-AGI-3基准上实现了DreamTeam多智能体框架，将SOTA从36%提升至38.4%，同时减少31%的环境操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型构建的智能体通常无法调整其权重，因此需要探索可训练的其他组件。本文论证智能体的"工作空间"(即其读写和测试的外部结构化基板)是可行的训练对象。

Method: 提出工作空间优化方法，模仿权重空间训练的结构：用工件替代参数、用证据替代数据、用反例替代损失、用文本反馈替代梯度。在ARC-AGI-3上实现DreamTeam多智能体框架，包含执行世界模型、规划、假设、探测、策略和故障路由等角色。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-3当前25个游戏的公开集上，按照官方评分协议，DreamTeam将SOTA协议匹配智能体的分数从36%提升至38.4%，同时每个游戏使用的环境操作减少31%。

Conclusion: 工作空间优化为无法调整权重的智能体提供了一种有效的学习范式，通过演化外部结构化工作空间而非内部参数，能够在多轮交互环境中提升性能并提高效率。

Abstract: Modern agents built on frontier language models often cannot adapt their weights. What, then, remains trainable? We argue it is the agent's \emph{workspace}, the structured external substrate it reads, writes, and tests; we call its evolution workspace optimization. Workspace optimization targets hard multi-turn environments where a frontier model has strong priors but cannot solve the task in a single shot, so the agent must learn through interaction. We propose a principled way to evolve the workspace, mirroring the structure of weight-space training: artifacts in place of parameters, evidence in place of data, counterexamples in place of losses, and textual feedback in place of gradients. We instantiate the idea in DreamTeam, a multi-agent harness for ARC-AGI-3 whose roles build an executable world model, plan, hypothesize, probe, strategize, and route failures. On the current 25-game ARC-AGI-3 public set under the official scoring protocol and averaged over two independent runs, DreamTeam improves the SOTA protocol-matched agent's score from 36% to 38.4%, while using 31% fewer environment actions per game.

</details>


### [234] [Absurd World: A Simple Yet Powerful Method to Absurdify the Real-world for Probing LLM Reasoning Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09678)
*Ryan Albright,Golam Md Muktadir,Zarif Ikram,S M Jubaer,Mehrab Hossain,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Absurd World基准框架，通过改变现实世界的符号、动作、序列和事件来创建逻辑一致的荒谬场景，测试大语言模型在简单逻辑推理中的鲁棒性，发现LLM在面对逻辑相同但背景荒谬的任务时推理能力显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究聚焦于用越来越复杂的问题打破LLM推理，但LLM在简单逻辑推理中的鲁棒性尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出Absurd World框架，将现实世界模型分解为符号、动作、序列和事件，自动改变这些要素创建荒谬世界，保持任务逻辑不变，评估多种模型在不同提示技术下的表现。

Result: Absurd World是评估LLM逻辑推理能力的有效工具，大量模型在荒谬世界中的表现证明其推理能力易受现实世界模式影响。

Conclusion: 该框架可用于广泛测试LLM在现实问题变体下的推理鲁棒性，揭示LLM逻辑推理能力的局限性。

Abstract: While extremely powerful and versatile at various tasks, the thinking capabilities of large language models (LLMs) are often put under scrutiny as they sometimes fail to solve problems that humans can systematically solve. However, recent literature focuses on breaking LLM reasoning with increasingly complex problems, and whether an LLM is robust in simple logical reasoning remains underexplored. This paper proposes Absurd World, a benchmarking framework, to test LLMs against altered realism, where scenarios are logically coherent, and humans can easily solve the tasks. Absurd World breaks a real-world model into symbols, actions, sequences, and events, which are automatically altered to create absurd worlds where the logic to solve the tasks remains the same. It evaluates a large collection of models with simple and advanced prompting techniques, and proves that it is an effective tool to determine LLMs' ability to think logically, ignoring the patterns learned from the real world. One can use this framework to extensively test an LLM against a real-world problem to verify whether the LLM's reasoning capability is robust against variations of the task.

</details>


### [235] [Unpredictability dissociates from structured control in language agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09692)
*Jia Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模消融实验（74,352次调用、26,946代生成、57,816条评分记录）证明，在语言智能体中，随机采样无法替代由理由、记忆、自我状态和抑制机制构成的耦合控制结构。高随机性变体在所有数据集上比结构控制变体更不可预测，而定向消融（理由、否决）破坏了预期控制轮廓。后续跨20个任务族、三种智能体支架的复现进一步确认，无场、乱序上下文和分布匹配的控制条件均无法恢复结构化动作耦合控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将不可预测行为视为智能体存在控制的证据，但随机散布与结构化动作控制本质上并不等价。作者旨在检验随机采样能否替代由理由、记忆、自我状态和抑制机制组成的结构化控制机制，从而厘清随机性与真正控制之间的混淆。

Method: 采用语言智能体实现，其控制组件（理由、记忆、自我状态、抑制）可选择性禁用。构建七数据集基线消融矩阵（74,352次调用），比较高随机性变体与结构控制变体；进行匹配接口控制（26,946代生成）和主要行为测试（57,816条评分记录，移除自由格式痕迹）；扩展至Qwen2.5 7B/14B/32B和Mistral-7B系列，跨20个任务族和三种智能体支架；实施三项注释者盲审（1,200重叠项）；尝试严格熵匹配、严格token/计算匹配和反事实翻转压力测试。

Result: 高随机性变体在7/7数据集上比结构控制变体更不可预测；理由和否决消融在7/7数据集上降低了预期结构控制轮廓。结构智能体在所有数据集上保持比随机、事后、乱序和冗长控制更强的动作-场耦合。结构控制变体在所有预定义行为组件上超过高随机性变体和消融条件。无场、乱序上下文和分布匹配控制均无法恢复结构化动作控制。三项盲审保持高度一致。严格熵匹配和严格token/计算匹配及反事实翻转测试未通过门限（作为局限性处理）。

Conclusion: 在该实现的智能体家族中，随机不可预测性无法再现结构化、动作耦合的控制。随机采样不能替代由理由、记忆、自我状态和抑制机制构成的耦合控制结构。

Abstract: Unpredictable behavior is often taken as evidence of control, yet stochastic dispersion and structured action control need not coincide. This paper tests whether stochastic sampling can substitute for structured mechanisms that couple reasons, memory, self-state and inhibition to action selection in a language-agent implementation whose control components can be selectively disabled. In a seven-dataset baseline lesion matrix comprising 74,352 calls, the high-stochasticity comparator was more unpredictable than the structured-control variant in 7/7 datasets, whereas targeted reason and veto lesions reduced the expected structured-control profiles in 7/7 datasets each. In a matched-interface control spanning 26,946 generations, the structured agent maintained stronger action-field coupling than all stochastic, post-hoc, scrambled and verbosity controls across every dataset. The primary behavioral test removed free-form trace wording from the evaluation: 57,816 scored records showed the structured-control variant exceeding the high-stochasticity comparator or the reason/veto lesions in 7/7 datasets for all predefined behavioral components. Later open-weight runs extended the no-context controls to Qwen2.5 7B, 14B and 32B and to an independent Mistral-7B family across 20 task families and three agent scaffolds; no-fields, scrambled-context and distribution-matched controls failed to recover structured action control. A three-annotator blinded audit over 1,200 overlap items preserved high agreement. Strict entropy matching, strict token/compute matching and a formal counterfactual-flip stress test did not meet their gates and are treated as limitations. Stochastic unpredictability did not reproduce structured, action-coupled control in this implemented agent family.

</details>


### [236] [Ambig-DS: A Benchmark for Task-Framing Ambiguity in Data-Science Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09698)
*Josefa Lia Stoisser,Marc Boubnovski Martell,Sidsel Boldsen,Kaspar Märtens,Robert Kitchen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ambig-DS基准测试，包含目标模糊（51个任务）和评估指标模糊（61个任务）两个诊断套件，用于检测数据科学智能体在任务框架模糊时的静默错误（silent misframing）。实验发现：（1）智能体失败模式是静默提交错误框架而非执行错误；（2）允许提一个澄清问题可恢复大部分性能损失；（3）但智能体无法可靠判断何时该提问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只评估数据科学智能体能否成功执行流水线，忽略了当任务规定不明确时，智能体会静默地选择一种看似合理但错误的任务框架（silent misframing），产生干净可执行的代码却掩盖了根本性的理解错误。

Method: 基于DSBench和MLE-bench构建两个诊断套件：每个任务配对一个完全指定的原始版本和一个通过受控编辑产生的模糊变体（多种合理诠释且影响决策后果），经人工+LLM验证流程确认；分别用原始评估器打分，测试5种不同能力级别的智能体。

Result: （1）模糊变体下性能显著下降，失败模式为静默提交错误框架而非执行错误；（2）允许提一个澄清问题可恢复大部分损失；（3）智能体无法可靠判断何时提问：宽松提示导致过度提问，保守提示导致静默默认。

Conclusion: 识别目标与评估指标的模糊性（而非流水线执行能力）是当前数据科学智能体评估中缺失的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: As data-science agents shift from co-pilots to auto-pilots, silent misframing becomes a critical failure mode. Agents quietly commit to plausible but unintended task framings, producing clean, executable artifacts that hide their incorrect assessment of the task. Existing benchmarks score whether the pipeline runs, ignoring whether the agent recognized the task was underspecified. We introduce Ambig-DS, two diagnostic suites: one for prediction-target ambiguity (Ambig-DS-Target, 51 tasks built on DSBench, a tabular modeling benchmark) and one for evaluation-objective ambiguity (Ambig-DS-Objective, 61 tasks built on MLE-bench, a Kaggle-style ML competition benchmark), constructed so that scoring uses each source benchmark's original evaluator. For every task we pair the original, fully specified version with an ambiguous variant produced by controlled edits; a human-and-LLM verification pipeline confirms each variant admits multiple plausible interpretations with decision-relevant consequences. The suites are analyzed independently and ambiguity lowers performance in both. Across five agents spanning efficient to frontier-class models, we find in our controlled diagnostic setting: (i) failures are silent commitments: wrong-target submissions on Target, wrong-metric or non-committal baseline submissions on Objective, rather than execution errors; (ii) allowing the agent to ask one clarifying question recovers much of the loss under idealized conditions, suggesting missing framing information drives a substantial part of the observed degradation; but (iii) agents cannot reliably tell when to use it: permissive prompts induce over-asking on clear tasks, while conservative prompts induce silent defaulting on ambiguous ones. Recognizing target and objective underspecification, not pipeline execution, is the bottleneck missing from standard DS-agent evaluations.

</details>


### [237] [Medical Model Synthesis Architectures: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09716)
*Katherine M. Collins,Marlene Berke,Ilia Sucholutsky,Ayman Ali,Adrian Weller,Timothy J. O'Donnell,Tyler Brooke-Wilson,Lionel Wong,Joshua B. Tenenbaum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedMSA框架，结合语言模型检索知识与概率建模，实现临床不确定情境下可校准、可验证的透明推理，初步应用于鉴别诊断并生成带不确定性权重的诊断列表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策充满不确定性，现有AI系统在不确定条件下缺乏校准推理能力且推理过程不透明，亟需一种兼具实用性与形式透明性的预测框架。

Method: 提出MedMSA框架，利用语言模型检索相关先验知识，构建形式化概率模型以支持不确定条件下的校准推理与可验证推断。

Result: 初步概念验证表明，该框架可用于鉴别诊断，生成带有不确定性权重的潜在诊断列表。

Conclusion: MedMSA为临床安全协作提供了更通用的框架方向，未来可进一步拓展应用场景。

Abstract: Medicine is rife with high-stakes uncertainty. Doctors routinely make clinical judgments and decisions that juggle many fundamental unknowns, like predictions about what might be causing a patients' symptoms or decisions about what treatment to try next. Despite increasing interest in developing AI systems that aid or even replace doctors in clinical settings, current systems struggle with calibrated reasoning under uncertainty, and are often deeply opaque about their reasoning. We propose a framework for AI systems that can make practically useful but formally transparent clinical predictions under uncertainty. Given a clinical situation, our framework (MedMSA) uses language models to retrieve relevant prior knowledge, but constructs a formal probabilistic model to support calibrated and verifiable inferences under uncertainty. We show how an initial proof-of-concept of this framework can be used for differential diagnosis, producing an uncertainty-weighted list of potential diagnoses that could explain a patients' symptoms, and discuss future applications and directions for applying this framework more generally for safe clinical collaborations.

</details>


### [238] [Primal-Dual Guided Decoding for Constrained Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09749)
*Federico Tomasi,Dmitrii Moor,Alice Wang,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种 primal-dual guided decoding 方法，用于在离散扩散模型的推理阶段通过自适应拉格朗日乘子将全局属性约束融入生成过程，无需重训练且支持多约束，在文本生成、分子设计和音乐播放列表生成任务中提升了约束满足度并保持了领域质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在生成结构化序列时能够逐步揭掩 token，但在生成过程中强制执行全局属性约束仍然是一个未解决的挑战。

Method: 将约束生成建模为 KL 正则化的优化问题，通过在线自适应拉格朗日乘子求解。在每个去噪步骤中，通过可加的、依赖约束的偏置修改 token logits，乘子通过镜像下降根据约束违反程度更新。该偏置是约束的最优 KL 正则化投影，使约束分布尽可能接近模型的无约束分布同时满足约束。

Result: 在主题文本生成、分子设计和音乐播放列表生成三个任务上进行了评估，表明单一算法通过领域特定评分函数实例化后，能够提高约束满足度，同时保持相关的领域特定质量指标。

Conclusion: 该方法无需重训练，无需额外的模型评估（除标准采样外），支持多个同时约束，并提供约束违反的形式化界限。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models generate structured sequences by progressively unmasking tokens, but enforcing global property constraints during generation remains an open challenge. We propose primal-dual guided decoding, an inference-time method that formulates constrained generation as a KL-regularised optimisation problem and solves it online via adaptive Lagrangian multipliers. At each denoising step, the method modifies token logits through an additive, constraint-dependent bias, with multipliers updated by mirror descent based on constraint violation. The bias arises as the optimal KL-regularised projection of the constraint, so the constrained distribution remains as close as possible to the model's unconstrained distribution while still satisfying the constraint. The method requires no retraining and no additional model evaluations beyond standard sampling, supports multiple simultaneous constraints, and provides formal bounds on constraint violation. We evaluate our approach on topical text generation, molecular design, and music playlist generation, showing that a single algorithm instantiated via domain-specific scoring functions improves constraint satisfaction while preserving relevant domain-specific quality metrics.

</details>


### [239] [Attribution-based Explanations for Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09780)
*Paul Kobialka,Andrea Pferscher,Francesco Leofante,Erika Ábrahám,Silvia Lizeth Tapia Tarifa,Einar Broch Johnsen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于马尔可夫决策过程(MDPs)的基于归因的解释方法，通过对状态和执行路径分配重要性分数来解释序贯决策模型，并利用策略综合技术高效计算这些分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归因技术主要针对静态输入特征，无法推广到序贯决策场景。本文旨在填补这一空白，为MDPs提供形式化的归因解释方法。

Method: 对MDPs中归因应表示的含义进行形式化表征，聚焦于对单个状态和执行路径分配重要性分数；利用策略综合技术高效计算重要性分数，以处理MDP的非确定性。

Result: 在五个案例研究上评估了该方法，证明了其在为序贯决策智能体提供可解释洞察方面的实用性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将归因技术从静态特征扩展到了序贯决策设置，为理解MDPs中智能体的决策逻辑提供了可解释的工具。

Abstract: Attribution techniques explain the outcome of an AI model by assigning a numerical score to its inputs. So far, these techniques have mainly focused on attributing importance to static input features at a single point in time, and thus fail to generalize to sequential decision-making settings. This paper fills this gap by introducing techniques to generate attribution-based explanations for Markov Decision Processes (MDPs). We give a formal characterization of what attributions should represent in MDPs, focusing on explanations that assign importance scores to both individual states and execution paths. We show how importance scores can be computed by leveraging techniques for strategy synthesis, enabling the efficient computation of these scores despite the non-determinism inherent in an MDP. We evaluate our approach on five case-studies, demonstrating its utility in providing interpretable insights into the logic of sequential decision-making agents.

</details>


### [240] [EnactToM: An Evolving Benchmark for Functional Theory of Mind in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09826)
*Gurusha Juneja,Dylan Lu,Saaket Agashe,Parth Diwane,Edward Gunn,Jayanth Srinivasa,Gaowen Liu,William Yang Wang,Yali Du,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有AI的ToM评测多聚焦于字面信念问答（字面ToM），而忽略了在具身多智能体环境中基于隐式信念做出最优行动的能力（功能性ToM）。本文提出EnactToM——一个包含300个3D家庭多智能体任务的演化基准，所有任务经形式化验证确保可解性和所需认知深度。在最难子集上，7个前沿模型的功能性任务完成率均为0.0%，而字面信念探测平均为45.0%；93%的失败源于认知协调崩溃（隐瞒信息、忽略同伴约束、消息错配）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ToM基准大多只测试字面ToM（直接回答信念问题），未能评估AI在具身多智能体环境中基于隐式信念执行最优行动的功能性ToM能力。

Method: 构建EnactToM基准，包含300个3D家庭多智能体任务，具备部分可观测性、私有信息和受限通信；每个任务经形式化验证确保可解性和所需认知深度；任务可演化生成以随模型能力提升而增加难度。

Result: 在困难子集上，7个前沿模型的功能性任务完成率均为0.0%，而字面信念探测平均为45.0%；93%的失败可归因于认知协调崩溃，包括隐瞒信息、忽略同伴约束和消息错配。

Conclusion: 当前AI模型在功能性ToM方面存在严重缺陷，需要从认知协调角度改进多智能体协作能力。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to track others epistemic state, makes humans efficient collaborators. AI agents need the same capacity in multi agent settings, yet existing benchmarks mostly test literal ToM by asking direct belief questions. The ability act optimally on implicit beliefs in embodied environments, called functional ToM, remains largely untested. We introduce EnactToM, an evolving benchmark of 300 embodied multi-agent tasks set in a 3D household with partial observability, private information, and constrained communication. Each task is formally verified for solvability and required epistemic depth, and new tasks are generated increase difficulty as models improve. On the hard split, all seven evaluated frontier models score 0.0% Pass^3 on functional task completion, while averaging 45.0% on literal belief probes. Manual analysis traces 93% of sampled failures to epistemic coordination breakdowns such as withheld information, ignored partner constraints, and misallocated messages, providing a concrete target for future work.

</details>


### [241] [Yield Curve Forecasting using Machine Learning and Econometrics: A Comparative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09842)
*Aman Singh,Tokunbo Ogunfunmi,Sanjiv Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对比了计量经济学/时间序列分析、经典机器学习和深度学习方法在美国国债收益率曲线预测上的表现，使用47年日度数据。结果表明ARIMA和朴素计量经济模型整体表现最优，而TimeGPT、LGBM和RNN是机器学习方法中表现最好的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在NLP和CV领域取得成功，但在时间序列预测（尤其是金融领域）的影响仍存在争议。国债收益率曲线对债券市场至关重要，而现有研究缺乏对多种方法（特别是深度学习算法）在收益率曲线预测上的系统比较。

Method: 使用47年美国国债收益率曲线日度数据，对比了ARIMA及其扩展、朴素基准、集成方法、RNN、多种Transformer架构（包括TimeGPT）等方法的预测性能，并探究了平稳与非平稳数据对深度学习模型输入的影响。

Result: ARIMA和朴素计量经济模型整体表现优于其他模型（仅一个时间段例外）。机器学习方法中TimeGPT、LGBM和RNN表现最好。

Conclusion: 在国债收益率曲线预测任务上，传统计量经济学方法仍具竞争力；深度学习方法虽表现不俗但未显著超越简单基线。

Abstract: While machine learning has revolutionized many fields such as natural language processing (NLP) and computer vision, its impact on time-series forecasting is still widely disputed, especially in the finance domain. This paper compares forecasting performance on U.S. Treasury yield curve data across econometrics/time-series analysis, classical machine learning, and deep learning methods, using daily data over 47 years. The Treasury yield curve is important because it is widely used by every participant in the bond markets, which are larger than equity markets. We examine a variety of methods that have not been tested on yield curve forecasting, especially deep learning algorithms. The algorithms include the Autoregressive Integrated Moving Average (ARIMA) model and its extensions, naive benchmarks, ensemble methods, Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs), and multiple transformers built for forecasting. ARIMA and naive econometric models outperform other models overall, except in one time block. Of the machine learning methods, TimeGPT, LGBM and RNNs perform the best. Furthermore, the paper explores whether stationary or nonstationary data are more appropriate as input to deep learning models.

</details>


### [242] [The Metacognitive Probe: Five Behavioural Calibration Diagnostics for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09844)
*Rafael C. T. Oliveira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Metacognitive Probe，一个包含五项任务、15个插槽的诊断工具，用于分解LLM的信心行为为五个行为维度。在8个前沿模型和69名人类上评估，发现模型间存在显著的行为分离现象，如Gemini 2.5 Flash在任务内校准最佳（88分）但在跨任务难度预测上最差（41分）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有复合基准（如MMLU、BIG-Bench等）仅评估模型是否能产生正确回答，但无法判断模型是否知道自己的回答何时错误。模型可能在整体校准基准上得分很高，却在某些狭窄领域过度自信，而这些缺陷被聚合指标掩盖了。因此需要一种能够揭示这些隐藏问题的诊断工具。

Method: 设计Metacognitive Probe，包含五个任务维度：信心校准（T1-CC）、认知警觉（T2-EV）、知识边界（T3-KB）、校准范围（T4-CR）和推理链验证（T5-RCV），共15个插槽。理论基础源于Flavell (1979)和Nelson & Narens (1990)的元认知理论，但仅关注可观测的信心-正确性对齐。在8个前沿LLM和69名人类上评估。

Result: 发现Gemini 2.5 Flash存在显著的47分内部分离：任务内校准表现最佳（T1-CC=88，Spearman rho=+0.551，p=0.005），但跨任务难度预测表现最差（T4-CR=41，sigma_conf=1.4）。预设定的人类发展假说被证伪。

Conclusion: Metacognitive Probe能够揭示复合基准无法暴露的模型信心行为分离现象，为评估LLM的元认知能力提供了更细粒度的诊断工具。该工具并非经过验证的跨物种元认知量表，其人类发展假说未得到支持。

Abstract: The Metacognitive Probe is an exploratory five-task, 15-slot diagnostic that decomposes an LLM's confidence behaviour into five behaviourally-distinct dimensions: confidence calibration (T1-CC), epistemic vigilance (T2-EV), knowledge boundary (T3-KB), calibration range (T4-CR), and reasoning-chain validation (T5-RCV). It is evaluated on N=8 frontier models and N=69 humans. The instrument is motivated by Flavell (1979) and Nelson and Narens (1990) but operates on observable confidence-correctness alignment; it is not a validated cross-species metacognition scale, and the pre-specified human developmental hypothesis was falsified.
  Composite benchmarks (MMLU, BIG-Bench, HELM, GPQA) ask whether a model produces a correct response. They are silent on whether the model knows when its response is wrong. A model can score 80 on a composite calibration benchmark and still be wildly overconfident in narrow pockets the aggregate cannot surface. The Metacognitive Probe surfaces those pockets.
  Our headline is a 47-point within-model dissociation in Gemini 2.5 Flash: panel-best within-task calibration (T1-CC = 88; Spearman rho = +0.551, 95% CI [+0.14, +0.80], p = 0.005) and panel-worst cross-task difficulty prediction (T4-CR = 41; sigma_conf = 1.4 across twelve factoids).

</details>


### [243] [Fairness of Explanations in Artificial Intelligence (AI): A Unifying Framework, Axioms, and Future Direction toward Responsible AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09852)
*Gideon Popoola,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文识别了算法公平性与可解释AI交叉领域中的“程序性偏差”盲点：一个模型可能输出符合公平标准的结果，但其推理过程却存在深度偏见。作者提出了解释公平性的条件不变性框架，构建了七维分类法，识别了三种解释不平等生成机制，并给出了六步评估工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将算法公平性与可解释AI视为两个独立领域，忽略了模型推理过程（而非仅输出结果）可能存在的不公平问题。作者旨在填补这一盲点，将解释的公平性确立为一个独立的研究对象。

Method: 采用统一的文献综述与理论分析方法，提出条件不变性框架形式化解释公平性，构建七维分类法，识别三种生成机制（表征驱动、解释模型失配、可行动性驱动），并设计六步评估工作流。

Result: 首次为该新兴交叉领域提供了统一的理论与文献综述；揭示了事后解释器在认证解释公平性方面的缺陷；提出的条件不变性框架能够统一现有所有解释公平性度量指标。

Conclusion: 解释公平性应被视为独立于结果公平性的研究目标，需要专门的理论框架与评估流程来检测和缓解程序性偏差。

Abstract: Machine learning algorithms are being used in high-stakes decisions, including those in criminal justice, healthcare, credit, and employment. The research community has responded with two largely independent research fields: \emph{algorithmic fairness}, which targets equitable outcomes, and \emph{explainable AI} (XAI), which targets interpretable reasoning. This survey identifies and maps a novel blind spot at their intersection, which is a model that can satisfy every standard fairness criterion in its outputs while being profoundly unfair in its \emph{reasoning process}. We refer to this as the procedural bias, and mitigating it requires treating the fairness of explanations as a distinct object of scientific study.
  To our knowledge, we provide the first unified theoretical and literature review of this emerging field and elucidate the drawbacks of post-hoc explainers in certifying explanation fairness. Our central contribution is a \emph{conditional invariance framework} formalizing explanation fairness as the requirement that explanations should be indifferent regardless of the protected attributes $ P(E(X) \in \cdot \mid X_\text{rel} = x_\text{rel},\, A = a) = P(E(X) \in \cdot \mid X_\text{rel} = x_\text{rel},\, A = b)$ for all task-relevant $x$, a single principle from which all existing explanation fairness metrics emerge as partial operationalizations. We introduce a seven-dimensional taxonomy, identify three generative mechanisms of explanation inequity (representation-driven, explanation-model mismatch, actionability-driven), and propose a canonical six-step evaluation workflow for operationalizing explanation fairness audits in practice.

</details>


### [244] [Cross-Family Universality of Behavioral Axes via Anchor-Projected Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09875)
*Su-Hyeon Kim,Yo-Sub Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出锚点投影框架（ACS），将不同大语言模型的隐藏表示映射到共享坐标空间，实现行为方向跨模型家族的迁移，无需微调。在五个指令微调模型家族和十个行为轴上的实验表明，Llama-Qwen-Mistral-Phi集群内方向高度对齐，跨模型检测准确率达0.83，平均AUROC达0.95。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同模型家族使用不同的隐藏维度、分词器和训练流程，导致行为方向难以跨模型比较或迁移，亟需一种统一的跨家族可解释性框架。

Method: 提出锚点投影框架（ACS）：将每个模型的隐藏表示映射到共享锚点坐标空间；从源模型提取行为方向并投影到ACS中平均为规范方向；对于新模型，仅使用锚点激活将规范方向重建到其原生隐藏空间，无需微调或目标特定方向提取。

Result: 在五个指令微调模型家族和十个行为轴上实验：LQMP集群内方向高度对齐；跨模型十路检测准确率0.83，平均二元AUROC 0.95；规范引导使拒绝率偏移最高+0.46%；两个源模型和小锚点池足以近似可迁移方向。

Conclusion: ACS提供了跨家族可解释性的新视角，证明表示级迁移在模型家族间保持鲁棒。

Abstract: Large language models from different families use different hidden dimensions, tokenizers, and training procedures, making behavioral directions difficult to compare or transfer across models. We introduce an anchor-projection framework that maps hidden representations from each model into a shared anchor coordinate space (ACS). Behavioral directions extracted from source models are projected into ACS and averaged into a canonical direction. For a new model, the canonical direction is reconstructed into its native hidden space using only anchor activations, without fine-tuning or target-specific direction extraction. We evaluate five instruction-tuned model families and ten behavioral axes. We find that same-axis directions align tightly across the Llama-Qwen-Mistral-Phi (LQMP) cluster in ACS. This shared structure transfers to downstream tasks. For the aligned LQMP cluster, held-out targets achieve (0.83) ten-way detection accuracy and (0.95) mean binary AUROC, while canonical steering induces refusal-rate shifts of up to +0.46% under distribution shift. Sensitivity analyses show that two source models and small anchor pools already suffice to approximate transferable directions. Overall, ACS provides a novel perspective on cross-family interpretability, revealing that representation-level transfer remains robust across model families.

</details>


### [245] [M2A: Synergizing Mathematical and Agentic Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09879)
*Junjian Wang,Xin Zhou,Qiran Xu,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出M2A方法，通过模型合并（model merging）将数学推理能力注入智能体推理模型中，具体在参数空间中识别智能体行为关键子空间，并沿其零空间合并数学推理任务向量，从而在不重新训练的情况下提升智能体推理深度。在SWE-Bench Verified上，将Qwen3-8B的解决率从44.0%提升至51.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学推理（依赖内在逻辑、封闭世界、单轮回答）与智能体推理（需要多轮交互、思考与行动交替）之间存在对齐偏差，导致多任务学习下推理行为不稳定且性能提升有限。

Method: M2A直接在参数空间操作：识别对智能体行为关键的特征子空间，将数学推理任务向量仅沿该子空间的零空间进行合并，从而在不扰动智能体行为的前提下注入推理能力。无需额外梯度更新，合并系数可作为控制推理长度的简单旋钮。

Result: 在真实世界编码智能体任务SWE-Bench Verified上，将Qwen3-8B的解决率从44.0%提升至51.2%，无需重新训练模型。

Conclusion: M2A通过模型合并有效解决了数学推理与智能体推理的对齐问题，在不重新训练的情况下显著提升了智能体推理性能，为多任务推理协同提供了新范式。

Abstract: While reasoning has become a central capability of large language models (LLMs), the reasoning patterns required for different scenarios are often misaligned. Mathematical reasoning typically relies on intrinsic logic to solve closed-world problems in a single response, whereas agentic reasoning requires not only internal reasoning but also multi-turn interaction with external environments, interleaving thought and action. This misalignment prevents mathematical and agentic reasoning from effectively benefiting from each other, often yielding unstable reasoning behavior and only limited performance gains under multi-task learning. In this paper, we propose M2A, a novel paradigm that synergizes mathematical and agentic reasoning via model merging. To avoid overfitting to superficial reasoning patterns under joint training, M2A operates directly in parameter space: it identifies the feature subspace critical for agent behavior, and merges the mathematical reasoning task vector only along its null space, thereby injecting reasoning capability along directions that do not perturb agent behavior. Unlike SFT or RL, M2A requires no additional gradient-update and exposes the merging coefficient as a simple knob for controlling reasoning length. Experiments in a challenging real-world coding agent setting show that our method effectively extends agentic reasoning depth and delivers substantial performance improvements. Applied to a fine-tuned Qwen3-8B, M2A improves its SWE-Bench Verified resolved rate from 44.0% to 51.2% without retraining the model. Code is available at https://github.com/laplucky/M2A.git.

</details>


### [246] [The Gordian Knot for VLMs: Diagrammatic Knot Reasoning as a Hard Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09900)
*Hao Liu,Jicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnotBench是一个包含858,318张图像、涵盖1,951种素结原型（交叉数3-19）的基准测试，用于评估视觉语言模型在结理论中的感知-操作能力。实验表明，Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5在14项任务中的表现普遍较差，过半任务接近随机基线，且所有模型都无法正确完成图表到符号的转录，揭示了当前视觉语言模型虽能感知图表特征但缺乏模拟操作能力的根本缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型能够"看"到结图表并报告所见内容，但无法基于该结构执行操作。作者希望系统性地量化这一"感知-操作鸿沟"，通过结理论这一具有明确符号验证标准的数学领域作为探针，揭示视觉语言模型在理解与操作之间的能力断层。

Method: 构建KnotBench基准，包含858,318张图像（来自1,951种素结原型，交叉数3-19），设计14项任务分为四类家族（等价判断、移动预测、识别、跨模态对齐），采用图像与符号的划分来定位失败原因，使用Regina规范结签名进行答案校验。评估Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5（各带/不带思考模式），在64K输出token预算下进行。

Result: 56个(任务, 模型)组合中，15个等于或低于随机基线；14项任务中有8项最佳得分低于随机基线的1.5倍。在图表到符号转录任务中，没有模型能生成严格正确的字符串，宽松的Regina解码也只能在0-4/100项中恢复正确结。思考模式将Claude的整体准确率提升1.65分，GPT-5提升9.25分，但差距缩小有限。

Conclusion: 四个任务家族的综合结果表明，当前视觉语言模型能够提取结图表的特征（感知），但缺乏对这些特征进行移动模拟的操作机制（操作），揭示了感知与操作之间的根本性能力鸿沟。

Abstract: A vision-language model can look at a knot diagram and report what it sees, yet fail to act on that structure. KnotBench pairs an 858,318-image corpus from 1,951 prime-knot prototypes (crossing numbers 3 to 19) with a protocol whose answers are checked against Regina's canonical knot signature. Its 14 tasks span four families, equivalence judgment, move prediction, identification, and cross-modal grounding; an image-versus-symbol split locates failures along the perception-operation gap. We score Claude Opus 4.7 and GPT-5, each with and without thinking, under a 64K output-token budget matched on both vendors. Across 56 (task, model) cases, 15 sit at or below a random baseline and 8 of 14 tasks have a best score under 1.5x random. On diagram-to-symbol transcription, no model produces a strictly correct string, and permissive Regina decoding recovers the knot in 0 to 4 of 100 items. Thinking-mode reasoning lifts overall accuracy by 1.65 points for Claude and 9.25 points for GPT-5, narrowing the gap only modestly. Read together, the four families suggest current vision-language models hold features of a diagram but lack apparatus to simulate moves on those features.

</details>


### [247] [Separate First, Fuse Later: Mitigating Cross-Modal Interference in Audio-Visual LLMs Reasoning with Modality-Specific Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09906)
*Xuanchen Li,Yuheng Lu,Chenrui Cui,Tianrui Wang,Zikang Huang,Yu Jiang,Long Zhou,Longbiao Wang,Jianwu Dang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SFFL框架，通过先分离后融合的音视频推理策略，减少跨模态干扰和幻觉。方法包括模态特定思维链推理、基于强化学习的模态偏好学习，以及模态隔离的推理机制。在通用AVQA基准上平均提升5.16%，在跨模态幻觉基准上提升11.17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前音视频大语言模型存在跨模态干扰问题：一种模态的信息误导另一种模态的解释，导致幻觉。根本原因是中间推理阶段的跨模态交互不受控制。

Method: 1) 提出"先分离后融合"(SFFL)框架；2) 强制进行模态特定的思维链推理，生成独立的音频和视觉推理轨迹；3) 构建模态偏好标签，作为强化学习的辅助奖励，鼓励实例依赖的模态线索利用；4) 引入模态特定推理机制，在分离推理阶段保持模态隔离，在证据融合阶段允许跨模态访问。

Result: 在通用AVQA基准上平均相对提升5.16%，在跨模态幻觉基准上提升11.17%，同时提升了准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 先分离后融合的推理策略能有效减少跨模态干扰，提升音视频问答的性能和抗幻觉能力。

Abstract: Audio and vision provide complementary evidence for audio-visual question answering, yet current audio-visual large language models may suffer from cross-modal interference: information from one modality misguides the interpretation of another, thereby inducing hallucinations. We attribute this issue to uncontrolled cross-modal interactions during intermediate reasoning. To mitigate this, we propose Separate First, Fuse Later (SFFL), an audio-visual reasoning framework designed to reduce cross-modal interference. SFFL enforces modality-specific chain-of-thought reasoning, producing separate audio and visual reasoning traces and integrating evidence for answering. We construct modality-preference labels via a data pipeline under different modality input settings. We use these labels as an auxiliary reward in reinforcement learning to encourage a instance-dependent preference for modality cues when answering. We further introduce a modality-specific reasoning mechanism that preserves modality isolation during the separated reasoning stage while enabling full access to cross-modal information at the evidence fusion stage. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in both accuracy and robustness, yielding an average relative gain of 5.16\% on general AVQA benchmarks and 11.17\% on a cross-modal hallucination benchmark.

</details>


### [248] [RADAR: Redundancy-Aware Diffusion for Multi-Agent Communication Structure Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09907)
*Zhen Zhang,Wanjing Zhou,Juncheng Li,Hao Fei,Jun Wen,Wei Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RADAR是一个面向大语言模型多智能体系统的冗余感知、查询自适应拓扑生成框架，通过条件离散图扩散模型逐步生成通信拓扑，在六个基准测试中实现更高精度、更低token消耗和更强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统的通信拓扑通常是固定或单步生成的，这限制了细粒度结构探索和灵活组合，导致简单任务上过度消耗token，复杂任务上能力受限。

Method: 提出RADAR框架，利用条件离散图扩散模型，以图的有效大小为指导，将通信拓扑设计建模为逐步生成过程，实现冗余感知和查询自适应的通信降噪。

Result: 在六个基准测试上，RADAR相比近期基线方法一致性地取得了更高准确率、更低token消耗和更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统的通信拓扑可以通过逐步生成的方式进行优化，在保持性能的同时显著降低通信开销。

Abstract: Compared with individual agents, large language model based multi-agent systems have shown great capabilities consistently across diverse tasks, including code generation, mathematical reasoning, and planning, etc. Despite their impressive performance, the effectiveness and robustness of these systems heavily rely on their communication topology, which is often fixed or generated in a single step. This restricts fine-grained structural exploration and flexible composition, resulting in excessive token utilization on simple tasks while limiting capability on complicated tasks. To mitigate this challenge, we introduce RADAR, a redundancy-aware and query-adaptive generative framework that actively reduce communication overhead. Motivated by recent progress in conditional discrete graph diffusion models, we formulate communication topology design as a step-by-step generation process, guided by the effective size of the graph. Comprehensive experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate that RADAR consistently outperforms recent baselines, achieving higher accuracy, lower token consumption, and greater robustness across diverse scenarios. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/cszhangzhen/RADAR.

</details>


### [249] [expo: Exploration-prioritized policy optimization via adaptive kl regulation and gaussian curriculum sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09923)
*Mingxiong Lin,Zhangquan Gong,Maowen Tang,Qian Li,Chuangchuang Wang,Jian Ma,Sutian Huang,Kai Tang,Haonan Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EXPO，通过两个轻量级模块（AKL动态KL正则化和GCS高斯课程采样）改进GRPO在LLM数学推理中的效率，在多个基准上显著提升性能，尤其在pass@32指标上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO存在两个未被充分研究的低效问题：固定的KL惩罚系数过度限制了模型需要显著偏离参考策略时的探索，以及训练问题的均匀采样忽略了中等难度问题能提供最具信息量的梯度信号。

Method: 提出EXPO，包含两个轻量级插件模块：1) Accuracy-Conditioned KL Scaling (AKL)，通过批平均精度的光滑非线性函数动态调整KL正则化强度；2) Gaussian Curriculum Sampling (GCS)，以0.5附近中等精度为中心的高斯分布为问题分配采样权重。

Result: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B和Qwen3-8B-Base上，EXPO稳定超越GRPO。AIME 2025 pass@32从63.33%提升至76.67%（绝对增益13.34），8B模型平均pass@32提升2.66。pass@32的增益远大于pass@1。

Conclusion: EXPO能有效扩大模型在固定推理成本预算下的探索边界，提升数学推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become the standard paradigm for LLM mathematical reasoning, where Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) serves as the mainstream algorithm. We point out two understudied inefficiencies existing in GRPO. First, the fixed KL penalty coefficient overly restricts policy exploration at stages where the model requires significant deviation from the reference policy. Second, uniform sampling of training questions ignores that moderately difficult problems provide the most informative gradient signals for optimization. We propose Exploration-Prioritized Policy Optimization (EXPO) with two lightweight plug-in modules. The Accuracy-Conditioned KL Scaling (AKL) dynamically adjusts KL regularization strength through a smooth nonlinear function of batch average accuracy, relaxing the penalty when the model underperforms and strengthening it when the model achieves good results. The Gaussian Curriculum Sampling (GCS) assigns sampling weights to questions following a Gaussian distribution centered at moderate accuracy around 0.5, focusing training on the model's learning frontier. We conduct extensive experiments on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B and Qwen3-8B-Base over six mathematical reasoning benchmarks. The results show EXPO steadily surpasses vanilla GRPO. It obtains an absolute gain of 13.34 on AIME 2025 pass@32, rising from 63.33 percent to 76.67 percent, and achieves an average pass@32 improvement of 2.66 on the 8B model. The much larger performance gains on pass@32 compared with pass@1 demonstrate that EXPO effectively enlarges the model's exploration boundary under a fixed inference cost budget.

</details>


### [250] [LoopVLA: Learning Sufficiency in Recurrent Refinement for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09948)
*Boyang Shen,Kaixiang Yang,Hao Wang,Qiuyu Yu,Qiang Xie,Qiang Li,Zhiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoopVLA提出了一种循环视觉-语言-动作架构，通过共享Transformer模块迭代细化表示、预测动作并估计充分性，在减少45%参数和提升1.7倍推理吞吐量的同时，匹配或超越强基线的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将视觉-语言骨干的最深层表示视为普遍最优的，但机器人操作包含大量闭环空间调整，过度抽象会浪费计算并削弱低级几何线索。现有的早期退出策略使用预定义层或启发式规则，无法直接判断表示何时对动作预测足够充分。

Method: LoopVLA采用循环架构，通过共享Transformer块迭代细化多模态token，每次迭代同时输出候选动作和充分性分数。引入自监督分布对齐目标，训练中间置信度分数匹配各细化步骤的相对动作质量，将充分性学习与策略优化信号关联起来。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus和VLA-Arena上，LoopVLA将参数减少45%，推理吞吐量提升1.7倍，同时在任务成功率上匹配或超越强基线。

Conclusion: LoopVLA通过循环细化与自监督充分性估计，有效推动了VLA策略的效率-性能前沿，为机器人操作中的计算效率与精确控制之间的权衡提供了新思路。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models typically treat the deepest representation of a vision-language backbone as universally optimal for action prediction. However, robotic manipulation is composed of many frequent closed-loop spatial adjustments, for which excessive abstraction may waste computation and weaken low-level geometric cues essential for precise control. Existing early-exit strategies attempt to reduce computation by stopping at predefined layers or applying heuristic rules such as action consistency, but they do not directly answer when a representation is actually sufficient for action. In this paper, we present LoopVLA, a recurrent VLA architecture that jointly learns representation refinement, action prediction, and sufficiency estimation. LoopVLA iteratively applies a shared Transformer block to refine multimodal tokens, and at each iteration produces both a candidate action and a sufficiency score that estimates whether further refinement is necessary. By sharing parameters across iterations, LoopVLA decouples refinement from absolute layer indices and grounds sufficiency estimation in the evolving representation itself. Since sufficiency has no direct supervision, we introduce a self-supervised distribution alignment objective, where intermediate confidence scores are trained to match the relative action quality across refinement steps, thereby linking sufficiency learning to policy optimization signals. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and VLA-Arena show that LoopVLA pushes the efficiency-performance frontier of VLA policies, reducing parameters by 45% and improving inference throughput by up to 1.7 times while matching or outperforming strong baselines in task success.

</details>


### [251] [Prospective Compression in Human Abstraction Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09985)
*Leonardo Hernandez Cano,Ivan Zareski,Luisa El Amouri,Pinzhe Zhao,Max Mascini,Emanuele Sansone,Yewen Pu,Bonan Zhao,Marta Kryven*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究非平稳环境下程序合成中的在线库学习问题，提出人类在该环境下采用前瞻性压缩策略选择抽象，而非传统的回顾性压缩。通过在Pattern Builder任务上的两项实验和六种计算模型对比，发现人类行为对潜在非平稳结构敏感，符合前瞻性压缩，而现有回顾性算法和LLM都无法捕捉该行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线库学习算法将库学习视为对静态任务分布的回顾性压缩，但真实学习环境常具有非平稳性，任务生成过程随时间演化。人类如何在非平稳领域中选择抽象是一个未被充分研究的问题。

Method: 使用Pattern Builder视觉程序合成范式设计两项实验，包含互补的潜在课程，用于区分前瞻性压缩与替代性库学习策略。构建六种计算模型（涵盖在线库学习策略和LLM程序合成）与人类行为进行对比分析。

Result: 人类抽象行为对任务生成过程中潜在的非平稳结构敏感，符合前瞻性压缩策略；现有回顾性压缩算法和LLM程序合成模型均无法捕捉该行为模式。

Conclusion: 非平稳领域中人类库学习选择抽象时采用前瞻性压缩策略，即针对未来任务的压缩进行抽象选择，这为设计更贴近人类认知的程序合成算法提供了新方向。

Abstract: A core challenge in program synthesis is online library learning: the incremental acquisition of reusable abstractions under uncertainty about future task demands. Existing algorithms treat library learning as retrospective compression over a static task distribution, where the learned library is determined by the corpus of past tasks. However, real-world learning domains are often non-stationary, with tasks arising from a generative process that evolves over time. We propose and test the hypothesis that in non-stationary domains human library learning selects abstractions prospectively: targeting compression of future tasks. We study this question using the Pattern Builder Task, a visual program synthesis paradigm in which participants construct increasingly complex geometric patterns from a small set of primitives, transformations, and custom helpers that carry forward across trials. Using this task, we conduct two experiments with complementary latent curricula, designed to dissociate between behaviors consistent with prospective compression, and alternative library learning accounts. Using six computational models spanning online library learning strategies, we show that human abstraction behavior reflects sensitivity to latent, non-stationary structure in the task-generating process. This behavior is consistent with prospective compression, and cannot be captured by existing retrospective compression-based algorithms, or inductive biases modeled by LLM-based program synthesis.

</details>


### [252] [TimeClaw: A Time-Series AI Agent with Exploratory Execution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10038)
*Hangchen Liu,Dongyuan Li,Renhe Jiang,Jiewen Deng,Weiwei Ye,Yoshihide Sekimoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeClaw是一个探索性执行学习框架，通过"探索-比较-蒸馏-回注"四阶段循环，将探索性执行转化为可复用的分层蒸馏经验，在保持基础模型冻结且避免在线测试时适应的情况下，显著提升时序分析任务的数值精度与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的时序分析方法以执行为中心，侧重于解决当前实例，但很少从探索性执行中学习。在可验证的数值场景中，多个候选执行和工具使用过程在任务上可能都有效，但在定量质量上差异显著，且早期成功会触发工具优先性坍缩，抑制进一步探索。

Method: TimeClaw提出四阶段循环：(1)探索(Explore)：执行多种候选方案；(2)比较(Compare)：基于度量监督对比执行结果质量；(3)蒸馏(Distill)：将高质量执行经验提炼为分层蒸馏经验；(4)回注(Reinject)：在推理时注入回用。核心组件包括度量监督的探索性执行学习、任务感知的工具丢弃机制、以及分层蒸馏经验。

Result: 在涵盖金融和天气预测与推理任务的17个任务的MTBench对齐评估中，TimeClaw在所有基线上均取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 对于科学系统，瓶颈不仅在于执行时的能力，更在于探索性经验如何被比较、蒸馏和复用。

Abstract: Time series analysis underpins forecasting, monitoring, and decision making in domains such as finance and weather, where solving a task often requires both numerical accuracy and contextual reasoning. Recent progress has moved from specialized neural predictors to approaches built on LLMs and foundation models that can reason over time series inputs and use external tools. However, most such systems remain execution-centric: they focus on solving the current instance but learn little from exploratory execution. This is especially limiting in verifiable numeric settings, where multiple candidate executions and tool-use procedures may all be task-valid yet differ sharply in quantitative quality, and where early success can trigger tool-prior collapse that suppresses further exploration. To address this limitation, we present TimeClaw, an exploratory execution learning framework that turns exploratory execution into reusable hierarchical distilled experience through a four-stage loop: Explore, Compare, Distill, and Reinject. TimeClaw combines metric-supervised exploratory execution learning, task-aware tool dropout, and hierarchical distilled experience for inference-time reinjection, while keeping the base model frozen and avoiding online test-time adaptation. In an MTBench-aligned evaluation with 17 tasks that span finance and weather prediction and reasoning tasks, TimeClaw delivers consistent gains over the baselines. These results suggest that, for scientific systems, the bottleneck is not only execution-time capability, but how exploratory experience is compared, distilled, and reused.

</details>


### [253] [Arcane: An Assertion Reduction Framework through Semantic Clustering and MCTS-Guided Rule Exploring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10107)
*Hongqin Lyu,Yonghao Wang,Zhiteng Chao,Tiancheng Wang,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Arcane框架，通过两级断言聚类和蒙特卡洛树搜索优化规则应用序列，有效减少冗余断言，在Assertionbench上实现最高76.2%的断言数量缩减，同时保持形式化覆盖率和突变检测能力，并带来2.6x-6.1x的仿真加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动化断言生成方法会产生大量冗余断言，严重降低仿真效率，因此需要一种高效的断言约简框架来缓解仿真开销。

Method: 提出Arcane框架，包含两级断言聚类方法实现大规模断言集的精确语义分类，并采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）探索最优规则应用序列以实现高效断言约简。

Result: 在Assertionbench上实现最高76.2%的断言数量缩减，完全保持形式化覆盖率和突变检测能力；仿真测试显示2.6x到6.1x的仿真时间加速。

Conclusion: Arcane能有效减少冗余断言数量，显著提升仿真效率，同时不损失覆盖率和检测能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Assertion-based Verification (ABV) is essential for ensuring that hardware designs conform to their intended specifications. However, existing automated assertion-generation approaches, such as LLM-based frameworks, often generate large numbers of redundant assertions, which significantly degrade simulation efficiency. To mitigate the simulation overhead caused by redundant assertions, this paper proposes Arcane, an efficient assertion reduction framework. It integrates a two-tier assertion clustering approach for accurate semantic classification of large assertion sets, and employs Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to explore optimal rule-application sequences for efficient assertion reduction. The experimental results on Assertionbench [20] show that Arcane achieves a reduction of up to 76.2% in the assertion count while fully preserving formal coverage and mutation-detection ability. Further simulation studies demonstrate a speedup of 2.6x to 6.1x speedup in simulation time. The proposed framework is released at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Arcane1-0A6F/.

</details>


### [254] [Rethinking Constraint Awareness for Efficient State Embedding of Neural Routing Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10122)
*Canhong Yu,Changliang Zhou,Rongsheng Chen,Zhenkun Wang,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对重型编码器-轻型解码器（HELD）神经路由求解器在处理复杂约束VRP变体时的局限性，从解码阶段状态嵌入（查询向量）的生成机制入手，指出现有机制限制了注意力计算中的观测空间。通过实证分析证明了全局观测空间的必要性，并提出约束感知残差调制（CARM）模块，通过自适应地调制上下文嵌入与约束相关变量，增强约束感知能力，使求解器能充分利用全局观测空间。实验证明CARM在多个单任务和多任务神经求解器上均能持续提升性能，并在大规模实例和未见VRP变体上展现出显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HELD神经路由求解器在处理具有复杂约束的VRP变体时表现不佳，现有解码阶段的状态嵌入生成机制限制了注意力计算中的观测空间，成为实现高质量解的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出约束感知残差调制（CARM）模块，通过自适应地使用约束相关变量调制上下文嵌入，增强约束感知能力，从而在不牺牲全局观测空间的前提下解决约束不可知问题。

Result: 在2个单任务和5个多任务神经路由求解器上，CARM模块持续提升基线性能，并在大规模实例扩展和未见VRP变体泛化方面取得显著改进。

Conclusion: CARM模块有效克服了全局观测空间的约束不可知缺陷，为神经路由求解器的架构设计提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Heavy-Encoder-Light-Decoder (HELD) neural routing solvers have emerged as a promising paradigm due to their broad applicability across multiple vehicle routing problems (VRPs). However, they typically struggle with VRP variants with complex constraints. To address this limitation, this paper systematically revisits existing neural solvers from the perspective of the generation mechanism for state embeddings (i.e., query vector prior to compatibility calculation) during decoding. We identify that current mechanisms restrict the observation space during attention computation, introducing a key bottleneck to achieving high-quality solutions. Through detailed empirical analysis, we demonstrate the necessity of preserving a global observation space. To overcome the constraint-agnostic drawback inherent to global observation spaces, we propose a simple yet powerful Constraint-Aware Residual Modulation (CARM) module. By adaptively modulating the context embedding with constraint-relevant variables, CARM effectively enhances constraint awareness, enabling the neural solver to fully leverage the global observation space and generate an efficient state embedding. Extensive experimental results across two single-task and five multi-task neural routing solvers confirm that the CARM module consistently boosts baseline performance. Notably, solvers equipped with our CARM achieve substantial improvements in scaling to large-scale instances and in generalizing to unseen VRP variants. These findings provide valuable insights for the architectural design of neural routing solvers.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [255] [Mitigating Many-shot Jailbreak Attacks with One Single Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08277)
*Kejia Chen,Jiawen Zhang,Boheng Li,Pengcheng Li,Jian Lou,Zunlei Feng,Mingli Song,Ruoxi Jia,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 许多样本越狱（MSJ）通过添加大量有害问答示例使安全对齐的模型回答有害问题。研究发现MSJ导致有害查询的表示逐步远离安全区域，可解释为隐式恶意微调。通过添加一个安全示例能抵消这种效应，恢复拒绝行为，无需修改参数或白盒访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究为什么许多样本越狱（MSJ）攻击会随着有害演示样本数量的增加而变强，并探索相应的防御机制。

Method: 1) 实证研究MSJ如何导致表示漂移（activation drift）；2) 理论上将MSJ解释为隐式恶意微调（SGD风格的更新）；3) 提出SafeEnd防御方法：在推理时添加固定的一次性安全演示示例，产生抵消性的安全导向更新。

Result: SafeEnd方法能有效恢复模型对有害查询的拒绝行为，提升模型对MSJ攻击的鲁棒性，且无需修改参数或白盒访问。

Conclusion: MSJ的攻击机制可理解为隐式恶意微调，而SafeEnd通过添加安全演示示例产生对抗性更新，将攻击机制转化为防御原则。

Abstract: Many-shot jailbreaking (MSJ) causes safety-aligned language models to answer harmful queries by preceding them with many harmful question-answer demonstrations. We study why this attack becomes stronger as the number of demonstrations increases. Empirically, we find that MSJ induces a progressive activation drift: the representation of a fixed harmful query moves step by step away from the safety-aligned region as more harmful demonstrations are added. Theoretically, we show that this drift can be interpreted as implicit malicious fine-tuning: conditioning on N harmful demonstrations induces SGD-style updates equivalent to optimizing on the corresponding N harmful samples. This view turns the attack mechanism into a defense principle. We append a fixed one-shot safety demonstration at inference time, which induces a counteracting safety-oriented update and restores refusal behavior. The resulting method improves the model's robustness to MSJ without modifying its parameters or requiring white-box access at deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/Thecommonirin/SafeEnd.

</details>


### [256] [WebTrap: Stealthy Mid-Task Hijacking of Browser Agents During Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08310)
*Zhichao Liu,Wenbo Pan,Haining Yu,Ge Gao,Tianqing Zhu,Xiaohua Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WebTrap是一种针对浏览器智能体的中任务劫持注入攻击，通过多步指令融合将攻击目标与用户目标无缝结合，并在攻击后恢复原始任务，实现了高成功率与高隐蔽性，暴露了长期任务中智能体系统的关键导航漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对浏览器智能体的提示注入攻击存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 有效性低，在复杂真实场景和长步骤任务中无法实现端到端攻击目标；(2) 隐蔽性差，多数攻击将攻击目标与用户目标对立，导致系统可用性显著下降。

Method: WebTrap提出两种核心技术：(1) 多步指令融合导向（Multi-step Instruction Fusion Steering），将攻击目标与用户目标无缝结合，使智能体执行攻击目标后能恢复原始用户任务；(2) 上下文接地生成（Context-grounded Generation），将注入内容与任务环境和系统指令对齐，最大化劫持成功率。

Result: 在基于扩展WASP和InjecAgent环境的两个浏览器智能体任务上，WebTrap实现了高攻击成功率，同时保持了原系统的可用性。实验发现WebTrap利用智能体的导航漏洞，将两个目标紧密绑定，标准防御机制无法将系统恢复正常运行。

Conclusion: WebTrap揭示了长期任务场景下浏览器智能体系统的关键安全漏洞——智能体可能在执行过程中被隐蔽劫持，现有防御机制难以抵御此类攻击，亟需针对多步指令融合的新型防御策略。

Abstract: Browser agents are increasingly deployed in long-horizon tasks, which require executing extended action chains to accomplish user goals. However, this prolonged execution process provides attackers with more opportunities to inject malicious instructions. Existing prompt injection attacks against browser agents expose two key gaps: (1) low effectiveness, as attacks optimized for toy baselines fail to achieve end-to-end goals in real-world scenarios with complex environments and longer steps; (2) weak stealthiness, since most attacks pit the attack goal against the user goal, causing a significant drop in system usability under attack. To address these gaps, we propose WebTrap, a mid-task hijacking injection attack. It employs multi-step instruction fusion steering to seamlessly combine both goals, enabling the agent to resume the original user task after executing the attack goal. Furthermore, we design a context-grounded generation method to align the injected content with the task environment and system instructions, maximizing the hijacking success rate. Extensive experiments on two browser agent tasks, based on extended WASP and InjecAgent environments, demonstrate that our method achieves a high attack success rate while preserving the usability of the original system. We find that WebTrap exploits the agent's navigation vulnerabilities, binding the two goals so tightly that standard defense mechanisms cannot restore the system to normal operation. These findings reveal a critical vulnerability in agent systems during long-horizon tasks that they can be stealthily hijacked.

</details>


### [257] [Seed Hijacking of LLM Sampling and Quantum Random Number Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08313)
*Ziyang You,Xiaoke Yang,Zhanling Fan,Feng Guo,Xiaogen Zhou,Xuxing Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SeedHijack攻击，通过操纵LLM自动回归采样中的伪随机数生成器，在不改变模型logits的情况下强制选择攻击者指定的token，攻击成功率达99.6%-100%；并提出了基于硬件量子随机数生成器的防御方案，仅带来可忽略的额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御忽视了LLM依赖确定性伪随机数生成器进行自动回归采样所带来的关键供应链攻击面。

Method: SeedHijack通过操纵PRNG输出，在不改变模型logits的情况下强制选择攻击者指定的token；防御方案采用硬件量子随机数生成器替代PRNG。

Result: 在GPT-2 (124M)上540次试验中达到99.6%的精确token注入成功率；在四个对齐模型（1.5B-7B，RLHF/SFT/推理蒸馏）上达到100%成功率；QRNG防御引入的中位延迟开销仅+0.6%，内存开销+7.7 MB。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM采样层的关键漏洞，并提供了实用、可部署的基于QRNG的防御方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) rely on deterministic pseudorandom number generators (PRNGs) for autoregressive sampling, creating a critical supply-chain attack surface overlooked by existing defenses. We present SeedHijack, a backdoor attack that manipulates PRNG outputs to force attacker-specified token selection without altering model logits. In a 540-trial benchmark on GPT-2 (124M), the attack achieves 99.6% exact token injection across 9 sampling configurations; it reaches 100% success on four aligned models (1.5B-7B, RLHF/SFT/reasoning distillation) and bypasses all alignment methods tested in this work. We further propose a defense based on a hardware quantum random number generator (QRNG), which neutralizes the attack in our evaluated threat model with negligible median overhead (+0.6% latency, +7.7 MB memory). Our work identifies a critical sampling-layer vulnerability and provides a practical, deployable QRNG-based defense.

</details>


### [258] [AI-Driven Security Alert Screening and Alert Fatigue Mitigation in Security Operations Centers: A Comprehensive Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08316)
*Samuel Ndichu,Akira Yamada,Tao Ban,Seiichi Ozawa,Takeshi Takahashi,Daisuke Inoue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对2015-2026年间AI驱动的安全告警筛选与告警疲劳缓解研究进行了综述，基于119篇文献（含87篇核心研究）构建了包含过滤、分类、关联和生成式增强四阶段工作流分类体系，指出了部署真实性、对抗鲁棒性、跨环境验证和评估实践方面的持续不足，并提出了可信认知安全运营中心的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心中告警筛选是下游关键任务，但告警疲劳问题日益严重，亟需对AI驱动的解决方案进行系统性梳理，明确现有方法的局限性与未来方向。

Method: 基于119篇文献（含87篇核心研究）进行系统性综述，构建了包含过滤（filtering）、分类（triage）、关联（correlation）和生成式增强（generative augmentation）四阶段的工作流分类体系。

Result: 识别出当前领域存在的四大持续不足：部署真实性不足、对抗鲁棒性缺乏、跨环境验证欠缺以及评估实践不完善。

Conclusion: 提出了向可信认知安全运营中心（Cognitive Security Operations Centers）发展的研究议程，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Security alert screening is the downstream task of filtering, prioritizing, correlating, and contextualizing alerts for analyst attention in Security Operations Centers. This survey reviews artificial-intelligence-driven alert screening and alert-fatigue mitigation from 2015 to 2026. We synthesize 119 records, including 87 core studies, into a four-stage workflow taxonomy covering filtering, triage, correlation, and generative augmentation. We find persistent gaps in deployment realism, adversarial robustness, cross-environment validation, and evaluation practice. The survey concludes with a research agenda toward trustworthy Cognitive Security Operations Centers.

</details>


### [259] [Kettle: Attested builds for verifiable software provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08363)
*Amean Asad,André Arko*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Kettle 是一个基于 TEE 的可信构建系统，通过在机密虚拟机内执行构建并生成经硬件签名的可验证来源证明文档，使验证者只需校验一次硬件签名和若干摘要，无需重新执行构建，从而将构建基础设施、运维人员和分发渠道从信任基中移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件构建系统无法提供端到端的可验证来源证明，验证者必须信任构建基础设施及其运维人员，且缺乏机密性保护，构建过程中源代码可能暴露给主机。

Method: 利用可信执行环境（TEE）中的机密虚拟机（CVM）执行构建，记录源提交、依赖集、工具链、构建环境和输出工件摘要至来源证明文档；将文档的 SHA-256 摘要写入 TEE 平台认证报告的 report-data 字段，使硬件签名的认证报告本身成为来源证明的签名；CVM 镜像可重现，启动度量值公开且稳定，允许构建请求者在提交输入前预先认证 CVM，并通过 TLS 通道在 CVM 内部终止以保护源代码机密性。

Result: 验证过程简化为一次针对厂商根证书的签名校验和少量摘要比较，无需重新执行构建；构建基础设施、运维人员和分发渠道被从信任基中移除，且构建过程具备端到端的机密性，主机无法看到明文源代码。

Conclusion: Kettle 通过 TEE 硬件信任链替代了对构建基础设施和运维人员的信任，实现了可密码学验证的软件构建来源证明，显著缩小了验证者必须接受的信任面。

Abstract: Kettle is an attested build system that produces cryptographically verifiable provenance for software built inside Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs). A Kettle build records the source commit, dependency set, toolchain, build environment, and output artifact digests in a provenance document produced inside a measured confidential VM. The SHA-256 digest of that document is committed to the TEE platform's attestation report-data field, so the hardware-signed attestation report is itself the signature on the provenance, with the signing identity chaining to the TEE manufacturer's root of trust rather than to the build infrastructure operator. Because the CVM image is itself reproducible, its launch measurement is public and stable, which lets a build requester pre-attest the CVM before submitting any input and optionally deliver source over a TLS channel terminated inside it, so the build runs end-to-end confidentially without the host ever seeing source code in plaintext. Verification reduces to one signature check against the vendor root and a small set of digest comparisons, with no need to re-execute the build. The result removes the build infrastructure, its operators, and the artifact distribution channel from the trust surface a verifier must accept when deciding whether a binary corresponds to its claimed inputs.

</details>


### [260] [Binge, Bot, Repeat: Unpacking the Ecosystem of Video Piracy on Telegram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08418)
*Sadikshya Gyawali,Jaishnoor Kaur,Taylor Graham,Josef Horacek,Nowshin Tabassum,Shirin Nilizadeh,Sayak Saha Roy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对Telegram上的大规模视频盗版生态系统进行了系统研究，分析了1,057个频道（2023年12月至2026年1月间分享209k条帖子），构建了细粒度分类法，发现这些频道共分发19,033个版权作品（来自175个国家），累计48.5亿次浏览，造成约174.9亿美元损失。该生态系统通过中介频道链和自动化机器人实现抗打击能力。作者开发了Anti-RIP实时检测框架，在61天内协助下架了524个盗版频道和71个机器人，并开源了数据集和框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram已成为大规模视频盗版的主要平台，但其生态系统的结构和运营动态缺乏系统性理解，现有研究不足。

Method: 采用混合方法分析1,057个频道的209k条帖子（2023年12月至2026年1月），开发细粒度分类法对每个帖子的活动和意图进行结构化理解，并构建Anti-RIP实时检测框架。

Result: 共分发19,033个版权作品（来自175个国家），累计48.5亿次浏览，预估损失174.9亿美元。生态系统通过中介频道链和自动化机器人实现抗打击能力。Anti-RIP在61天内协助下架524个盗版频道和71个机器人。

Conclusion: Telegram视频盗版生态系统规模巨大且工程化程度高，具有强大的抗打击能力。Anti-RIP框架能有效检测和辅助下架盗版内容，开源数据集和框架有助于未来研究。

Abstract: Telegram has emerged as a major platform for large-scale video piracy, where copyrighted content is rapidly distributed among users. Despite its prominence, the structural and operational dynamics of this ecosystem remain insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we present the first large-scale study of video piracy on Telegram through a mixed-method analysis of 1,057 channels that shared 209k unique posts between December 2023 and January 2026 - systematically characterizing their content, distribution strategies, and how the ecosystem is sustained at scale. Central to our approach is the development of a fine-grained taxonomy that enables a structured understanding of the activity and intent of these channels on a per-post level. The channels collectively distributed 19,033 unique copyrighted titles originating from 175 countries, accumulating over 4.85B unique views and resulting in a lower-bound estimated financial loss of $17.49B for content rights holders. We also find that this ecosystem is deliberately engineered to be resilient against takedown efforts, frequently redirecting users through chains of intermediary channels and automated bots that collectively handle hosting, access control, monetization, and channel discovery. The scale and persistence of this ecosystem motivated the development of Anti-RIP, a real-time framework for detecting emerging video piracy communities on Telegram. Anti-RIP utilizes our taxonomy to generate contextual, interpretable insights that stakeholders confirmed improve the triaging action against reported posts and channels. Over a 61-day period, the framework facilitated the takedown of 524 previously unknown piracy channels and 71 bots. To support reproducibility and future research, we open-source both the dataset and the Anti-RIP framework.

</details>
