### [261] [Deterministic Fully-Static Whole-Binary Translation without Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08419)
*Hongyu Chen,James McGowan,Michael Franz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Elevator 是首个无需调试信息或源码，即可将完整 x86-64 二进制程序静态翻译为 AArch64 的二进制翻译器。它通过穷举所有字节解释路径生成独立二进制文件，性能与 QEMU JIT 相当或更优，但具有无运行时组件、可预认证等优势，主要代价是代码体积显著膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制翻译系统依赖启发式方法或运行时回退机制来处理代码与数据解码错误，缺乏确定性且部署前无法充分验证。Elevator 旨在实现完全静态的、确定性的全程序二进制翻译，消除运行时组件，使得翻译结果可在部署前进行测试、验证、认证和加密签名，降低安全风险。

Method: Elevator 对所有字节的所有可能解释（数据、操作码、操作码参数）进行穷举，生成所有可行解释对应的控制流路径，仅剪枝导致异常终止的路径。翻译通过自动从源 ISA 的高层描述中派生的代码"tiles"组合构建，采用轻量级翻译框架。

Result: 在包含完整 SPECint 2006 基准套件的多样化真实世界二进制程序上评估，Elevator 达到了与 QEMU 用户模式 JIT 模拟相当或更优的性能，证明了静态全程序二进制翻译的可靠性和实用性。

Conclusion: 静态全程序二进制翻译可以是可靠且实用的。尽管代码体积显著膨胀，但 Elevator 提供了确定性翻译、无运行时可信组件、可预部署验证等关键优势，为二进制翻译提供了不同于 JIT/模拟器的新范式。

Abstract: We present Elevator, the first binary translator that statically translates entire x86-64 executables to AArch64 without debug information, source code, or assumptions about code layout. Unlike existing systems, which rely on heuristics or runtime fallbacks to handle code-versus-data decoding errors, Elevator considers all possible interpretations of every byte and produces a separate translation for each feasible one ahead of time. Any byte may be interpreted as data, an opcode, or an opcode argument; we generate separate control flow paths for all interpretations, pruning only those leading to abnormal termination.
  Translations are built by composing code "tiles" automatically derived from a high-level description of the source ISA, yielding a nimble translation framework. The approach is deterministic and produces complete, self-contained binaries with no runtime component in the trusted code base. The principal cost is substantial code size expansion. The key benefit is that the output is the actual code that will run, enabling testing, validation, certification, and cryptographic signing prior to deployment, reducing risk compared to emulators or JIT compilers.
  We evaluate Elevator on a diverse corpus of real-world binaries, including the entire SPECint 2006 suite, demonstrating that static full-program binary translation can be both reliable and practical. Elevator achieves performance on par with or better than QEMU's user-mode JIT emulation.

</details>


### [262] [Improving Parameter-Efficient Federated Learning with Differentially Private Refactorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08443)
*Linh Tran,Ana Milanova,Stacy Patterson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedPower 是一种差分隐私跨孤岛联邦学习框架，通过显式重构和裁剪全秩客户端更新来限制敏感度，并利用 PowerDP 机制（基于同步子空间迭代，在正交归一化前注入校准噪声）将聚合更新投影回安全低秩空间，有效缓解 DP 噪声对 LoRA 的不利影响，在严格隐私预算下实现鲁棒性并提升隐私-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DP+LoRA 的跨孤岛 FL 方法在全局聚合时会引入误差，且低秩子空间中的加性噪声容易淹没权重矩阵信号，导致次优精度。本文旨在解决这一脆弱性。

Method: 提出 FedPower 框架，包含：（1）显式重构和裁剪全秩客户端更新以限制敏感度；（2）设计 PowerDP——一种基于同步子空间迭代的差分隐私低秩分解机制，在正交归一化前注入校准 DP 噪声，通过保持矩阵正交性缓解噪声负面影响。

Result: 在多种语言理解任务的跨孤岛 FL 实验中，FedPower 在严格隐私预算下具有鲁棒性且计算开销可忽略；消融研究验证了 PowerDP 在隐私-精度权衡上的有效性；三种成员推理攻击评估验证了隐私保护能力。

Conclusion: FedPower 通过重构全秩更新和新型低秩分解机制，有效解决了 DP+LoRA 在跨孤岛 FL 中的噪声放大问题，提供了样本级和客户级 DP 的理论保证，并在隐私、精度和效率之间取得了优越的平衡。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) with parameter-efficient fine-tuning, such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), enables scalable model training on distributed data. However, when combined with Differential Privacy (DP), LoRA often introduces errors during global aggregation and amplifies the negative effect of DP noise. Existing cross-silo FL approaches mitigate the aggregation error by freezing one LoRA module and applying output perturbation. However, in a restricted low-rank subspaces, this additive noise frequently overwhelms the signals of the weight matrices, leading to suboptimal accuracy. To address this vulnerability, we propose FedPower, a differentially private cross-silo FL framework that reshapes server-side aggregation. Instead of perturbing mismatched low-rank factors, FedPower explicitly reconstructs and clips full-rank client updates to bound the sensitivity. The server then projects the exact aggregated update back into a secure low-rank space using PowerDP, a novel differentially private low-rank factorization mechanism. Based on simultaneous subspace iteration, PowerDP injects calibrated DP noise prior to the final orthonormalization step, effectively mitigates the negative effect of DP noise by preserving matrix orthogonality. We provide rigorous theoretical analyses establishing sensitivity bounds for subspace projections, proving that FedPower achieves both sample-level and client-level DP. Extensive experiments on various language understanding tasks in cross-silo FL settings show that FedPower is robust against tight privacy budgets while adding negligible computational overheads. Additional empirical study on different DP noise injection schemes validates the effectiveness of PowerDP in improving the tradeoff in accuracy and privacy. Evaluation on three different membership inference attacks validates the robustness and privacy-preserving capability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [263] [HEART: A High-Efficiency Adaptive Real-Time Telemonitoring Framework for Secure Electrocardiogram Signal Transmission Using Chaotic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08456)
*Beyazıt Bestami Yuksel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可学习密钥生成器（LKG）的实时心电监测系统，利用患者自身ECG信号特征动态生成唯一加密密钥，结合逻辑映射混沌加密，实现心电数据的实时安全传输与云存储，并通过全面的安全评估验证了其高安全性和近乎无损的重构性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心电图信号的实时分析对于远程医疗中的准确诊断至关重要，同时患者隐私保护也面临严峻挑战。传统静态密钥加密方法可预测性高、个性化不足，且密钥分发过程存在安全风险，亟需一种既保证实时性又具备高安全性的ECG加密方案。

Method: 提出可学习密钥生成器（LKG），利用患者自身ECG信号特征动态生成唯一加密密钥；该密钥用于确定逻辑映射（logistic map）的参数r和x0，进而执行混沌加密；系统在ECG数据采集后立即加密，实现端到端安全传输与云存储；解密时在医生端使用匹配密钥进行解密。每段生物特征密钥刷新结合排列与XOR扩散操作，通过最小熵评估增强安全性。

Result: Shannon熵达7.678比特；重构保真度MSE约5×10⁻⁶，PSNR大于52 dB，MAE约0.002；通过了NIST SP 800-22频率测试、明文密钥敏感性测试、雪崩效应测试、FFT频谱平坦度测试及噪声遮挡鲁棒性测试；加密延迟低，保持实时性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于LKG的混沌加密系统在保证实时性的同时提供了高安全性和近乎无损的解密质量，消除了传统密钥交换需求，显著提高了穷举密钥搜索成本，适用于远程医疗中的ECG安全传输场景。

Abstract: The realtime analysis and secure transmission of electrocardiogram ECG signals are critical for accurate diagnosis and safeguarding patient privacy in telemedicine applications This study presents a novel realtime ECG monitoring system that employs a learnable key generator LKG derived from each patients own ECG signal characteristics to dynamically produce unique encryption keys These keys determine the parameters r and x0 of a logistic map used for chaotic encryption The system securely encrypts realtime ECG data immediately after acquisition ensuring confidential transmission and storage in the cloud For remote clinical access the encrypted data is downloaded and decrypted on the doctors side using the matching key generated at the source or securely stored in the cloud This approach eliminates the need for traditional key exchange and substantially raises the cost of exhaustive key search in practice through persegment biometric key refresh and combined permutation and XOR diffusion supported by minentropy evaluation Compared to statickey methods the learnable biometric key design offers greater unpredictability and individualization A comprehensive set of security assessments including Shannon entropy 7678 bits correlation and autocorrelation disruption histogram statistics NIST SP 80022 frequency testing plaintextkey sensitivity avalanche effect FFTbased spectral flatness and robustness to noise and occlusion confirms the methods strength Reconstruction fidelity MSE approximately 5x106 PSNR greater than 52 dB MAE approximately 0002 demonstrates nearlossless decryption and preserved diagnostic features Encryption latency remains low preserving realtime performance.

</details>


### [264] [Computer Science Conferences Should Require Nonrepudiable Experimental Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08586)
*Mamadou K. Keita,Christopher Homan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文主张计算机科学会议应要求对实验结果进行防篡改、不可否认的认证，定义了“实验不可否认性”问题，提出了安全属性要求和威胁模型，并构建了参考实现K-Veritas。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算机科学会议依赖自我报告、可选的代码共享和作者控制的日志，这些机制无法让审稿人验证论文中报告的数字是否确实由所描述的代码计算产生，缺乏不可否认的认证机制。

Method: 形式化定义了实验不可否认性问题及其安全属性，描述了威胁模型，并构建了K-Veritas参考实现（Go语言），可生成不访问训练数据的签名报告。

Result: K-Veritas作为测试床实现，证明了该问题可解，能够生成签名的实验结果报告。

Conclusion: 呼吁会议和社区将不可否认性作为一等公民要求，共同建立开放、独立的标准化解决方案。

Abstract: This position paper argues that computer science conferences should require tamper-evident, nonrepudiable attestations of experimental results. We name the underlying problem experiment nonrepudiation: a compliant protocol must bind the numbers in a paper to an actual executed computation in a way the author cannot later alter or deny. The current system relies on self-reported checklists, optional code sharing, and author-controlled logging. None of these mechanisms answer the question a reviewer cannot check: did the code the paper describes produce the numbers the paper reports? We define the problem formally, state the security properties any compliant protocol must satisfy, and describe a threat model that includes attacks current approaches do not prevent. To show that the problem is solvable, we built K-Veritas, a reference implementation in Go that produces signed reports without accessing training data. K-Veritas is a testbed, not a finished answer. We call on conferences and the community to treat nonrepudiation as a first-class requirement and to help build an open, independent standard for it.

</details>


### [265] [WATSON: Leveraging Data Watchpoints for Shadow Stack Protection on Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08604)
*Xi Tan,Sagar Mohan,Ziming Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WATSON 是一种针对嵌入式/IoT 设备的影子栈方案，利用标准硬件调试单元（数据监视点）实现系统级保护，无需 TEE 或专用硬件扩展，在 ARM Cortex-M 上实现了低开销（7.33%/1.81%）和有效防御控制流劫持攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入式影子栈方案存在四个主要局限：缺乏对中断和异常的系统级保护、性能开销高、依赖可信执行环境等非通用安全扩展、依赖具有可配置约束的硬件特性导致兼容性挑战。

Method: WATSON 利用标准硬件调试单元中的数据监视点（data watchpoints）的地址匹配特性来强制执行影子栈的写保护，防止未授权访问；同时兼容编译器选项以支持前向边控制流完整性。

Result: 在 ARM Cortex-M 架构上实现原型：BEEBS 基准测试开销 7.33%，CoreMark-Pro 基准测试开销 1.81%；异常处理和两个真实应用的性能开销可忽略不计，最坏情况下代码大小开销为 2.11%；安全评估证明有效防御攻击。

Conclusion: WATSON 提供了一种高效、有效的影子栈解决方案，克服了现有方案的主要局限，且概念可扩展到其他平台。

Abstract: Embedded and Internet-of-Things (IoT) devices play a critical role in modern life. Their software and firmware, often developed in memory-unsafe languages like C, are susceptible to memory safety vulnerabilities that can lead to control-flow hijacking attacks. Shadow stack is a defense mechanism against control-flow hijacking that targets return addresses. However, existing shadow stack solutions for embedded systems have the following limitations. First, they lack system-wide protection, particularly for interrupts and exceptions. Second, they introduce high performance overhead. Third, they depend on security extensions like a trusted execution environment, which are not universally available on embedded devices. Finally, they rely on hardware features that have inherent configurable constraints, which pose compatibility challenges when integrating security mechanisms that require similar hardware support.
  To overcome these limitations, we present WATSON, an efficient and effective shadow stack solution. It leverages a standard hardware debug unit named data watchpoints for shadow stack protection on embedded systems. To prevent unauthorized access to the shadow stack, WATSON leverages the address-matching features of the debug unit to enforce the write protection of the shadow stack. Additionally, WATSON is compatible with compiler options to enforce forward-edge control-flow integrity.
  We implemented a prototype of WATSON on the ARM CortexM architecture, and the concept also applies to other platforms. The introduced overhead is 7.33% and 1.81% on BEEBS and CoreMark-Pro benchmarks, respectively. We also evaluate WATSON on exception handling and two real-world applications, observing negligible performance overhead and a worst-case code size overhead of 2.11%. Furthermore, our security evaluation demonstrates that WATSON effectively prevents attacks.

</details>


### [266] [AI-Accelerated Brute Force Cryptanalysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08690)
*Gideon Samid*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出了AI加速暴力破解的新型攻击方式，并指出NIST后量子密码学标准对此并未免疫；作为应对，提出了“无模式密码学”（Pattern Devoid Cryptography）作为新的安全类别，其核心防御手段包括非平凡密文、单边随机性和可变密钥长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密码学依赖“不从不正确的密钥中学习”这一原则，但AI能从看似随机的错误明文候选中发现模式，从而加速密钥空间的搜索。这一新的攻击向量要求重新审视现有的密码安全体系。

Method: 利用AI对看似随机数据中模式的学习能力，通过分析错误密钥产生的“错误明文”来去平坦化剩余密钥空间的概率分布曲线，从而加速暴力破解过程。

Result: AI可以显著加速暴力破解攻击，且当前NIST PQC标准对此类攻击并未免疫。

Conclusion: 需要引入新的防御策略：非平凡密文、单边随机性和可变密钥长度，并据此提出“无模式密码学”（Pattern Devoid Cryptography）作为新的安全范式。

Abstract: Modern cryptography is hinged on "not learning from mistakes": trying numerous wrong keys, should not help one identify the right key. Indeed, it worked -- until recently when the surprising power of AI to see pattern in apparent randomness has turned the 'wrong plaintexts' generated by the 'wrong key' into productive inferential input. Crunching through these random-looking plaintext candidates AI can de-flatten the probability curve over the remaining key space. The more spiked this curve, the faster the ciphertext is defeated. This new attack vector demands a thorough review of our cryptographic security posture. NIST PQC is not immunized against AI-Accelerated Brute Force attack. Defense is rooted in non-trivial ciphertexts, in unilateral randomness, and in variable key size. This points to a new security class: Pattern Devoid Cryptography which is to be added into the toolbox used by the cyber security community.

</details>


### [267] [When LLMs Team Up: A Coordinated Attack Framework for Automated Cyber Intrusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08763)
*Minfeng Qi,Tianqing Zhu,Zijie Xu,Congcong Zhu,Qin Wang,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CAESAR是一个协调多智能体框架，通过五种角色分工、有界轮次协议、持久知识库、验证器门控知识提升和能力令牌写隔离等机制，在25个CTF任务中相比单智能体基线提升了任务成功率并降低了性能方差，尤其适用于多步骤利用组合任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化入侵式工作流需要LLM智能体在有限预算下对部分观测、工具输出和可执行工件进行推理。单个LLM实例将证据提取、规划、执行和验证压缩到一个上下文中，增加了上下文漂移和错误传播的风险。现有基于LLM的多智能体系统支持通用协作，但未明确建模多阶段入侵工作流中的角色边界、工件溯源和成本约束。

Method: CAESAR将工作流分解为五种有类型角色，通过有界轮次协议进行协调，包含持久知识库、每轮工作空间、验证器门控知识提升以及能力令牌写隔离机制。

Result: 在25个CTF任务（五种类别、四种LLM后端）上，CAESAR相比匹配预算和工具访问的单智能体基线提升了任务成功率并降低了性能方差，在需要多步骤利用组合的任务上增益更大。二次模拟交互安全研究表明角色结构可迁移到代码原生表面之外。

Conclusion: 角色转换、工件溯源和知识提升事件为监控协调的LLM智能体行为提供了超越单次提示和输出检查的结构性信号。

Abstract: Automated intrusion-style workflows require LLM agents to reason over partial observations, tool outputs, and executable artifacts under bounded budgets. A single LLM instance often compresses evidence extraction, planning, execution, and validation into one context, which increases the risk of context drift and error propagation. Existing LLM-based multi-agent systems support general collaboration, but they do not explicitly model the role boundaries, artifact provenance, and cost constraints that characterize multi-stage intrusion workflows.
  This paper presents CAESAR, a coordinated multi-agent framework for controlled analysis of LLM-agent behavior in intrusion-style tasks. CAESAR decomposes the workflow into five typed roles and coordinates them through a bounded round protocol with a persistent knowledge base, a per-round workspace, validator-gated knowledge promotion, and capability-token write isolation. We evaluate CAESAR on 25 CTF tasks across five categories and four LLM backends. Compared with a single-agent baseline under matched budgets and tool access, CAESAR improves task success and reduces performance variance, with larger gains on tasks requiring multi-step exploit composition. A secondary simulated interactional-security study suggests that the role structure can transfer beyond code-native surfaces. The results indicate that role transitions, artifact provenance, and knowledge-promotion events provide useful structural signals for monitoring coordinated LLM-agent behavior beyond individual prompt and output inspection. The dataset, implementation, and evaluation logs are released at https://github.com/Xu-Qiu/CMAS.

</details>


### [268] [Why Do Aligned LLMs Remain Jailbreakable: Refusal-Escape Directions, Operator-Level Sources, and Safety-Utility Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08878)
*Yu Chen,Yuanhao Liu,Qi Cao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过连续输入变换视角，发现对齐LLM中存在「拒绝-逃逸方向（RED）」：沿该方向持续扰动有害输入会导致模型从拒绝回答转向回答，而语义理解不变。RED可分解为归一化、残差连接和终端源等算子级贡献。消除RED需要自注意力和MLP模块同时抑制这些贡献并保留良性回答能力，形成条件性安全-效用权衡。实验验证了RED的存在及其与终端源贡献的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽已发现与越狱攻击相关的潜在特征和表征偏移，但未能回答根本性问题：为什么对齐的LLM仍然容易被越狱？模型中的何种结构漏洞导致了这种脆弱性？本文旨在从理论层面解释对齐模型越狱的结构性原因。

Method: 采用连续输入变换视角，提出RED（Refusal-Escape Directions）概念，即围绕有害输入的局部扰动方向。通过理论证明RED可分解为模型算子结构的三种源贡献：归一化源、残差wiring源和终端源。实验上从两个互补视角（添加token维度暴露RED、分析成功越狱中的RED对齐程度）验证理论。

Result: 1. 成功证明RED的存在性及其分解性；2. 识别出三类算子级源贡献；3. 发现消除RED需自注意力和MLP同时满足竞争性约束，导致安全-效用权衡；4. 实验表明添加token维度可暴露RED，成功越狱中拒绝-回答转变与终端源贡献高度对齐。

Conclusion: 对齐LLM的越狱脆弱性根源于模型算子结构中的固有RED方向，尤其是终端源贡献。由于消除RED需要共享表达模块在抑制越狱路径和保留良性功能之间平衡，安全性和效用性之间存在根本性的条件性权衡。这一发现为理解LLM安全机制提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Aligned large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks. Recent mechanistic studies have identified latent features and representation shifts associated with jailbreak success, but they leave a more fundamental question open: why do aligned LLMs remain jailbreakable, and what structural vulnerabilities in the model make this possible? We study this question through a continuous input-transformation view. Our theoretical finding is that aligned models can still exhibit Refusal-Escape Directions (RED): local perturbation directions around a harmful input that shift the model's behavior from refusal to answering while preserving the model's harmful-semantics interpretation. From this perspective, a jailbreak is not only a successful discrete prompt construction, but can also be understood as a refusal-to-answer behavior transition induced by continuously perturbing a harmful input along RED. We then prove that RED can be exactly decomposed into contributions from operator-level sources across the model's operator structure, and identify normalization, residual-wiring, and terminal sources as analytically constrained operator-level sources. To eliminate RED, the shared expressive modules -- self-attention and MLP -- must eliminate the contributions from these analytically constrained sources while preserving the mechanisms that support benign responses. These competing requirements give rise to a conditional safety-utility trade-off. Experiments across multiple models and attack methods empirically analyze RED from two complementary perspectives and show that added token dimensions can expose RED, while successful jailbreaks exhibit refusal-to-answer shifts largely aligned with terminal-source contributions.

</details>


### [269] [Hardware-Accelerated Line-Rate Bitstream Screening for Secure FPGA Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08984)
*Rye Stahle-Smith,Carter Antley,Jason D. Bakos,Rasha Karakchi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BLADEI是一个面向FPGA部署阶段的比特流级安全检测框架，无需源代码或网表即可检测恶意配置，在1383个比特流上达到0.91的宏F1分数，但软件特征提取占92%延迟；为此提出PL加速引擎可将特征提取延迟降至毫秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着FPGA在多租户云和边缘AI环境中规模化部署，配置比特流成为关键但不可见的安全边界。现有硬件木马检测方法依赖可信设计工件或计算密集的反向工程，在动态"即时"重配置工作流中引入难以接受的延迟。

Method: 提出混合架构，结合多尺度字节序列学习与紧凑统计表示，直接从原始比特流检测异常配置。在PYNQ-Z1上实现端到端云到边缘流水线，并提出流式硬件加速特征提取引擎部署于FPGA可编程逻辑(PL)。

Result: 在1383个比特流上评估，宏F1分数达0.91。软件特征提取占总延迟16.4秒的92%（约15秒），模型推理仅需1.4秒。PL加速引擎可将特征提取延迟降至毫秒级。

Conclusion: 比特流级筛查可作为一流安全原语，硬件加速预处理是实现下一代可重构计算设备在线速率安全防护的关键使能技术。

Abstract: As Field-Programmable Gate Arrays (FPGAs) scale in multi-tenant cloud and edge-AI environments, the configuration bitstream has become a critical, yet opaque, security boundary. Existing hardware Trojan detection methods often rely on trusted design artifacts or computationally intensive reverse-engineering, introducing prohibitive latencies in dynamic, "just-in-time" reconfiguration workflows. This paper presents BLADEI (Bitstream-Level Abnormality Detection for Embedded Inference), a bitstream-level security framework designed for deployment-time screening of FPGA configurations without requiring source code, netlists, or vendor-specific tooling. BLADEI introduces a hybrid architecture that combines multi-scale byte-sequence learning with compact statistical representations to detect anomalous configurations directly from raw bitstreams. We implement the framework on a Xilinx PYNQ-Z1 system, demonstrating an end-to-end cloud-to-edge pipeline that enforces security prior to FPGA configuration. Evaluating across 1,383 bitstreams, BLADEI achieves a macro F1-score of 0.91. However, our systems-level characterization reveals a "preprocessing wall": software-based feature extraction accounts for 92% of the total 16.4-second latency, while model inference requires only 1.4 seconds. To address this bottleneck, we propose a streaming hardware-accelerated feature extraction engine designed for the FPGA programmable logic (PL). The evaluation shows that PL-based streaming engine can reduce feature-extraction latency to the millisecond range. This work positions bitstream-level screening as a first-class primitive and demonstrates that hardware-accelerated preprocessing is the key enabler for securing next-generation reconfigurable custom computing machines at line rate.

</details>


### [270] [ShadowMerge: A Novel Poisoning Attack on Graph-Based Agent Memory via Relation-Channel Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09033)
*Yang Luo,Zifeng Kang,Tiantian Ji,Xinran Liu,Yong Liu,Shuyu Li,Lingyun Peng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SHADOWMERGE攻击，针对基于图的智能体记忆系统，利用关系通道冲突注入恶意关系，在Mem0和三个数据集上达到93.8%平均攻击成功率，比现有最佳基线高出50.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图的智能体记忆虽能支持结构化长期记忆和多跳推理，但也引入了新的投毒面：攻击者可注入精心构造的关系到图记忆中，后续被检索时影响智能体行为。现有攻击主要针对平面文本记录，在图记忆场景下无效，因为恶意关系往往无法被提取、合并到目标锚点邻域或为受害者查询检索到。

Method: 提出SHADOWMERGE攻击，核心洞察是利用关系通道冲突：投毒关系可与良性证据共享相同的查询激活锚点和规范化关系通道，但携带冲突值。为此设计AIR流水线，将冲突转化为可被图记忆系统提取、合并和检索的普通交互。

Result: 在Mem0和PubMedQA、WebShop、ToolEmu三个真实数据集上评估，SHADOWMERGE达到93.8%平均攻击成功率，比最佳基线提高50.3个百分点，且对无关良性任务影响可忽略。机制研究表明能克服现有攻击的三个关键局限；防御分析表明代表性输入侧防御不足以缓解。

Conclusion: SHADOWMERGE成功揭示了基于图的智能体记忆系统的安全漏洞，已负责任披露给相关厂商并开源代码。

Abstract: Graph-based agent memory is increasingly used in LLM agents to support structured long-term recall and multi-hop reasoning, but it also creates a new poisoning surface: an attacker can inject a crafted relation into graph memory so that it is later retrieved and influences agent behavior. Existing agent-memory poisoning attacks mainly target flat textual records and are ineffective in graph-based memory because malicious relations often fail to be extracted, merged into the target anchor neighborhood, or retrieved for the victim query.
  We present SHADOWMERGE, a poisoning attack against graph-based agent memory that exploits relation-channel conflicts. Its key insight is that a poisoned relation can share the same query-activated anchor and canonicalized relation channel as benign evidence while carrying a conflicting value. To realize this, we design AIR, a pipeline that converts the conflict into an ordinary interaction that can be extracted, merged, and retrieved by the graph-memory system. We evaluate SHADOWMERGE on Mem0 and three public real-world datasets: PubMedQA, WebShop, and ToolEmu. SHADOWMERGE achieves 93.8% average attack success rate, improving the best baseline by 50.3 absolute points, while having negligible impact on unrelated benign tasks. Mechanism studies show that SHADOWMERGE overcomes the three key limitations of existing agent-memory poisoning attacks, and defense analysis shows that representative input-side defenses are insufficient to mitigate it. We have responsibly disclosed our findings to affected graph-memory vendors and open sourced SHADOWMERGE.

</details>


### [271] [AI Native Asset Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09115)
*Gal Engelberg,Leon Goldberg,Konstantin Koutsyi,Boris Plotnikov,Tiltan Gilat,Ben Benhemo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AI原生资产智能框架，通过建模层（资产、身份、关系、攻击向量、爆炸半径）和评分层（内在暴露度+上下文重要性）将异构安全数据转化为结构化资产优先级评分。在131,625个资源、15个供应商、178种资产类型的生产环境中验证，结果表明该框架能实现稳定、可解释的资产优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全助手仍是被动的：用户必须提出正确的问题并解释零散发现，这在企业环境中无法扩展。重复AI查询可能产生不稳定的优先级排序，缺乏比较资产的结构化基础。

Method: 提出AI原生资产智能框架，包含两个核心层：(1) 建模层——表示资产、身份、关系、控制措施、攻击向量和爆炸半径模式；(2) 评分层——将碎片化信号转化为归一化资产重要性度量。评分系统将内在暴露度（基于错误配置和攻击向量证据）与上下文重要性（基于异常、爆炸半径、业务关键性和数据关键性）分离。AI上下文化优化严重性和业务/数据分类，确定性聚合保持一致性。

Result: 在包含131,625个资源、15个供应商、178种资产类型的生产快照上评估。敏感度分析和消融实验表明：严重性映射控制发现敏感性，AI严重性调整优化优先级排序，攻击向量评分响应该有的可利用性证据，上下文调制根据业务或数据重要性选择性修改暴露资源。

Conclusion: AI原生资产智能可作为稳定优先级排序和主动安全态势推理的基础。

Abstract: Modern security environments generate fragmented signals across cloud resources, identities, configurations, and third-party security tools. Although AI-native security assistants improve access to this data, they remain largely reactive: users must ask the right questions and interpret disconnected findings. This does not scale in enterprise environments, where signal importance depends on exposure, exploitability, dependencies, and business context. Repeated AI queries may therefore produce unstable prioritization without a structured basis for comparing assets. This paper introduces AI-native asset intelligence, a framework that transforms heterogeneous security data into a structured intelligence layer for consistent, contextual, and proactive asset-level reasoning. The framework combines a modeling layer, representing assets, identities, relationships, controls, attack vectors, and blast-radius patterns, with a scoring layer that converts fragmented signals into a normalized measure of asset importance. The scoring system separates intrinsic exposure, based on misconfigurations and attack-vector evidence, from contextual importance, based on anomaly, blast radius, business criticality, and data criticality. AI contextualization refines severity and business/data classifications, while deterministic aggregation preserves consistency. We evaluate the scoring system on a production snapshot with 131,625 resources across 15 vendors and 178 asset types. Sensitivity analyses and ablations show that severity mappings control finding sensitivity, AI severity adjustment refines prioritization, attack-vector scoring responds to rare exploitability evidence, and contextual modulation selectively modifies exposed resources based on business or data importance. The results support AI-native asset intelligence as a foundation for stable prioritization and proactive security-posture reasoning.

</details>


### [272] [Smart Contract Security Beyond Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09124)
*Tamer Abdelaziz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文围绕智能合约安全的四个前沿方向（基于基础模型的语义推理、自动修复、对抗鲁棒性、实时利用检测）构建了一个顶点研究框架，旨在帮助学生设计技术扎实、可衡量且符合当代研究趋势的顶点项目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约安全研究已从简单的漏洞检测扩展到更广泛的议程，需要系统化的教育框架来引导学生开展技术扎实、可衡量的顶点研究项目。

Method: 论文构建了一个四个方向的研究叙事：1) 基于基础模型的智能合约语义与漏洞推理；2) 带形式化保证的自动修复；3) 对抗学习用于鲁棒的恶意合约与交易检测；4) 区块链规模的实时交易级利用检测。并将这些方向与两项前沿研究（诊断分析[5]和实时检测系统[6]）相连接。

Result: 提出了一个整合四个前沿方向与两项代表性研究的框架，该框架在技术基础、经验可测量性和当代研究对齐方面支持顶点项目设计。

Conclusion: 本文提供的框架有助于学生制定在技术基础、经验可测量性和当代研究对齐方面都具备优势的顶点项目。

Abstract: Smart contract security has progressed from vulnerability detection toward a broader research agenda that includes semantic reasoning, automated repair, adversarial robustness, and real-time exploit detection. This paper develops a capstone-oriented research narrative around four directions: foundation-model-based smart contract semantics and vulnerability reasoning [1], automated smart contract repair with formal guarantees [2], adversarial learning for robust malicious contract and transaction detection [3], and real-time transaction-level exploit detection at blockchain scale [4]. We connect these directions to two recent studies that characterize the current frontier: a diagnostic analysis of where smart contract security analyzers fall short [5] and a scalable real-time system for malicious Ethereum transaction detection [6]. The resulting framework is intended to help students formulate capstone projects that are technically grounded, empirically measurable, and aligned with contemporary smart contract security research.

</details>


### [273] [Removing the Watermark Is Not Enough: Forensic Stealth in Generative-AI Watermark Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09203)
*Yevin Nikhel Goonatilake,Giuseppe Ateniese*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 当前AI图像水印移除攻击虽然能骗过水印检测器，但会留下可被法医检测器识别的痕迹（在1%假阳性率下真阳性率超98%），无法实现与干净图像的法医不可区分性。研究揭示水印移除存在"移除成功-图像质量-法医隐蔽性"的三难困境，现有基准评测遗漏了法医隐蔽性这一关键维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印移除攻击仅关注能否绕过水印检测器，忽略了移除后输出图像是否在法医学层面与干净图像不可区分。作者认为，真正成功的水印移除必须同时满足三个条件：水印规避、效用保持和法医不可区分性。

Method: 对六种最先进的水印移除攻击（涵盖四类攻击家族）进行系统性评估，使用独立的法医检测器区分移除处理后的输出与干净图像。以UnMarker (IEEE S&P 2025)为详细案例，分析其输出中的可检测信号特征。

Result: 在1%假阳性率下，法医检测器对移除处理输出的真阳性率超过98%。UnMarker的输出表现出特征性的两区域光谱变形，该信号在常见后处理下持续存在。

Conclusion: 现有水印移除基准评测不完整，仅奖励水印规避和效用保持，遗漏了法医隐蔽性。一个可行的水印移除攻击必须同时满足三个条件：水印规避、效用保持和与干净图像的法医不可区分性。

Abstract: Watermarks for AI-generated images are meant to support downstream decisions about provenance, manipulation, and trust. In the settings that motivate watermark removal, therefore, success means more than causing the watermark test to fail. A successful remover must also preserve the utility of the image and make the output forensically indistinguishable from clean content, so that defeating the verifier restores deniability rather than merely replacing one detection signal with another. We show that current watermark removal attacks fail this stronger objective. Across six state-of-the-art removers spanning four attack families, independent forensic detectors distinguish removal-processed outputs from clean images at over 98% true-positive rate under a 1% false-positive budget. Thus, current removers often replace the watermark with a different detectable signal. Using UnMarker (IEEE S&P 2025) as a detailed case study, we show that this signal persists under common post-processing, exhibits a characteristic two-regime spectral deformation, and yields a three-way tension among removal success, image quality, and forensic stealth. These results show that existing removal benchmarks are incomplete: they reward verifier evasion and utility preservation while omitting forensic stealth. A workable watermark remover must satisfy all three conditions at once: watermark evasion, utility preservation, and forensic indistinguishability from clean content.

</details>


### [274] [Privacy-Preserving Distributed Learning in IoT Systems: A Unified Threat Model and Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09232)
*John Cartmell,Alexander Williams*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对物联网环境下分布式学习的隐私保护技术进行了结构化分析，提出了统一威胁模型和评估框架，系统比较了差分隐私、同态加密、安全多方计算、分布式选择性随机梯度下降和布隆过滤器等方法，揭示了隐私强度与系统效率之间的根本权衡，并指出布隆过滤器编码作为一种轻量级隐私保护方案具有低计算和通信开销的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究孤立评估隐私保护方法，缺乏统一的比较框架来在 realistic attack models 和 IoT 资源约束下进行有效性对比。

Method: 提出了统一威胁模型（涵盖模型反演、成员推断、梯度泄露和通信攻击），构建了同时评估隐私鲁棒性和系统效率（计算、内存、通信开销）的评估框架，并基于该框架对比分析了差分隐私、同态加密、安全多方计算、分布式选择性随机梯度下降和布隆过滤器等方法。

Result: 揭示了隐私强度与系统效率之间的根本权衡；布隆过滤器编码通过碰撞诱导的模糊性提供了轻量级隐私保护，同时保持了较低的计算和通信开销。

Conclusion: 本文为物联网分布式学习系统的隐私保护设计选择提供了统一的视角。

Abstract: The increasing deployment of Internet-of-Things (IoT) devices has accelerated the use of distributed learning frameworks, where data remains local while model updates are shared across decentralized systems. Although this reduces centralized data collection, it introduces privacy risks through the exchange of gradients, model parameters, and intermediate representations. A variety of privacy-preserving techniques have been proposed to address these risks, including differential privacy, cryptographic methods, and lightweight system-level approaches. However, existing surveys often evaluate these methods in isolation and lack a unified framework for comparing their effectiveness under realistic attack models and IoT resource constraints.
  This paper presents a structured analysis of privacy-preserving techniques for distributed learning in IoT environments. A unified threat model is introduced that captures model inversion, membership inference, gradient leakage, and communication-based attacks. Building on this model, an evaluation framework is developed to compare methods in terms of both privacy robustness and system-level efficiency, including computational, memory, and communication overhead.
  Using this framework, representative approaches including differential privacy, homomorphic encryption, secure multi-party computation, distributed selective stochastic gradient descent, and Bloom Filter-based methods are analyzed. The results highlight a fundamental trade-off between privacy strength and system efficiency. In particular, Bloom Filter-based encodings are shown to provide lightweight privacy through collision-induced ambiguity while maintaining low computational and communication overhead. The paper provides a unified perspective on privacy-preserving design choices for distributed learning in IoT systems.

</details>


### [275] [Enforcing Attestable Workflows across Untrusted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09297)
*Hung Dang,Tue Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为\codename的架构，通过分离TCB设计（控制平面使用MAKE，数据平面使用eBPF）实现跨联邦域的机密高性能计算，在提供安全加密路由的同时仅带来微小的性能开销（每包6μs执行成本，分布式管线仅6.1%的性能损失）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机密计算框架要么依赖高开销的用户态库操作系统，要么假设单主机执行，无法在高性能计算场景下高效地编排跨联邦域的机密工作负载。

Method: 采用分体式TCB设计：硬件隔离的控制平面执行MAKE协议，将密钥释放锚定到硬件测量值；内核中运行的eBPF数据平面执行加密路由策略，实现接近原生的转发性能。

Result: 稳态执行成本每包6μs，绝对延迟开销13-15μs；分布式管线相比明文基线仅6.1%的执行惩罚，而用户态方案为62%；100节点集群初始化时间低于1.5秒。

Conclusion: \codename提供了一种高效的机密互连方案，适用于长期运行的工作负载，在安全性与性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Confidential high-performance computing orchestrates workloads across federated domains, yet existing frameworks rely on high-overhead user-space library operating systems or assume single-host execution. We propose \codename, an architecture federating Trusted Execution Environments via a split Trusted Computing Base (TCB) design. It couples a hardware-isolated Control Plane executing Mutually Attested Key Exchange (\make) with a measured guest-resident extended Berkeley Packet Filter (eBPF) Data Plane. By anchoring cryptographic key release to hardware measurements and executing enforcement in the kernel, \codename\ achieves native-speed encrypted routing. Empirical evaluation demonstrates a steady-state enforcement cost of $6\,μ$s per packet, imposing a $13$--$15\,μ$s absolute latency overhead. On distributed pipelines, \codename\ incurs just a $6.1\%$ execution penalty over plaintext baselines, bypassing the $62\%$ penalty of user-space counterparts. The system initializes a 100-node cluster in under 1.5 seconds, providing an efficient confidential interconnect for long-running workflows.

</details>


### [276] [BadDLM: Backdooring Diffusion Language Models with Diverse Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09397)
*Shengfang Zhai,Xiaoyang Ji,Yuling Shi,Haoran Gao,Fanyu Meng,Yan Zeng,Yuejian Fang,Yinpeng Dong,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出BadDLM统一框架研究扩散语言模型的后门攻击，通过触发感知训练目标利用前向掩码过程植入后门，在概念注入、语义属性操控、对齐绕过和代码载荷注入等不同目标上均有效，且能规避现有防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）作为自回归（AR）语言模型的替代范式，其安全性（尤其是后门攻击的脆弱性）尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出BadDLM框架，引入触发感知训练目标，强调中毒样本中与目标相关的位置，理论上证明该目标等价于在诱导的前向掩码分布下训练，利用前向掩码过程植入后门。

Result: 在主流开源DLM上，BadDLM在概念注入、语义属性操控、对齐绕过和代码载荷注入等多个目标层面实现强攻击效果，同时保持良性效用，并能规避针对AR后门的防御。

Conclusion: 暴露了扩散语言生成中的新型安全风险，呼吁针对DLM去噪动态特性设计防御机制。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) have recently emerged as an alternative modeling paradigm to autoregressive (AR) language models, enabling parallel generation and bidirectional context modeling. Yet their security implications, particularly their vulnerability to backdoor attacks, remain underexplored. We propose BadDLM, a unified framework for studying backdoor attacks against DLMs with diverse targets. We introduce a trigger-aware training objective that emphasizes target-relevant positions in poisoned samples, and theoretically prove that this objective is equivalent to training under an induced forward masking distribution. Unlike backdoors in autoregressive models, which typically manipulate next-token prediction, this characterization indicates that BadDLM can implant backdoors by exploiting the forward masking process. We instantiate BadDLM across different target levels: concept injection (BadDLM_Concept), semantic attribute steering (BadDLM_Attribute), alignment bypass (BadDLM_Align), and code payload injection (BadDLM_Payload). Experiments on mainstream open-source DLMs show that BadDLM achieves strong attack effectiveness across diverse targets while largely preserving benign utility, and remains effective against defenses designed for AR backdoors. Our findings expose a new class of security risks in diffusion-based language generation and call for defenses tailored to DLM denoising dynamics.

</details>


### [277] [Permit: Permission-Aware Representation Intervention for Controlled Generation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09480)
*Pengcheng Sun,Lan Zhang,Zhaopeng Zhang,Jiewei Lai,Chen Tang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Permit框架，通过干预隐藏状态实现细粒度权限控制，在保持模型冻结的同时仅用极少量参数实现超过18%的F1提升且近乎零信息泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有访问控制仅限制信息是否可被模型访问，但无法约束模型在生成时如何使用这些信息，敏感内容进入上下文后输出仍可能超出用户授权范围。

Method: 两步法：1）通过不同权限条件下的激活差异识别权限敏感子空间；2）在该子空间内进行轻量级干预（基于偏移或门控两种实现），仅需少量权限专用参数，模型主干保持冻结。

Result: 在多个权限设置下优于现有最优方法，信息泄露近乎为零，F1分数提升超过18%，可训练参数减少超过98%。

Conclusion: 通过挖掘隐藏状态中权限条件的可分离几何结构，在冻结主干的条件下实现高效精准的细粒度权限控制，为安全可控的LLM部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in enterprise settings where they handle sensitive documents and user context, raising acute concerns over security and controllability. Conventional access control regulates whether information is accessible to the model, yet leaves how the model uses that information at generation time largely unconstrained: once sensitive content enters the context, outputs may still drift beyond a user's authorized scope. We present Permit, a novel permission-aware representation intervention framework that closes this gap by enforcing fine-grained control directly on the model's hidden states. Through exploratory analysis, we find that permission conditions induce hidden-state shifts that are (i) separable across permissions and (ii) concentrated in a small set of dominant directions. Permit exploits this geometry in two stages: it first identifies a permission-sensitive subspace from activation differences across permission conditions, and then performs lightweight interventions within this subspace to steer generation, with two concrete instantiations (offset-based and gated). Both operate atop a frozen backbone with only a handful of permission-specific parameters, achieving precise control with minimal overhead. Experimental results demonstrate that Permit performs better than the state-of-the-art method across multiple permission settings while driving information leakage to near zero, achieving over 18% F1-score improvement with >98% fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [278] [Position: AI Security Policy Should Target Systems, Not Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09504)
*Michael A. Riegler,Inga Strümke*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出swarm-attack开源框架，通过多个轻量级LLM智能体协同实现前沿模型越狱和软件漏洞挖掘。实验一：5个1.2B参数模型对GPT-4o实现45.8%有效伤害率(49个严重违规)，对Claude Sonnet-4为0%有效伤害率(40%技术成功率)。实验二：相同模型在4分钟内恢复9/9个CWE漏洞(100%召回率)，无脚手架组件时恢复0/9。结论：限制级能力可通过低成本系统脚手架在消费级硬件上零成本复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证通过多个轻量级开源LLM智能体协同，能否在零成本条件下复现原本需要大规模封闭模型才能实现的安全绕过和漏洞发现能力，挑战前沿模型安全护栏的有效性。

Method: 构建swarm-attack框架：多智能体通过共享内存协同、并行探索和进化优化进行对抗性测试。实验一：5个1.2B参数模型实例对GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet-4进行225次越狱攻击。实验二：相同模型对含9个植入CWE漏洞的C应用进行源代码分析和二进制模糊测试。

Result: 实验一：对GPT-4o有效伤害率45.8%(49个严重违规)，对Claude Sonnet-4有效伤害率0%(技术成功率40%)。实验二：启用脚手架组件时4分钟内恢复9/9漏洞(100%召回率)；禁用后通过崩溃验证恢复0/9，通过引用恢复2/9。

Conclusion: 限制级安全绕过和漏洞发现能力可在消费级硬件上以零成本复现，关键使能因素是系统脚手架本身，它弥补了小模型个体有限的推理能力。

Abstract: We present swarm-attack, an open-source adversarial testing framework in which multiple lightweight LLM agents coordinate through shared memory, parallel exploration, and evolutionary optimization. Together, our results demonstrate that both safety bypass of frontier models and software vulnerability discovery, i.e., the capability class that motivated restricted release of Anthropic's Mythos Preview, are achievable at effectively zero cost using commodity hardware and openly available models. We report two experiments. In the first, five instances of a 1.2 billion parameter model conducted 225 jailbreak attacks each against GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet~4. Against GPT-4o, the swarm achieved an Effective Harm Rate of 45.8%, producing 49 critical-severity breaches; against Claude Sonnet-4, the Effective Harm Rate was 0% despite a 40% technical success rate. In the second experiment, the same models performed combined source code analysis and binary fuzzing against a vulnerable C application with 9 planted CWEs. With a hand-crafted exploit seed corpus, regex pattern detection, and AddressSanitizer-based crash classification, the pipeline recovers 9 of 9 vulnerabilities (100% recall) in approximately four minutes on a consumer MacBook. With those scaffold components disabled, the same model recovers 0 of 9 by crash verification and 2 of 9 by citation. The capability class that motivated restricted release of Anthropic's Mythos Preview is therefore reproducible at effectively zero cost; the important enabler is the system scaffold itself, which compensates for the limited reasoning capacity of small individual models.

</details>


### [279] [MemPrivacy: Privacy-Preserving Personalized Memory Management for Edge-Cloud Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09530)
*Yining Chen,Jihao Zhao,Bo Tang,Haofen Wang,Feiyu Xiong,Zhiyu Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MemPrivacy提出了一种面向边缘-云环境的智能体隐私保护框架，通过边缘端识别隐私敏感词、用类型占位符替换、云端处理、本地恢复的机制，在保护隐私的同时最小化记忆系统效用损失（<1.6%），并构建了包含200用户、5.2万隐私实例的评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘-云环境中LLM智能体的个性化记忆管理会暴露用户敏感信息，现有基于掩码的隐私保护方法会去除任务相关语义，导致记忆效用和个性化质量下降。

Method: 在边缘设备上识别隐私敏感片段，将其替换为语义结构化的类型占位符后上传至云端处理，云端返回结果后在本地恢复原始值。同时构建了MemPrivacy-Bench数据集和四级隐私分类体系，支持可配置的保护策略。

Result: 在隐私信息提取上显著超越GPT-5.2和Gemini-3.1-Pro等通用模型；在多个记忆系统中效用损失控制在1.6%以内，优于基线掩码策略；同时降低了推理延迟。

Conclusion: MemPrivacy在隐私保护与个性化记忆效用之间实现了有效平衡，为边缘-云智能体提供了安全、实用、用户透明的部署方案。

Abstract: As LLM-powered agents are increasingly deployed in edge-cloud environments, personalized memory has become a key enabler of long-term adaptation and user-centric interaction. However, cloud-assisted memory management exposes sensitive user information, while existing privacy protection methods typically rely on aggressive masking that removes task-relevant semantics and consequently degrades memory utility and personalization quality. To address this challenge, We propose MemPrivacy, which identifies privacy-sensitive spans on edge devices, replaces them with semantically structured type-aware placeholders for cloud-side memory processing, and restores the original values locally when needed. By decoupling privacy protection from semantic destruction, MemPrivacy minimizes sensitive data exposure while retaining the information required for effective memory formation and retrieval. We also construct MemPrivacy-Bench for systematic evaluation, a dataset covering 200 users and over 52k privacy instances, and introduce a four-level privacy taxonomy for configurable protection policies. Experiments show that MemPrivacy achieves strong performance in privacy information extraction, substantially surpassing strong general-purpose models such as GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3.1-Pro, while also reducing inference latency. Across multiple widely used memory systems, MemPrivacy limits utility loss to within 1.6%, outperforming baseline masking strategies. Overall, MemPrivacy offers an effective balance between privacy protection and personalized memory utility for edge-cloud agents, enabling secure, practical, and user-transparent deployment.

</details>


### [280] [On the Generation and Mitigation of Harmful Geometry in Image-to-3D Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09606)
*Yule Liu,Yilong Yang,Jiale Teng,Hanze Jia,Zeren Luo,Jingyi Zheng,Zifan Peng,Ke Li,Yifan Liao,Zhen Sun,Jiaheng Wei,Yang Liu,Zhuo Ma,Xinlei He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统测量了image-to-3D模型生成有害几何体的风险，发现现有模型能有效重构有害几何体，但仅有不到0.3%触发商业审核标志。现有防护措施存在明显弱点，堆叠防御可将有害保留率降至<1%，但需付出11%的误报成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: image-to-3D模型的重建能力可能被滥用于生成有害几何体（如可通过3D打印制造现实危险），但当前对该风险缺乏系统理解，不清楚模型生成这类几何体的能力以及现有防护措施的有效性。

Method: 系统测量方法：定义三类有害几何体（直接物理危害、危险模板/组件、欺骗性复制品），在原始、退化、视角偏移和语义伪装输入下评估开源和商业模型，采用几何有效性、多视角VLM语义评分、人工验证和物理制造等多维度指标。

Result: 当前image-to-3D模型能有效重建有害几何体，但仅有不到0.3%触发商业审核标志；现有防护措施（输入审核、模型级良性对齐、输出级过滤）各自存在明显弱点；堆叠防御可将有害保留率降至<1%，但误报率达11%。

Conclusion: 现有image-to-3D系统存在严重安全风险，需要更好的几何感知防护措施来进行审核。

Abstract: Recent advances in image-to-3D models have significantly improved the fidelity and accessibility of 3D content creation. Such a powerful reconstruction capability that enables creative design can also be misused by the adversary to generate harmful geometries, which can be further fabricated via 3D printers and pose real-world risks. However, such risks are largely underexplored: it remains unclear how well current image-to-3D models can produce these harmful geometries, and whether existing safeguards can reliably prevent such generation.
  To fill this gap, we conduct a systematic measurement study of harmful geometry generation and mitigation. We first describe this risk through three kinds of unsafe categories: direct-use physical hazards, risky templates or components, and deceptive replicas. Each category is instantiated with representative objects. We evaluate both open-source and commercial image-to-3D models under original, degraded, viewpoint-shifted, and semantically camouflaged inputs. We consider different evaluation metrics, including geometric validity, multi-view VLM-based semantic scoring, targeted human validation, and controlled physical fabrication. The results reveal a concerning reality that current image-to-3D models can effectively reconstruct the harmful geometries, while fewer than 0.3% of such geometries trigger commercial moderation flags.
  As a first step toward mitigation, we evaluate three representative safeguard families, including input moderation, model-level benign alignment, and output-level filtering. We find that existing safeguards have distinct weaknesses. We further develop a stacked defense that can reduce harmful retention to <1%, but still at 11% overall false-positive cost. Taken together, our findings demonstrate that the risk in current system and encourage better geometry-aware safeguards for moderation.

</details>


### [281] ["Training robust watermarking model may hurt authentication!'' Exploring and Mitigating the Identity Leakage in Robust Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09646)
*Xinyu Zhang,Ziping Dong,Qingyu Liu,Yuan Hong,Zhongjie Ba,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: W-IR是首个同时实现身份保护与鲁棒性的图像水印框架，通过新颖的随机平滑技术提供像素级和坐标级双重变换空间的认证鲁棒性，并利用残差信息损失策略最小化残差与水印图像间的互信息，有效缓解身份泄露问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展凸显了图像所有权识别和版权保护的需求，但现有后处理图像水印方法易受对抗攻击（操纵水印图像导致解码失败）和身份泄露攻击（伪造水印图像），尤其经验和认证鲁棒水印方法中身份泄露威胁更为严重。

Method: 提出W-IR框架：1）引入新型随机平滑技术实现认证鲁棒性，在像素级和坐标级两个变换空间提供鲁棒保证；2）提出基于残差信息损失的策略，通过最小化残差与水印图像间的互信息来缓解身份泄露。

Result: W-IR在鲁棒性和身份泄露缓解之间取得了优越平衡，实现了高认证准确率同时有效降低身份泄露。

Conclusion: W-IR是首个同时兼顾身份保护与鲁棒性的图像水印框架，为图像水印领域提供了新的安全范式。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative AI has underscored the critical need for identifying image ownership and protecting copyrights. This makes post-processing image watermarking an essential tool -- it involves embedding a specific watermark message into an image, with successful verification if a similar message can be decoded from the watermarked image. However, this method is susceptible to both adversarial attacks that manipulate the watermarked image to yield an unverified message upon decoding, and the proposed identity leakage-related attacks (e.g., forging watermarked images). The threat of identity leakage is particularly exacerbated in both empirical and certified robust watermarking methods. To defend against the aforementioned attacks, we propose W-IR, the first image watermarking framework that simultaneously incorporates identity protection and robustness. To enhance model robustness, we introduce a novel randomized smoothing technique as part of a robust watermarking, that offers certified robustness against perturbations across two distinct transformation spaces: pixel-level and coordinate-level. Moreover, to further mitigate identity leakage, we propose a new strategy based on residual information loss, aimed at minimizing the mutual information between the residual and watermarked images. Our work strikes a superior balance between robustness and identity leakage mitigation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our W-IR framework achieves high certified accuracy for authenticity while effectively reducing identity leakage. \footnote{The code is available at https://github.com/holdrain/W-I-R.}

</details>


### [282] [FreeMOCA: Memory-Free Continual Learning for Malicious Code Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09664)
*Zahra Asadi,Haeseung Jeon,Sohyun Han,Md Mahmuduzzaman Kamol,Se Eun Oh,Mohammad Saidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FreeMOCA提出了一种基于参数空间自适应层间插值的持续学习框架，用于恶意代码分析，在不使用重放的情况下有效缓解灾难性遗忘，在EMBER和AZ基准测试中分别提升准确率最高达42%和37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每年超过2亿新恶意软件样本被发现，防病毒系统需要持续适应不断演变的威胁环境。仅在新样本上重新训练会导致灾难性遗忘和可利用盲区，而在整个数据集上重新训练则计算成本过高。

Method: 提出FreeMOCA框架，通过连续任务更新之间的自适应层间插值（adaptive layer-wise interpolation）来保留先前知识，利用热启动任务最优值在参数空间中由低损失路径连接的特性。

Result: 在类增量（Class-IL）和域增量（Domain-IL）设置下的大规模Windows（EMBER）和Android（AZ）恶意软件基准测试中，FreeMOCA在Class-IL上优于11个基线方法，显著减少遗忘，在EMBER和AZ上准确率分别提升高达42%和37%。

Conclusion: 参数空间中的热启动插值为持续恶意软件检测提供了一种可扩展且有效的替代重放的方法。

Abstract: As over 200 million new malware samples are identified each year, antivirus systems must continuously adapt to the evolving threat landscape. However, retraining solely on new samples leads to catastrophic forgetting and exploitable blind spots, while retraining on the entire dataset incurs substantial computational cost. We propose FreeMOCA, a memory- and compute-efficient continual learning framework for malicious code analysis that preserves prior knowledge via adaptive layer-wise interpolation between consecutive task updates, leveraging the fact that warm-started task optima are connected by low-loss paths in parameter space.
  We evaluate FreeMOCA in both class-incremental (Class-IL) and domain-incremental (Domain-IL) settings on large-scale Windows (EMBER) and Android (AZ) malware benchmarks. FreeMOCA achieves substantial gains in Class-IL, outperforming 11 baselines on both EMBER and AZ benchmarks. It also significantly reduces forgetting, achieving the best retention across baselines, and improving accuracy by up to 42% and 37% on EMBER and AZ, respectively. These results demonstrate that warm-started interpolation in parameter space provides a scalable and effective alternative to replay for continual malware detection.
  Code is available at: https://github.com/IQSeC-Lab/FreeMOCA.

</details>


### [283] [MonitoringBench: Semi-Automated Red-Teaming for Agent Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09684)
*Monika Jotautaitė,Maria Angelica Martinez,Ollie Matthews,Tyler Tracy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对编码智能体监控器的红队测试方法，通过构建攻击分类法、分解攻击构建流程以及半自动化管线，生成了包含2,644条攻击轨迹的MonitoringBench基准。实验表明，当前监控器的捕获率在高强度攻击下显著下降（如Opus-4.5从94.9%降至60.3%），揭示了监控器在面对多样化攻击时的脆弱性，尤其是容易被说服性攻击欺骗或无法合理校准可疑度评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队测试存在三大挑战：攻击生成模式坍塌（覆盖不足）、构思-执行差距（前沿LLM无法同时完成攻击构思与执行）、人工成本高昂。这些问题导致当前实践低估了攻击难度，高估了监控器性能。

Method: 提出三阶段攻击构建方法：策略生成、执行与事后轨迹精炼；构建结构化的攻击分类法以扩大覆盖范围；设计半自动化红队测试管线，在BashArena环境中生成攻击轨迹，形成MonitoringBench基准。

Result: Opus-4.5监控器捕获率从94.9%（基础攻击）降至60.3%（精炼攻击）；多个中端监控器性能下降更大；针对三个开发监控器优化的攻击可泛化至十个未见监控器；发现前沿监控器虽能检测可疑行为，但易受说服性攻击影响且可疑度评分校准不佳。

Conclusion: MonitoringBench为当前工具使用监控器提供了静态基准，同时提供可复用的方法论以随智能体与监控器进步而更新评估。识别了监控器改进的可处理路径，尤其是对抗说服性攻击和提升评分校准能力。

Abstract: We introduce a red-teaming methodology that exposes harder-to-catch attacks for coding-agent monitors, suggesting that current practices may under-elicit attacks and overstate monitor performance. We identify three challenges with current red-teaming. First, mode collapse in attack generation, which we reduce with a novel attack taxonomy for broader coverage. Second, a conceive-execute gap: frontier LLMs can propose strong attack ideas or execute them, but not all at once. We mitigate this by decomposing attack construction into strategy generation, execution, and post-hoc trajectory refinement. Third, manual elicitation is costly to scale, which we address with our semi-automated red-teaming pipeline. Applied to BashArena, an AI control setting for tool-using coding agents, this pipeline produces MonitoringBench, a benchmark of 2,644 attack trajectories for evaluating monitor capabilities and failure modes. Our pipeline produces more diverse and stronger attacks: Opus-4.5 monitor's catch rate falls from 94.9\% on elicited-only Opus attacks to 60.3\% on our best refined attacks, with larger drops for several mid-tier monitors. Attacks optimized against three development monitors generalize to ten held-out monitors, with catch rates generally increasing with monitor capability. Using this benchmark, we provide a snapshot of the current monitor capabilities and find that frontier monitors often detect suspicious actions but fall for persuasion or fail to calibrate suspiciousness scores appropriately, suggesting tractable paths for improvement. MonitoringBench provides both a static benchmark for current tool-use monitors and a reusable methodology for refreshing these evaluations as agents and monitors improve.

</details>


### [284] [Oracle Poisoning: Corrupting Knowledge Graphs to Weaponise AI Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09822)
*Ben Kereopa-Yorke,Guillermo Diaz,Holly Wright,Reagan Johnston,Ron F. Del Rosario,Timothy Lynar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"Oracle Poisoning"（预言中毒）攻击，指攻击者污染AI智能体在运行时通过工具协议查询的结构化知识图谱，使模型在正确推理下得出错误结论。在4200万节点的生产级代码知识图谱上实验表明，中等攻击复杂度下所有9个模型100%信任被污染数据（270次有效试验中269次接受伪造安全声明），而只读访问控制可消除直接变异向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注提示注入攻击，但忽略了攻击者通过污染AI智能体运行时查询的结构化知识图谱数据来操纵模型结论的可能性。需要系统性地定义和实证展示此类攻击。

Method: 在4200万节点的生产级代码知识图谱上设计6种攻击场景，使用9个模型（来自三家提供商，N=30）在真实SDK工具调用下进行实验，评估模型对污染数据的信任程度。设置攻击者复杂度梯度、对比定向查询与开放式提示、比较内联评估与真实智能体工具调用三种交付模式。

Result: 中等攻击复杂度下所有模型100%信任污染数据（270次有效试验中269次接受伪造声明）；开放式提示下信任度降至3-55%；内联评估产生假阴性（GPT-5.1内联0%信任但智能体工具调用下100%信任）；五种防御中仅只读访问控制有效消除直接变异向量。

Conclusion: Oracle Poisoning是一种独立于提示注入的新型攻击范式，影响所有测试模型。攻击存在离散的复杂度阈值（从0%到100%信任的突变点）。交付模式是首要混淆变量，内联评估不可靠。防御方案有限，此攻击可能泛化至整个知识图谱生态系统。

Abstract: We define Oracle Poisoning, an attack class in which an adversary corrupts a structured knowledge graph that AI agents query at runtime via tool-use protocols, causing incorrect conclusions through correct reasoning. Unlike prompt injection, Oracle Poisoning manipulates the data agents reason over, not their instructions. We demonstrate six attack scenarios against a production 42-million-node code knowledge graph, providing the first empirical demonstration of knowledge graph poisoning against a production-scale agentic system, distinct from CTI embedding poisoning. Primary evaluation uses real SDK tool-use across nine models from three providers (N=30 per model), where models autonomously invoke a graph query tool and reason from results. The result is unambiguous: every tested model trusts poisoned data at 100% at moderate attacker sophistication(L2), with 269 valid trials (of 270) accepting fabricated security claims under directed queries. Under open-ended prompts, trust drops to 3-55%, confirming prompt framing as a confound; we report both conditions. An attacker sophistication gradient reveals discrete break points, a minimum skill at which trust flips from 0% to 100%, reframing the attack as a question not of whether but of how much. A controlled delivery-mode comparison shows that inline evaluation produces false negatives: GPT-5.1 shows 0% trust inline but 100% under both simulated and real agentic tool-use, demonstrating that delivery mode is a first-order confound. We evaluate five defences; read-only access control eliminates the direct mutation vector, while the remaining four are partial and model-dependent. Analysis of four additional platforms suggests the attack may generalise across the knowledge-graph ecosystem.

</details>


### [285] [Nautilus Compass: Black-box Persona Drift Detection for Production LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09863)
*Chunxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Nautilus Compass 是一个黑盒人格漂移检测器与智能体记忆层，完全在提示文本层操作，通过余弦相似度与加权top-k均值聚合实现，无需索引时调用LLM提取事实。作为Claude Code插件等部署，漂移检测ROC AUC 0.83，LongMemEval-S 56.6%，成本仅$3.50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的LLM编码智能体在长时间会话中会出现人格漂移（遗忘约束、重复错误、虚构先前协议）。现有的白盒方法（如persona vectors）需要模型权重，无法应用于大多数用户使用的闭源API（Claude、GPT-4），因此需要一种黑盒方案。

Method: 完全在提示文本层操作：使用BGE-m3嵌入计算用户提示与行为锚文本之间的余弦相似度，通过加权top-k均值聚合进行漂移检测。索引时不调用LLM提取事实或构建图，直接嵌入原始对话文本。系统以Claude Code插件、MCP A2A服务器、CLI及REST API形式部署，并带有Merkle链审计日志。

Result: 漂移检测ROC AUC 0.83；LongMemEval-S v0.8 56.6%，EverMemBench-Dynamic 44.4%（n=500），超过EverMemBench四个已发布基线。端到端复现成本$3.50，比GPT-4o评判的堆栈便宜约14倍。LongMemEval-S 56.6% 比白盒领导者（90%+）低约30点，作者认为这是无提取设计的架构上限。

Conclusion: Nautilus Compass 是目前已知唯一在索引时不调用LLM的公开智能体记忆层系统，以极低成本实现了有效的黑盒人格漂移检测与记忆管理，代码与数据均MIT开源。

Abstract: Production LLM coding agents drift over long sessions: they forget user-specified constraints, slip into mistakes the user already flagged, and confabulate prior agreements. White-box approaches such as persona vectors require model weights and so cannot be applied to closed APIs (Claude, GPT-4) that most users actually interact with. We present Nautilus Compass, a black-box persona drift detector and agent memory layer for production coding agents. The method operates entirely at the prompt-text layer: cosine similarity between user prompts and behavioral anchor texts, aggregated by a weighted top-k mean using BGE-m3 embeddings. Compass is, to our knowledge, the only public agent memory layer (among Mem0, Letta, Cognee, Zep, MemOS, smrti verified May 2026) that does not call an LLM at index time to extract facts or build a graph; raw conversation text is embedded directly. The system ships as a Claude Code plugin, an MCP 2024-11-05 A2A server (Cursor, Cline, Hermes), a CLI, and a REST API on one daemon, with a Merkle-chained audit log for tamper-evident anchor updates. On a held-out test set built from real Claude Code session traces and labeled by an independent LLM judge, Compass reaches ROC AUC 0.83 for drift detection. The embedded retrieval pipeline scores 56.6% on LongMemEval-S v0.8 and 44.4% on EverMemBench-Dynamic (n=500), topping the four published EverMemBench Table 4 baselines. LongMemEval-S 56.6% is ~30 points below recent white-box leaders (90+%); we treat that as the architectural ceiling of the no-extraction design. End-to-end reproduction cost is $3.50 (~14x cheaper than GPT-4o-judged stacks). A paired cross-vendor behavior A/B accompanies these numbers as preliminary system-level evidence.
  Code, anchors, frozen test data, and audit-log tooling are MIT-licensed at github.com/chunxiaoxx/nautilus-compass.

</details>


### [286] [Deep Learning under Fractional-Order Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09890)
*Mohammad Partohaghighi,Roummel Marcia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FO-DP-SGD，一种在差分隐私随机梯度下降中引入分数阶记忆的机制扩展，通过在加噪前将当前裁剪梯度与历史输出的幂律加权聚合相结合，在保持标准结构的同时注入分数阶记忆，并在SVHN、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上取得了优于多种DP基线的隐私-效用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典DP-SGD仅使用当前步的裁剪梯度信息，忽略了历史梯度中可能蕴含的有用信号。现有方法缺乏一种机制层面的、能够引入长期记忆效应同时保持严格隐私保证的扩展。

Method: 在加高斯噪声之前，将当前裁剪梯度之和与有限窗口内、幂律加权的历史私有求和输出进行分数阶递归组合，形成分数阶记忆查询；保持sum-then-noise-then-divide的标准结构；在Poisson子采样/添加移除邻接关系下，当前步的有效ℓ2敏感度上限为βC，其中β∈(0,1]控制当前步贡献，噪声-敏感度比为σ/β，可使用标准的Rényi DP核算。

Result: 在SVHN、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上，FO-DP-SGD在测试准确率和隐私-效用权衡上均优于DP-SGD、DP-Adam、DP-IS、SA-DP-SGD、ADP-AdamW、DP-SAT、DP-Adam-AC等多种差分隐私基线方法。

Conclusion: FO-DP-SGD提供了一个研究私有优化中长期记忆效应的框架，通过分数阶、记忆窗口和混合系数三个参数调控当前步敏感度、信号保留和私有历史影响之间的权衡，为差分隐私学习中的记忆机制设计开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) is a standard approach to privacy-preserving learning based on per-example clipping, subsampling, Gaussian perturbation, and privacy accounting. Classical DP-SGD releases a noisy version of the current clipped subsampled gradient sum. We propose Fractional-Order Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (\textbf{FO-DP-SGD}), a mechanism-level extension that replaces this current-only query, before Gaussian noise is added, with a fractional recursive query combining the current clipped sum with a finite-window, power-law-weighted aggregation of previously released private sum-level outputs. This injects fractional memory into the release mechanism while preserving the standard \emph{sum-then-noise-then-divide} structure.
  Under add/remove adjacency with Poisson subsampling, the current-step sensitivity analysis shows that the only newly data-dependent term is the scaled current clipped sum. Hence, conditioned on the private history, the effective \(\ell_2\)-sensitivity is at most \(βC\), where \(C\) is the clipping threshold and \(β\in(0,1]\) controls the current-step contribution. Thus, FO-DP-SGD admits standard per-step Rényi differential privacy accounting via a Poisson-subsampled Gaussian mechanism with effective noise-to-sensitivity ratio \(σ/β\), and composes to yield overall \((\varepsilon,δ)\)-differential privacy guarantees.
  FO-DP-SGD provides a framework for studying long-memory effects in private optimization. The fractional order, memory window, and mixing coefficient govern the trade-off among current-step sensitivity, signal retention, and private-history influence. Experiments on SVHN, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 show improved test accuracy and privacy--utility performance over DP-SGD and private baselines including DP-Adam, DP-IS, SA-DP-SGD, ADP-AdamW, DP-SAT, and DP-Adam-AC.

</details>


### [287] [Towards LLM-Based Analysis of Virtualization-Obfuscated Code through Automated Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09961)
*Sangjun An,Hyeyeon Park,Yejin Son,Seoksu Lee,Eun-Sun Cho*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出通过结构分析而非完整语义分析来应对基于虚拟化的混淆二进制代码，将代码分解为语义连贯的单元并自动化标注，在LLM约束下实现大规模分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于虚拟化的混淆会产生体积极大且结构复杂的二进制程序，对基于LLM的分析构成挑战，主要问题在于输入大小限制和大规模标注数据的缺乏。

Method: 聚焦结构分析而非完整语义分析，将混淆二进制分解为最大语义连贯单元（适应LLM约束），并根据结构角色进行标注。实现了一个静态分析框架来自动化标注过程，支持大规模数据集的生成。

Result: 原型系统在真实世界的虚拟化混淆器上表现出色，证明了该方法在LLM约束下对混淆二进制进行结构分析的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过结构分解与自动化标注的策略，可以在LLM输入限制下有效分析复杂混淆二进制，解决了大规模标注数据获取难的问题。

Abstract: Virtualization-based obfuscation produces extremely large and structurally complex binaries, posing challenges for LLM-based analysis due to input size limits and the need for large-scale labeled data. We address this by focusing on structural rather than full semantic analysis. Obfuscated binaries are decomposed into the largest semantically coherent units that fit within LLM constraints and are labeled according to their structural roles. We implement a static analysis framework to automate labeling and enable large-scale dataset generation. Our prototype shows strong performance on real-world virtualization obfuscators.

</details>


### [288] [Hardness Amplification for (Sparse) LPN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10056)
*Divesh Aggarwal,Rishav Gupta,Li Zeyong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对学习带噪声奇偶性问题(LPN)及其稀疏变体，证明了新的难度放大结果：任何以概率ε求解LPN的算法都可以转化为以概率1-δ成功求解相关LPN分布的算法，实现了从"小部分实例"到"几乎所有实例"的自放大。该结论还扩展到了有限域上的LPN和稀疏LPN问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加强LPN及其稀疏变体平均情况难度的理论基础，通过难度放大定理表明如果LPN在某个小成功概率下是困难的，那么它在几乎全部实例上也是困难的，从而为基于LPN的密码学构造提供更坚实的安全基础。

Method: 基于Hirahara和Shimizu [HS23]引入的直积框架，构建实例分数放大定理：利用参数缩放（将噪声率η和秘密长度n均缩放1/k倍，其中k=Θ((1/δ)log(1/ε))），将低成功概率算法转化为高成功概率算法。

Result: 证明了LPN的难度自放大定理，并将该方法扩展到了有限域F_q上的LPN以及稀疏LPN（每个查询向量恰好有σ个非零条目）问题，建立了一类LPN型问题的难度自放大性质。

Conclusion: 这些结果为假设LPN及其稀疏变体的平均情况困难性提供了更坚实的理论基础，支持了相关密码学构造的安全性假设。

Abstract: We prove new hardness amplification results for Learning Parity with Noise ($\mathsf{LPN}$) and its sparse variants. In $\mathsf{LPN}_{η,n,m}$, the goal is to recover a secret $\vec s\in\mathbb{F}_2^n$ from $m$ noisy linear samples $(\vec a,b)$, where $\vec a\leftarrow \mathbb{F}_2^n$ is uniform and $b=\langle \vec a,\vec s\rangle + e$ with $e\leftarrow \mathrm{Ber}(η)$.
  Building on the direct-product framework introduced by Hirahara and Shimizu [HS23], we show an 'instance-fraction amplification' theorem: for any $\varepsilon,δ>0$, any algorithm that solves $\mathsf{LPN}_{η,n,m}$ with success probability $\varepsilon$ can be transformed into an algorithm that succeeds with probability $1-δ$ on a related \textsf{LPN} distribution with scaled parameters $\mathsf{LPN}_{η/k,\;n/k,\;m}$, where $ k=Θ\!\left(\frac{1}δ\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right). $ Equivalently, an algorithm that solves $\mathsf{LPN}$ on a 'small fraction of instances' can be converted into an algorithm that solves $\mathsf{LPN}$ on 'almost all instances', yielding a self-amplification for a wide range of parameters.
  We extend the same amplification approach to $\mathsf{LPN}$ over $\mathbb{F}_q$ and to Sparse-$\mathsf{LPN}$, where each query vector $\vec a$ has exactly $σ$ nonzero entries. Together, these results establish hardness self-amplification for a broad family of $\mathsf{LPN}$-type problems, strengthening the foundations for assuming the average-case hardness of $\mathsf{LPN}$ and its sparse variants.

</details>


### [289] [Agentic Fuzzing: Opportunities and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10074)
*Junyoung Park,Insu Yun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于智能体的模糊测试方法AFuzz，通过深度智能体直接分析历史漏洞的根因、假设新场景并验证假设，在V8、SpiderMonkey和JavaScriptCore引擎中发现了59个逻辑漏洞，获得35,000美元奖金和2个CVE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊测试和静态分析工具难以发现成熟代码库中的逻辑漏洞，因为这类漏洞需要多步推理且不会产生显著的执行反馈；LLM辅助方法虽有一定帮助，但仅将LLM作为辅助工具而非推理引擎。

Method: 提出agentic fuzzing方法：以历史漏洞为种子，智能体分析根因、假设代码库中可能存在相同根因的其他场景、生成并运行PoC代码验证。实现为AFuzz系统，包含四阶段智能体流水线、场景去重覆盖机制和基于DPP-MAP的调度器。

Result: 在V8引擎上运行约一个月，发现40个漏洞（含3个重复），获得35,000美元奖金和2个CVE；利用V8种子在SpiderMonkey和JavaScriptCore中又发现19个漏洞（含1个重复）。

Conclusion: Agentic fuzzing是发现逻辑漏洞的有前景方向，但尚处于早期阶段，存在若干未解决的开放问题。

Abstract: Fuzzers and static analyzers find many bugs but struggle with logic bugs in mature codebases. Triggering such a bug often requires multi-step reasoning that produces no distinctive execution feedback, and variants can appear across implementations too different for a single pattern to match. Recent LLM-assisted approaches help, but they use LLMs as auxiliaries rather than as the reasoning engine.
  We propose agentic fuzzing, a bug-finding approach seeded by historical bugs in which deep agents perform the reasoning directly. Given a reference bug, the agent analyzes its root cause, hypothesizes new scenarios elsewhere in the codebase that may share that cause, and verifies each hypothesis by generating and running proof-of-concept code. This lets the agent find variants that differ completely in trigger path or code structure from the reference.
  We identify three practical challenges in implementing agentic fuzzing: harness engineering, redundant investigations across seeds with similar root causes, and scheduling seeds in a large corpus. We address these in AFuzz through a four-stage agent pipeline, scenario coverage that deduplicates previously explored scenarios, and a DPP-MAP scheduler that orders seeds by diversity. We ran AFuzz on the V8 JavaScript engine for about one month, finding 40 bugs (including three duplicates), receiving a total $35,000 bounty, and being assigned two CVEs. AFuzz also found 19 bugs (including one duplicate) in SpiderMonkey and JavaScriptCore using the seeds from V8. However, agentic fuzzing is in its early stages with several remaining open problems we discuss in the paper. Still, we think it points to a promising direction for finding logic bugs.

</details>


### [290] [Usability as a Weapon: Attacking the Safety of LLM-Based Code Generation via Usability Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10133)
*Yue Li,Xiao Li,Hao Wu,Yue Zhang,Yechao Zhang,Yating Liu,Fengyuan Xu,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出UPAttack攻击方法，通过向LLM施加可用性压力（如新功能、性能优化、简洁性要求），诱导其在满足显式可用性目标的同时，无意识地放弃隐式安全约束（奖励黑客行为）。作者开发了自动化框架U-SPLOIT，在25个CWE×3个场景共75个种子场景中，对GPT-5.2-chat和Gemini-3-Flash-Preview等先进模型实现了高达98.1%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实开发中，安全需求通常隐式且信号弱，而可用性需求显式且信号强。这种不对称性导致LLM在编码时可能为了满足显式的可用性目标而牺牲隐式的安全约束，构成一种实际威胁。

Method: 提出U-SPLOIT自动化框架，包含三步：(i) 选择模型初始安全的任务；(ii) 通过三个向量（功能性、实现方式、权衡）合成可用性压力，识别不安全替代方案的可用性奖励；(iii) 利用现有测试用例和动态生成的利用载荷验证安全回归。

Result: 在25个CWE×3个场景共75个种子场景中，跨Python、C、JavaScript多种语言，对GPT-5.2-chat和Gemini-3-Flash-Preview等模型实现了最高98.1%的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 可用性压力是LLM安全编码的一个实际攻击面，现有模型对此类隐式安全约束丢失问题缺乏有效防御，需引起重视。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for automated software development, making their ability to preserve secure coding practices critical. In practice, however, many security requirements are implicit or underspecified, whereas usability requirements are explicit and high-signal. This asymmetry motivates our investigation of usability pressure as a practical attack surface: realistic usability-oriented requirements (e.g., new features, performance constraints, or simplicity demands) can cause coding LLMs to satisfy explicit usability goals while silently dropping implicit security constraints -- a form of reward hacking. We formalize this threat as UPAttack and propose U-SPLOIT, an automated framework to craft UPAttack that (i) selects tasks where a model is initially secure, (ii) synthesizes usability pressures by identifying usability rewards of insecure alternatives across three vectors (Functionality, Implementation, Trade-off), and (iii) verifies security regression via both existing test cases and dynamically generated exploit payloads. Across 75 seed scenarios (25 CWEs x 3 cases), spanning multiple languages (Python, C, and JavaScript), U-SPLOIT achieves attack success rates up to 98.1% on multiple state-of-the-art models (e.g., GPT-5.2-chat and Gemini-3-Flash-Preview).

</details>


### [291] [Key Encapsulation Mechanism-Based Integrated Encryption Scheme (KEM-IES)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10175)
*Abel C. H. Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于密钥封装机制的集成加密方案（KEM-IES），通过集成后量子密码（PQC）的KEM增强抗量子攻击能力，并引入NIST于2025年8月发布的Ascon算法提升计算效率、适配嵌入式系统。在树莓派4上的实验对比了ECIES与KEM-IES的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECIES虽被广泛采用，但量子计算技术对其安全性构成潜在威胁，现有方案缺乏抗量子攻击能力。

Method: 提出KEM-IES方案，采用基于PQC的KEM替代传统密钥交换机制，并集成Ascon算法优化效率；在树莓派4上实现并进行性能对比评估。

Result: 在树莓派4上实现了KEM-IES及其Ascon变体，并与ECIES进行了性能对比。

Conclusion: KEM-IES能增强抗量子攻击能力，结合Ascon算法可提升计算效率并适用于嵌入式系统。

Abstract: The Elliptic Curve Integrated Encryption Scheme (ECIES) is widely regarded as a practical method and has been adopted by multiple standards. However, the advancement of quantum computing technologies poses potential security risks to ECIES. Therefore, this study proposes a Key Encapsulation Mechanism-Based Integrated Encryption Scheme (KEM-IES), which enhances resistance to quantum attacks by incorporating a Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC)-based Key Encapsulation Mechanism (KEM). Furthermore, the study integrates the Ascon algorithm, released by the National Institute of Standards and Technology (NIST) in August 2025, to further improve computational efficiency and enable applicability to embedded systems. The proposed KEM-IES and its Ascon-based variant are implemented on a Raspberry Pi 4, and evaluations are conducted to compare the performance of ECIES and KEM-IES.

</details>


### [292] [When Prompts Become Payloads: A Framework for Mitigating SQL Injection Attacks in Large Language Model-Driven Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10176)
*Farzad Nourmohammadzadeh Motlagh,Mehrdad Hajizadeh,Mehryar Majd,Pejman Najafi,Feng Cheng,Christoph Meinel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种多层安全框架，用于检测和缓解LLM驱动的数据库应用中通过提示转SQL翻译过程放大的SQL注入攻击。该框架整合了前端提示净化、行为与语义异常检测以及签名匹配三层机制，在多样化攻击场景下实现了高检测率和低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言数据库接口虽然提升了可用性，但提示转SQL的翻译过程引入了新的安全风险，恶意用户可通过对抗性提示操纵模型生成不安全查询，放大了SQL注入漏洞。

Method: 提出多层安全框架，包含：(1)前端安全盾进行提示净化；(2)高级威胁检测模型用于行为与语义异常识别；(3)基于签名的控制层匹配已知攻击模式。框架在包含提示注入、混淆SQL载荷和上下文操纵攻击的对抗性基准数据集上评估。

Result: 在多样化攻击场景下，该方法实现了高检测准确率，同时保持低误报率，显著提升了LLM驱动数据库应用的安全性。

Conclusion: 该多层防御框架能够有效应对LLM-mediated SQL注入攻击，对提升基于自然语言接口的数据库系统的安全部署具有重要价值。

Abstract: Natural language interfaces to structured databases are becoming increasingly common, largely due to advances in large language models (LLMs) that enable users to query data using conversational input rather than formal query languages such as SQL. While this paradigm significantly improves usability and accessibility, it introduces new security risks, particularly the amplification of SQL injection vulnerabilities through the prompt-to-SQL translation process. Malicious users can exploit these mechanisms by crafting adversarial prompts that manipulate model behavior and generate unsafe queries. In this work, we propose a multi-layered security framework designed to detect and mitigate LLM-mediated SQL injection attacks. The framework integrates a front-end security shield for prompt sanitization, an advanced threat detection model for behavioral and semantic anomaly identification, and a signature-based control layer for known attack patterns. We evaluate the proposed framework under diverse and realistic attack scenarios, including prompt injection, obfuscated SQL payloads, and context-manipulation attacks. To ensure robustness, we generate and curate a comprehensive benchmark dataset of adversarial prompts and assess performance across a fine-tuned LLM configuration. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves high detection accuracy while maintaining low false-positive rates, significantly improving the secure deployment of LLM-powered database applications.

</details>


### [293] [Knowledge Poisoning Attacks on Medical Multi-Modal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10253)
*Peiru Yang,Haoran Zheng,Tong Ju,Shiting Wang,Wanchun Ni,Jiajun Liu,Shangguang Wang,Yongfeng Huang,Tao Qi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出M³Att框架，针对医学多模态RAG系统，在仅有有限分布知识假设下，通过向文本注入隐蔽错误信息并以视觉数据作为查询无关触发器，实现知识投毒攻击。实验表明该方法能在多个LLM和数据集上生成临床看似合理但实际错误的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学RAG系统面临知识投毒攻击风险，但已有研究大多假设攻击者掌握用户查询的先验知识，这在现实中难以成立。本文旨在设计一种更现实的攻击框架，仅需有限的数据库分布知识即可实施攻击。

Method: 首先提出统一框架，对视觉输入施加不可察觉的扰动以操控检索概率；其次利用医学诊断固有的歧义性设计隐蔽错误信息注入策略，使错误内容能绕过LLM的自我纠正机制，降低诊断准确性。

Result: 在5个LLM和多个数据集上的实验表明，M³Att能够持续生成临床看似合理但实际错误的输出。

Conclusion: M³Att揭示了医学多模态RAG系统在面对更具现实约束的攻击时的脆弱性，对系统可靠性提出了警示。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a widely adopted paradigm for enhancing LLMs in medical applications by incorporating expert multimodal knowledge during generation. However, the underlying retrieval databases may naturally contain, or be intentionally injected with, adversarial knowledge, which can perturb model outputs and undermine system reliability. To investigate this risk, prior studies have explored knowledge poisoning attacks in medical RAG systems. Nevertheless, most of them rely on the strong assumption that adversaries possess prior knowledge of user queries, which is unrealistic in deployments and substantially limits their practical applicability. In this paper, we propose M\textsuperscript{3}Att, a knowledge-poisoning framework designed for medical multimodal RAG systems, assuming only limited distribution knowledge of the underlying database. Our core idea is to inject covert misinformation into textual data while using paired visual data as a query-agnostic trigger to promote retrieval. We first propose a unified framework that introduces imperceptible perturbations to visual inputs to manipulate retrieval probabilities. Besides, due to the prior medical knowledge in LLMs, naively poisoned medical content with explicit factual errors can be corrected during generation. Thus, we leverage the inherent ambiguity of medical diagnosis and design a covert misinformation injection strategy that degrades diagnostic accuracy while evading model self-correction. Experiments on five LLMs and datasets demonstrate that M\textsuperscript{3}Att consistently produces clinically plausible yet incorrect generations. Codes: https://github.com/ypr17/M3Att.

</details>


### [294] [Mapping Partisan Fault Lines Within DAOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10316)
*Thomas Lloyd,Daire Ó Broin,Martin Harrigan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种通过分析链上投票行为检测DAO分裂前党派社区的方法，并以Nouns DAO为例验证了该方法能在分叉发生前数月识别出即将分叉的地址集群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAO在治理结构中可能出现党派社区，导致组织分裂（分叉）。现有方法缺乏在分叉发生前早期检测这些新兴社区的手段。

Method: 从治理智能合约提取投票事件，构建投票者矩阵编码参与模式，应用成对相异度分析量化地址间的意识形态分歧，通过多维尺度分析可视化关系，并使用k-means聚类结合轮廓系数优化识别党派社区。

Result: 在Nouns DAO的330个提案分析中，90%的分叉地址在最后44个提案中聚类在一起，而随机数据中仅为47%。

Conclusion: 通过链上治理分析可以检测和可视化党派社区，为组织分裂前的早期预警提供依据。

Abstract: Decentralised Autonomous Organisations (DAO) can fragment when partisan communities emerge within their governance structures, leading to organisational splits known as "forks". We present a method to detect these emerging communities by analysing on-chain voting behaviour before fragmentation occurs. Our approach extracts voting events from governance smart contracts, constructs voter matrices encoding participation patterns, and applies pairwise dissimilarity analysis to quantify ideological divergence between addresses. We visualise these relationships using multidimensional scaling and identify partisan communities through k-means clustering with silhouette score optimisation. Using Nouns DAO as a case study, a protocol that has experienced multiple documented forks, we demonstrate that addresses destined to fork cluster together months before actual fragmentation events. Our analysis of 330 proposals spanning from contract deployment to the first major fork shows that 90% of fork addresses cluster together in the final 44 proposals, compared to only 47% in randomised data. These results indicate that partisan communities can be detected and visualised through on-chain governance analysis, offering early warnings of emerging divisions before they cause organisational fragmentation.

</details>


### [295] [DRIFT: Drift-Resilient Invariant-Feature Transformer for DGA Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10436)
*Chaeyoung Lee,Chaeri Jung,Seonghoon Jeong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对DGA检测中的时间漂移问题，提出了一个基于Transformer的漂移鲁棒框架，通过混合分词策略和多任务自监督预训练学习不变表示，在9年纵向研究（2017-2025）中显著缓解了时间退化问题，持续优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于深度学习的DGA检测器在静态条件下表现良好，但面对时间漂移（新DGA变种不断出现）时性能严重下降，缺乏长期有效的防御能力。

Method: 提出了一种漂移鲁棒的Transformer框架，包含：(i)字符级编码捕获随机形态模式，(ii)子词级编码处理基于单词的DGA，以及三个自监督预训练任务学习鲁棒的结构和上下文特征，再通过监督微调完成分类。

Result: 在9年（2017-2025）的前向链式实验中，该方法显著缓解了时间退化，持续优于现有最先进的基线模型。

Conclusion: 该方法为不断演变的威胁环境中长期DGA防御提供了可靠的基准方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Domain Generation Algorithms (DGAs) evolve continuously to evade botnet detection, posing a persistent challenge for dependable network defense.
