While deep learning-based detectors achieve strong performance under static conditions, they suffer severe degradation when facing temporal drift. Through a 9-year longitudinal study (2017-2025), we empirically show that state-of-the-art character- and word-based DGA classifiers rapidly lose effectiveness as new DGA variants emerge.
  To address this problem, we propose a drift-resilient Transformer-based framework that learns invariant representations through a hybrid tokenization strategy and multi-task self-supervised pre-training. The model integrates (i) character-level encoding to capture stochastic morphological patterns and (ii) subword-level encoding for word-based DGAs. Three pre-training tasks enable the model to learn robust structural and contextual features prior to supervised fine-tuning. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that our method significantly mitigates temporal degradation and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in forward-chaining experiments. The proposed approach offers a dependable foundation for long-term DGA defense in evolving threat landscapes. Our code is available at: https://github.com/snsec-net/2026-DSN-DRIFT.

</details>


### [296] [A Note on Banaszczyk's Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10461)
*Hongyuan Qu,Chengliang Tian,Guangwu Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Banaszczyk不等式进行了改进，通过施加适当条件获得了更优的界，该改进可用于研究LWE问题的对偶攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Banaszczyk不等式是格密码学中的重要工具，Tian、Liu和Xu给出了改进版本。本文旨在进一步优化该不等式，以获得更紧的尾界估计，从而更好地应用于LWE问题的安全性分析。

Method: 通过施加适当条件，对现有的Banaszczyk不等式（包括Tian-Liu-Xu的改进版本）进行进一步优化，推导出显著更优的界。

Result: 得到了一个显著更优的Banaszczyk不等式变体，其尾界估计更紧。

Conclusion: 改进后的不等式可用于研究学习带误差（LWE）问题的对偶攻击，有助于更精确地评估格基密码方案的安全性。

Abstract: Banaszczyk's inequality establishes a tail estimate for the discrete Gaussian measure on a lattice in $\mathbb{R}^n$. This classic result has been influential and plays an important role in lattice-based cryptography. An improvement of the inequality with a transparent proof was given by Tian, Liu and Xu. In this note, we further improve this inequality by imposing an appropriate condition, obtaining a significantly better bound. This refined inequality can be used to investigate dual attacks against the Learning With Errors (LWE) problem.

</details>


### [297] [Acceptance Cards:A Four-Diagnostic Standard for Safe Fine-Tuning Defense Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10575)
*Phongsakon Mark Konrad,Toygar Tanyel,Serkan Ayvaz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Acceptance Cards评估协议，用于检验安全微调防御声明的有效性。协议包含四项诊断：统计可靠性、新鲜语义泛化、机制对齐和跨任务迁移。应用该协议重新评估SafeLoRA在Gemma-2-2B-it上的表现，发现其在严格条件下四项全失败，宽松条件下四项中仅通过一项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全微调防御评估常基于保留集上的差距缩小来声称有效性，但这种缩小可能来自采样噪声、主体伪影、能力损失或不具迁移性的机制，缺乏严格的评估标准来验证防御声明的真实性。

Method: 提出Acceptance Cards评估协议，包含四项诊断：（1）统计可靠性检验；（2）新鲜语义泛化检验；（3）机制对齐检验；（4）跨任务迁移检验。将防御声明视为"安装差距"（installed-gap），只有当四项诊断全部通过才视为完整卡片通过（full-card pass）。

Result: 对SafeLoRA在Gemma-2-2B-it上的重新评估表明：在严格机制类编码下四项诊断全部失败；在宽松的收缩重标记下仍有三项失败。在46格审计中，没有一格通过严格合取条件。最接近的家族通过了可靠性和机制检查（在所需数据可用的情况下），但未通过新鲜主体阈值，缺乏严格的迁移通过，并带有可测量的部署精度成本。

Conclusion: Acceptance Cards为安全微调防御声明提供了更严格的评估标准，揭示了现有防御方法（如SafeLoRA）在严格审计下的局限性。但这是针对单一模型家族的窄审计，并非对SafeLoRA有效性的全局判断。

Abstract: Safe fine-tuning defenses are often endorsed on the basis of a held-out gap reduction, but the same reduction can come from sampling noise, subject artifacts, capability loss, or a mechanism that does not transfer. We introduce Acceptance Cards: an evaluation protocol, a documentation object, an executable audit package, and a claim-specific evidential standard for safe fine-tuning defense claims. The protocol checks statistical reliability, fresh semantic generalization, mechanism alignment, and cross-task transfer before treating a gap reduction as a full-card pass. Re-scored under this installed-gap protocol, SafeLoRA fails the full-card pass on Gemma-2-2B-it: under strict mechanism-class coding it fails all four diagnostics, and under a permissive shrinkage relabel it still fails three of four. This is a narrow installed-gap audit on one model family, not a global judgment of SafeLoRA's effectiveness. In a 46-cell audit, no cell satisfies the strict conjunction. The closest family is a near miss that passes reliability and mechanism checks where the required data are available, but fails the fresh-subject threshold, lacks a strict transfer pass, and carries a measurable deployment-accuracy cost.

</details>


### [298] [Guaranteed Jailbreaking Defense via Disrupt-and-Rectify Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10582)
*Zheng Lin,Zhenxing Niu,Haoxuan Ji,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DR-Smoothing的平滑式防御方法，通过"扰乱-修正"两阶段提示处理机制来防御针对大语言模型的越狱攻击，在理论上给出了防御成功概率的紧界，并在实验上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型面临越狱攻击的威胁，现有平滑式防御方法仅采用"仅扰乱"策略，会导致扰乱后的提示偏离原有分布，引发LLM不可预测的行为，且难以兼顾无害性与有用性。

Method: 借鉴对抗防御领域去噪平滑思想，提出Disrupt-and-Rectify Smoothing (DR-Smoothing)方法，在传统平滑防御框架中引入两阶段提示处理：首先扰乱输入提示，然后进行修正，将偏离分布的扰乱提示恢复为分布内形式。

Result: 理论上给出了通用平滑框架的防御成功概率的紧界及对扰乱强度的要求。实验证明该方法在无害性和有用性两方面均优于当前最先进的防御方法，能有效防御token级和prompt级越狱攻击，包括自适应攻击场景。

Conclusion: DR-Smoothing通过"扰乱-修正"两阶段方案有效平衡了防御的无害性与有用性，提供了理论保证，是一种通用且高效的LLM越狱攻击防御方法。

Abstract: This paper proposes a guaranteed defense method for large language models (LLMs) to safeguard against jailbreaking attacks. Drawing inspiration from the denoised-smoothing approach in the adversarial defense domain, we propose a novel smoothing-based defense method, termed Disrupt-and-Rectify Smoothing (DR-Smoothing). Specifically, we integrate a two-stage prompt processing scheme-first disrupting the input prompt, then rectifying it-into the conventional smoothing defense framework. This disrupt-and-rectify approach improves upon previous disrupt-only approaches by restoring out-of-distribution disrupted prompts to an in-distribution form, thereby reducing the risk of unpredictable LLM behavior. In addition, this two-stage scheme offers a distinct advantage in striking a balance between harmlessness and helpfulness in jailbreaking defense. Notably, we present a theoretical analysis for generic smoothing framework, offering a tight bound for the defense success probability and the requirements on the disruption strength. Our approach can defend against both token-level and prompt-level jailbreaking attacks, under both established and adaptive attacking scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach surpasses current state-of-the-art defense methods in terms of both harmlessness and helpfulness.

</details>


### [299] [Generate "Normal", Edit Poisoned: Branding Injection via Hint Embedding in Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10600)
*Desen Sun,Jason Hon,Howe Wang,Saarth Rajan,Meng Xu,Sihang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种针对文本到图像生成工作流的新型安全漏洞：攻击者可以在输入图像中嵌入几乎不可见的隐藏信息（如品牌标识），下游生成模型会识别并将这些信息重新渲染到语义相关的物体上，即使用户提示中未明确提及。研究提出了两种攻击场景（钓鱼攻击和投毒攻击）及相应的防御策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI的快速发展，用户在图像设计过程中通常采用多轮交互（先文本生成图像，再文本引导图像编辑）。这种工作流存在被恶意利用的潜在安全隐患，即攻击者可以利用生成模型对输入图像中隐藏内容的识别能力进行隐蔽攻击。

Method: 提出两种攻击场景：(1) 钓鱼攻击——攻击者控制在线图像生成服务，在返回给用户的生成图像中注入隐藏内容；(2) 投毒攻击——攻击者分发被篡改的文本到图像扩散模型，其输出包含隐藏内容。使用六种注入载荷（包括知名标识和定制设计）进行评估，并开发了相应的缓解方案。

Result: 钓鱼攻击平均成功率44.4%，投毒攻击平均成功率32.2%，且注入的标识在视觉上几乎不可察觉。提出的缓解方案对钓鱼攻击和投毒攻击的平均防御成功率分别为87.4%和92.3%。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了生成式AI多轮交互工作流中存在的新型隐蔽攻击面，证明了隐藏载荷注入的可行性，并提供了有效的防御方案，对AI安全领域具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of generative AI, users increasingly rely on image-generation models for image design and creation. To achieve faithful outputs, users typically engage in multi-turn interactions during image refinement: a text-to-image generation phase followed by a text-guided image-to-image editing phase. In this paper, we investigate a novel security vulnerability associated with such a workflow. Our key insight is that a nearly invisible hint, like branding information (e.g., a logo), embedded in an input image can be recognized by downstream generative models and subsequently re-rendered onto semantically related objects, even when the user prompt does not explicitly mention it. This form of hidden payload injection makes the attack stealthy.
  We study two realistic attack scenarios. The first is a phishing-based setting, in which an attacker controls an online image generation service and injects hidden content into generated images before they are returned to users. The second is a poison-based setting, where an attacker distributes a compromised text-to-image diffusion model whose output contains hidden content. We evaluate both attacks using six injected payloads, including well-known logos and customized designs, and demonstrate that the two attacks can achieve success rates of 44.4% and 32.2% on average, respectively, while ensuring the injected logos are visually imperceptible. We also develop a mitigation solution that achieves an average success rate of 87.4% and 92.3% against the phishing-based and poison-based attacks, respectively.

</details>


### [300] [Re-Triggering Safeguards within LLMs for Jailbreak Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10611)
*Zheng Lin,Zhenxing Niu,Haoxuan Ji,Yuzhe Huang,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于嵌入扰动的越狱提示检测方法，通过重新激活LLM内置的安全机制来防御越狱攻击，在白盒和黑盒场景下均表现出色，并能抵御自适应攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM虽配备安全机制，但攻击者仍能构造越狱提示绕过这些防护；以往防御方法多作为独立解决方案，未充分利用模型自身的安全能力。

Method: 提出嵌入扰动（embedding disruption）方法，重新触发LLM内部的安全防御机制；通过深入分析扰动效应，开发高效搜索算法寻找合适的扰动以实现越狱检测。

Result: 在白盒和黑盒设定下有效防御了最先进的越狱攻击，并能抵抗自适应攻击。

Conclusion: 与模型自身防御机制协同而非替代的嵌入扰动方法，能有效且鲁棒地检测越狱提示。

Abstract: This paper proposes a jailbreaking prompt detection method for large language models (LLMs) to defend against jailbreak attacks. Although recent LLMs are equipped with built-in safeguards, it remains possible to craft jailbreaking prompts that bypass them. We argue that such jailbreaking prompts are inherently fragile, and thus introduce an embedding disruption method to re-activate the safeguards within LLMs. Unlike previous defense methods that aim to serve as standalone solutions, our approach instead cooperates with the LLM's internal defense mechanisms by re-triggering them. Moreover, through extensive analysis, we gain a comprehensive understanding of the disruption effects and develop an efficient search algorithm to identify appropriate disruptions for effective jailbreak detection. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach effectively defends against state-of-the-art jailbreak attacks in white-box and black-box settings, and remains robust even against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [301] [Security Analysis of Time-of-Arrival Estimation via Cross-Correlation under Narrow-Band Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10632)
*Claudio Anliker,Daniele Coppola,Giovanni Camurati,Srdjan Čapkun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了两种针对窄带测距系统中基于互相关的ToA估计的新型距离缩减攻击：时域符号周期波形乘法攻击和负群延迟滤波攻击。这两种攻击无需实时符号检测，完全符号无关，其中NGD攻击在蓝牙CS RTT测距中可实现高达18米的距离缩减，并通过原型验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窄带系统中基于互相关的ToA估计容易遭受距离递减攻击（如ED/LC），现有攻击需要实时符号检测或自适应补偿，存在局限性。作者希望设计更隐蔽、更易实施的符号无关攻击方法。

Method: 1) 第一种攻击：在时域上将信号与符号周期波形相乘，重塑相关函数峰值位置；2) 第二种攻击：将信号通过负群延迟（NGD）滤波器，使信号包络前移，产生虚假的早到时间估计。两种攻击均不需要实时符号检测或自适应补偿，具有符号无关性。作者通过LTspice进行NGD电路仿真，MATLAB进行ToA估计仿真，并用商用现货（COTS）组件构建原型验证。

Result: NGD攻击在蓝牙CS RTT测距中可实现高达18米的距离缩减效果。通过LTspice仿真和MATLAB联合验证了攻击的有效性，并使用COTS组件构建的原型证明了NGD方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 首次提出了两种无需符号检测的符号无关型距离缩减攻击方法，其中NGD攻击在蓝牙CS这类窄带测距系统中具有显著效果，对窄带无线系统的安全性提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Time-of-arrival (ToA) estimation via cross-correlation is an essential building block of time-of-flight ranging. However, in narrowband systems, it is notoriously difficult to protect against distance-decreasing attacks such as Early-Detect/Late-Commit (ED/LC). We present and analyze two new attacks that reshape ranging signals to compromise correlation-based ToA estimation. The first attack multiplies the signal by a symbol-periodic waveform in the time domain, while the second passes it through a negative group delay (NGD) filter. In contrast to ED/LC, our attacks do not require real-time symbol detection or adaptive compensation; they are completely symbol-agnostic. We describe implementation strategies for both attacks and discuss NGD filtering in the context of Bluetooth Channel Sounding (CS), a recent narrowband ranging system. To this end, we simulate an NGD circuit in LTspice and a ToA estimator in MATLAB, demonstrating that the attack can result in distance reductions of up to 18 m against Bluetooth CS RTT ranging. Finally, we verify the feasibility of the NGD approach by building a prototype using commercial off-the-shelf components.

</details>


### [302] [LITMUS: Benchmarking Behavioral Jailbreaks of LLM Agents in Real OS Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10779)
*Chiyu Zhang,Huiqin Yang,Bendong Jiang,Xiaolei Zhang,Yiran Zhao,Ruyi Chen,Lu Zhou,Xiaogang Xu,Jiafei Wu,Liming Fang,Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LITMUS是一个针对LLM自主智能体在真实操作系统环境中行为安全的基准测试，通过语义-物理双重验证机制和OS级状态回滚，检测智能体执行危险OS操作的"行为越狱"问题。包含819个高风险测试用例，覆盖三种攻击范式，评估发现最强模型仍执行40.64%的高危操作，并揭示了"执行幻觉"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试要么仅在语义层评估安全性而忽略物理层危害，要么无法隔离测试用例导致污染，缺乏对LLM智能体在真实OS环境中执行危险操作（行为越狱）的系统性评估基准。

Method: 设计语义-物理双重验证机制（同时检查对话层响应和OS级物理操作）与OS级状态回滚机制；构建819个高风险测试用例，包含1个有害种子子集和6个攻击扩展子集，覆盖三种对抗范式：越狱说话（jailbreak speaking）、技能注入（skill injection）和实体包装（entity wrapping）；采用全自动多智能体评估框架。

Result: (1) 当前智能体缺乏有效安全意识，最强模型Claude Sonnet 4.6仍执行40.64%的高危操作；(2) 发现普遍存在的"执行幻觉"（Execution Hallucination）现象，智能体口头拒绝请求但危险操作已在系统层面完成，此前所有语义级框架均无法检测；(3) 技能注入和实体包装攻击成功率极高，暴露出显著脆弱性。

Conclusion: LITMUS提供了首个标准化、可复现、基于物理层的LLM智能体行为安全评估平台，填补了语义层评估无法检测实际OS级危害的空白。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of LLM-based autonomous agents in real operating system environments introduces a new category of safety risk beyond content safety: behavior jailbreak, where an adversary induces an agent to execute dangerous OS-level operations with irreversible consequences. Existing benchmarks either evaluate safety at the semantic layer alone, missing physical-layer harms, or fail to isolate test cases, letting earlier runs contaminate later ones. We present LITMUS (LLM-agents In-OS Testing for Measuring Unsafe Subversion), a benchmark addressing both gaps via a semantic-physical dual verification mechanism and OS-level state rollback. LITMUS comprises 819 high-risk test cases organized into one harmful seed subset and six attack-extended subsets covering three adversarial paradigms (jailbreak speaking, skill injection, and entity wrapping), plus a fully automated multi-agent evaluation framework judging behavior at both conversational and OS-level physical layers. Evaluation across frontier agents reveals three findings: (1) current agents lack effective safety awareness, with strong models (e.g., Claude Sonnet 4.6) still executing 40.64% of high-risk operations; (2) agents exhibit pervasive Execution Hallucination (EH), verbally refusing a request while the dangerous operation has already completed at the system level, invisible to every prior semantic-only framework; and (3) skill injection and entity wrapping attacks achieve high success rates, exposing pronounced agent vulnerabilities. LITMUS provides the first standardized platform for reproducible, physically grounded behavioral safety evaluation of LLM agents in real OS environments.

</details>


### [303] [Can You Keep a Secret? Involuntary Information Leakage in Language Model Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10794)
*Ari Holtzman,Peter West*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 前沿语言模型在需要保密信息的情况下（如系统提示、思维链、敏感数据）无法有效隐藏秘密信息。即使秘密词从未在输出中直接出现，模型也会通过主题、意象、场景等间接方式泄露秘密，泄露率高达79%。模型规模越大泄露越严重，短文本写作不会泄露，使用"干扰概念"可部分转移泄露。关注秘密似乎会打开一个信息通道，即使被明确指示关闭也无法完全阻止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型被部署在需要信息分区的场景中（系统提示保密、思维链对用户隐藏、敏感数据通过共享上下文传递），但模型能否真正将提示中的秘密信息排除在输出之外尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 给每个模型一个秘密词并指示不要泄露，要求模型写一个故事；然后由第二个模型通过二元区分测试从故事中识别秘密词。测试了五种前沿模型，确保秘密词从未在输出中直接出现。

Result: 所有五种前沿模型均通过主题选择、意象和背景等方式泄露了秘密信息，泄露率显著高于随机水平（最高达79%）。泄露是跨模型可读的，在两个模型系列内与模型规模正相关，短文本写作（如笑话）中泄露完全消失。使用"干扰概念"可部分将泄露从真实秘密转移到干扰概念上。

Conclusion: 关注秘密似乎会打开一个信息通道，即使是前沿LLM也无法在收到指示后完全关闭它。这提示当前模型在信息隔离方面存在根本性局限，可能对安全部署构成挑战。

Abstract: Language models are deployed in settings that require compartmentalization: system prompts should not be disclosed, chain-of-thought reasoning is hidden from users, and sensitive data passes through shared contexts. We test whether models can keep prompted information out of their writing. We give each model a secret word with instructions not to reveal it, then ask it to write a story. A second model tries to identify the secret from the story in a binary discrimination test. The secret word never appears literally in any output, but all five frontier models we test leak it thematically -- through topic choice, imagery, and setting--6hy-at rates significantly different from chance, up to 79\%. When told to actively hide the secret, models write \emph{away from} it, and this avoidance is itself detectable. The leakage is cross-model readable, scales sharply with model size within two model families, and disappears entirely for short-form writing like jokes. Giving the model a decoy concept to ``focus on instead'' partially redirects the leakage from the real secret to the decoy. Attending to a secret appears to open up an information channel that frontier LLMs cannot close, even when instructed to.

</details>


### [304] [Threat Modelling using Domain-Adapted Language Models: Empirical Evaluation and Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10808)
*Saba Pourhanifeh,AbdulAziz AbdulGhaffar,Ashraf Matrawy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了领域自适应语言模型（包括LLM和SLM）在5G安全领域基于STRIDE的结构化威胁建模任务中的表现，发现领域自适应模型并不一致优于通用模型，解码策略显著影响输出质量，且模型规模带来的收益不稳定，表明当前语言模型在此任务上存在根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅在有限提示设置下评估了少量通用大语言模型在威胁建模中的表现，缺乏对领域自适应模型、模型规模、解码策略及提示技术的系统评估，尤其缺乏在结构化威胁建模（如STRIDE）下的全面实证研究。

Method: 使用8种不同语言模型（包括领域自适应模型和通用模型），构建52种配置，系统分析领域自适应、模型规模、解码策略（贪婪vs.随机采样）和提示技术四个因素对STRIDE威胁分类任务的影响。

Result: 领域自适应模型并未一致优于通用模型；解码策略显著影响模型行为与输出有效性；更大模型虽有更高性能但增益不稳定，不足以支撑可靠的威胁建模。

Conclusion: 当前语言模型在结构化威胁建模任务上存在根本性局限，仅靠增加训练数据或扩大模型规模难以突破瓶颈，需要融入更多任务特定推理和更强的安全概念基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models(LLMs) are increasingly explored for cybersecurity applications such as vulnerability detection. In the domain of threat modelling, prior work has primarily evaluated a number of general-purpose Large Language Models under limited prompting settings. In this study, we extend the research area of structured threat modelling by systematically evaluating domain-adapted language models of different sizes to their general counterparts. We use both LLMs and Small Language Models(SLMs) that were domain adapted to telecommunications and cybersecuirty. For the structured threat modelling, we selected the widely used STRIDE approach and the application area is 5G security.
  We present a comprehensive empirical evaluation using 52 different configurations (on 8 different language models) to analyze the impact of 1) domain adaptation, 2) model scale, 3) decoding strategies (greedy vs. stochastic sampling), and 4) prompting technique on STRIDE threat classification. Our results show that domain-adapted models do not consistently outperform their general-purpose counterparts, and decoding strategies significantly affect model behavior and output validity. They also show that while larger models generally achieve higher performance, these gains are neither consistent nor sufficient for reliable threat modelling. These findings highlight fundamental limitations of current LLMs for structured threat modelling tasks and suggest that improvements require more than additional training data or model scaling, motivating the need for incorporating more task-specific reasoning and stronger grounding in security concepts.
  We present insights on invalid outputs encountered and present suggestions for prompting tailored specifically for STRIDE threat modelling.

</details>


### [305] [Engineering Robustness into Personal Agents with the AI Workflow Store](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10907)
*Roxana Geambasu,Mariana Raykova,Pierre Tholoniat,Trishita Tiwari,Lillian Tsai,Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文批评当前AI智能体主导的"即时合成"范式缺乏严谨的软件工程流程，主张将迭代设计、严格测试、对抗性评估等工程实践整合到智能体循环中，并提出建立"AI工作流商店"来存储可复用的、经过加固的工作流，以解决灵活性-鲁棒性之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体采用即时合成范式，在秒到分钟级别内响应提示并执行动作，绕过了软件工程中经过验证的严谨流程（迭代设计、严格测试、对抗性评估、分阶段部署等），导致在高风险场景中用户可能将"即兴原型"误当作可靠系统来使用，存在严重的可靠性和安全性隐患。

Method: 本文通过批判性分析指出即时合成范式的局限性，提出将严谨SE流程集成到智能体循环中，构建确定性约束的生产级智能体工作流；引入"AI工作流商店"概念，通过社区范围内的复用来摊销严谨流程带来的额外计算和时间成本。

Result: 提出了一个概念框架：AI Workflow Store，包含经过加固的、可复用的工作流，相比即时合成的工具链具有更高的可靠性和安全性；识别出从灵活性-鲁棒性张力中衍生的一系列研究挑战。

Conclusion: 即时合成范式需要被超越，必须将软件工程的严谨性引入智能体循环，通过社区复用机制摊销成本，才能构建适用于高风险场景的可靠AI智能体系统。

Abstract: The dominant paradigm for AI agents is an "on-the-fly" loop in which agents synthesize plans and execute actions within seconds or minutes in response to user prompts. We argue that this paradigm short-circuits disciplined software engineering (SE) processes -- iterative design, rigorous testing, adversarial evaluation, staged deployment, and more -- that have delivered the (relatively) reliable and secure systems we use today. By focusing on rapid, real-time synthesis, are AI agents effectively delivering users improvised prototypes rather than systems fit for high-stakes scenarios in which users may unwittingly apply them?
  This paper argues for the need to integrate rigorous SE processes into the agentic loop to produce production-grade, hardened, and deterministically-constrained agent *workflows* that substantially outperform the potentially brittle and vulnerable results of on-the-fly synthesis. Doing so may require extra compute and time, and if so, we must amortize the cost of rigor through reuse across a broad user community. We envision an *AI Workflow Store* that consists of hardened and reusable workflows that agents can invoke with far greater reliability and security than improvised tool chains. We outline the research challenges of this vision, which stem from a broader flexibility-robustness tension that we argue requires moving beyond the ``on-the-fly'' paradigm to navigate effectively.

</details>
